+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import type { ts } from "../ts-api.js";
+import type { FieldDef, ValType } from "../ir/types.js";
+import type { CodegenContext } from "./context/types.js";
+import { resolveClosedDataFieldStorage } from "./bigint-field-carrier.js";
+import { resolveNativeTypeAnnotation } from "./index.js";
+
+/** Append the final physical field and its callable dedup evidence together. */
+export function appendAnonymousDataField(
+  ctx: CodegenContext,
+  fields: FieldDef[],
+  methodSigParts: string[],
+  property: ts.Symbol,
+  semanticType: ts.Type,
+  refinedType: ValType,
+  callSignatures: readonly ts.Signature[],
+): void {
+  // Choose storage after refinements but before hashing/publication. Physical
+  // dedup must never transfer source-logical BigInt intent to unrelated fields.
+  const storage = resolveClosedDataFieldStorage(
+    ctx,
+    semanticType,
+    refinedType,
+    resolveNativeTypeAnnotation(semanticType),
+  );
+  fields.push({ name: property.name, type: storage.type, mutable: true });
+  if (callSignatures.length === 0) return;
+  const signature = callSignatures[0]!;
+  // Arity alone conflates valueOf/toString returning bigint with a throwing
+  // method of the same arity, corrupting placeholder types and trampolines.
+  const returnType = ctx.checker.getReturnTypeOfSignature(signature);
+  const returnName = returnType ? ctx.checker.typeToString(returnType) : "void";
+  methodSigParts.push(`${property.name}#${signature.parameters.length}->${returnName}`);
+}

 import { isStrictContext } from "./helpers/is-strict-function.js";
 import type { ts } from "../ts-api.js";
+import { emitVecLengthHoleFill } from "./vec-length-hole-fill.js";
+
+/**
+ * Overlay validation owns deletion when available. Filling holes before it
+ * could erase elements even when a non-writable length rejects the shrink.
+ * Preserve the plain store only when there is no descriptor-overlay route.
+ */
+export function buildValidatedArrayLengthStore(
+  ctx: CodegenContext,
+  fctx: FunctionContext,
+  vecTmp: number,
+  newLenTmp: number,
+  strictTarget: ts.Node,
+  plainStore: Instr[],
+): Instr[] {
+  const overlay = buildOverlayArrayLengthSet(ctx, fctx, vecTmp, newLenTmp, strictTarget);
+  if (overlay !== null) return overlay;
+  const savedBody = fctx.body;
+  fctx.body = [];
+  try {
+    emitVecLengthHoleFill(ctx, fctx, vecTmp, newLenTmp, "shrink-only", true);
+    return [...fctx.body, ...plainStore];
+  } finally {
+    fctx.body = savedBody;
+  }
+}

+/** Keep the existing nullish receiver rejection ahead of any observable Get. */
+function emitIteratorReceiverCheck(ctx: CodegenContext, fctx: FunctionContext, member: string): void {
+  const thisThrow: Instr[] = [];
+  emitBrandCheckTypeError(ctx, thisThrow, `Array.prototype.${member} called on null or undefined`);
+  fctx.body.push({ op: "local.get", index: 1 }, { op: "ref.is_null" });
+  const isUndefinedIdx = undefinedSingletonActive(ctx) ? ctx.funcMap.get("__extern_is_undefined") : undefined;
+  if (isUndefinedIdx !== undefined) {
+    fctx.body.push({ op: "local.get", index: 1 }, { op: "call", funcIdx: isUndefinedIdx }, { op: "i32.or" });
+  }
+  fctx.body.push({ op: "if", blockType: { kind: "empty" }, then: thisThrow });
+}
diff --git a/src/codegen/bigint-field-carrier.ts b/src/codegen/bigint-field-carrier.ts

index 0000000000..0ac1394c46

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { getNullablePrimitiveInfo, isBigIntType } from "../checker/type-mapper.js";
+import type { ValType } from "../ir/types.js";
+import type { ts } from "../ts-api.js";
+import type { CodegenContext, FunctionContext } from "./context/types.js";
+import { addUnionImportsViaRegistry, flushLateImportShifts } from "./shared.js";
+
+/** Logical field intent, not a second registry of physical layouts. */
+export interface ClosedDataFieldStoragePlan {
+  readonly type: ValType;
+  readonly nativeBigIntReference: boolean;
+}
+
+/** Pure: choose storage BEFORE layout hashing/publication; never mint helpers. */
+export function resolveClosedDataFieldStorage(
+  ctx: CodegenContext,
+  semanticType: ts.Type,
+  scalarMapping: ValType,
+  explicitNativeOverride?: ValType | null,
+): ClosedDataFieldStoragePlan {
+  if (explicitNativeOverride) return { type: explicitNativeOverride, nativeBigIntReference: false };
+  const nativeBigInt =
+    ctx.standalone &&
+    ctx.nativeStrings &&
+    (isBigIntType(semanticType) || getNullablePrimitiveInfo(semanticType)?.primitiveKind === "bigint");
+  const widen = nativeBigInt && scalarMapping.kind === "i64" && scalarMapping.bigint === true;
+  return {
+    type: widen ? { kind: "externref" } : scalarMapping,
+    nativeBigIntReference: nativeBigInt && (widen || scalarMapping.kind === "externref"),
+  };
+}
+
+/**
+ * Before the caller's ONE compileExpression(rhs, storage). No RHS evaluation,
+ * runtime conversion, cached numeric handle, or reconstruction from low64.
+ * A supplied plan must describe the logical source field, not a dedup cache's
+ * same-shaped externref field. Dynamic/Reflect callers instead prove the RHS.
+ */
+export function prepareClosedFieldValue(
+  ctx: CodegenContext,
+  fctx: FunctionContext,
+  rhs: ts.Expression,
+  storage: ValType,
+  semanticPlan?: ClosedDataFieldStoragePlan,
+): void {
+  if (!ctx.standalone || !ctx.nativeStrings || (storage.kind !== "externref" && storage.kind !== "anyref")) return;
+  if (!semanticPlan?.nativeBigIntReference && ctx.oracle.staticJsTypeOf(rhs) !== "bigint") return;
+  addUnionImportsViaRegistry(ctx);
+  flushLateImportShifts(ctx, fctx);
+}

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+/** Native BigInt.prototype.valueOf: preserve the complete primitive carrier. */
+import type { ValType } from "../ir/types.js";
+import type { CodegenContext, FunctionContext } from "./context/types.js";
+import { allocLocal } from "./context/locals.js";
+import { buildThrowJsErrorInstrs } from "./js-errors.js";
+import { ensureObjectRuntime, FLAG_INTERNAL, WRAPPER_PRIMITIVE_KEY } from "./object-runtime.js";
+import { addUnionImportsViaRegistry } from "./shared.js";
+import { stringConstantExternrefInstrs } from "./native-strings.js";
+
+/**
+ * Emit the standalone `BigInt.prototype.valueOf` closure body.
+ *
+ * The value read can precede the first BigInt expression in source order, so
+ * the native carrier is demanded here rather than relying on a later boxing
+ * site. The `$Object` arm recognizes the internal `[[PrimitiveValue]]` slot
+ * used by native primitive wrappers, while the flag and carrier checks keep a
+ * user property with the same spelling from being mistaken for that slot.
+ */
+export function emitBigIntProtoValueOfBody(ctx: CodegenContext, fctx: FunctionContext): ValType | null {
+  if (!ctx.standalone) return null;
+
+  addUnionImportsViaRegistry(ctx);
+  ensureObjectRuntime(ctx);
+  // Register throw dependencies before capturing type/function handles.
+  const wrongReceiver = buildThrowJsErrorInstrs(
+    ctx,
+    "TypeError",
+    "BigInt.prototype.valueOf called on incompatible receiver",
+    {
+      forceInModuleCtor: true,
+    },
+  );
+  const bigIntTypeIdx = ctx.nativeBigIntTypeIdx;
+  if (bigIntTypeIdx < 0) throw new Error("Missing native BigInt carrier for valueOf");
+  const objectTypes = ctx.objectRuntimeTypes;
+  const objFindIdx = ctx.funcMap.get("__obj_find");
+  if (!objectTypes || objFindIdx === undefined) return null;
+
+  const { objectTypeIdx, propEntryTypeIdx } = objectTypes;
+  const entry = allocLocal(fctx, `__bigvo_entry_${fctx.locals.length}`, {
+    kind: "ref_null",
+    typeIdx: propEntryTypeIdx,
+  });
+  const slot = allocLocal(fctx, `__bigvo_slot_${fctx.locals.length}`, { kind: "anyref" });
+
+  // $BigIntWide is a subtype of $BigInt. Return the full externref without
+  // reading its low i64 field or applying __to_bigint; wide limbs stay intact.
+  fctx.body.push(
+    { op: "local.get", index: 1 },
+    { op: "any.convert_extern" },
+    { op: "ref.test", typeIdx: bigIntTypeIdx },
+    {
+      op: "if",
+      blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+      then: [{ op: "local.get", index: 1 }, { op: "return" }],
+    },
+  );
+
+  // BigInt wrapper receiver: recover the internal [[PrimitiveValue]] slot and
+  // require both the internal flag and the native BigInt carrier brand.
+  fctx.body.push(
+    { op: "local.get", index: 1 },
+    { op: "any.convert_extern" },
+    { op: "ref.test", typeIdx: objectTypeIdx },
+    {
+      op: "if",
+      blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+      then: [
+        { op: "local.get", index: 1 },
+        { op: "any.convert_extern" },
+        { op: "ref.cast", typeIdx: objectTypeIdx },
+        ...stringConstantExternrefInstrs(ctx, WRAPPER_PRIMITIVE_KEY),
+        { op: "call", funcIdx: objFindIdx },
+        { op: "local.tee", index: entry },
+        { op: "ref.is_null" },
+        { op: "i32.eqz" },
+        {
+          op: "if",
+          blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+          then: [
+            { op: "local.get", index: entry },
+            { op: "ref.as_non_null" },
+            { op: "struct.get", typeIdx: propEntryTypeIdx, fieldIdx: 2 },
+            { op: "i32.const", value: FLAG_INTERNAL },
+            { op: "i32.and" },
+            {
+              op: "if",
+              blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+              then: [
+                { op: "local.get", index: entry },
+                { op: "ref.as_non_null" },
+                { op: "struct.get", typeIdx: propEntryTypeIdx, fieldIdx: 1 },
+                { op: "local.set", index: slot },
+                { op: "local.get", index: slot },
+                { op: "ref.test", typeIdx: bigIntTypeIdx },
+                {
+                  op: "if",
+                  blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+                  then: [{ op: "local.get", index: slot }, { op: "extern.convert_any" }, { op: "return" }],
+                },
+              ],
+            },
+          ],
+        },
+      ],
+    },
+  );
+
+  fctx.body.push(...wrongReceiver);
+  return { kind: "externref" };
+}

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import type { ts } from "../ts-api.js";
+import type { FieldDef, ValType } from "../ir/types.js";
+import type { CodegenContext } from "./context/types.js";
+import { ensureAnyValueType } from "./any-helpers.js";
+import { resolveWasmType } from "./index.js";
+import { resolveClosedDataFieldStorage, type ClosedDataFieldStoragePlan } from "./bigint-field-carrier.js";
+
+/** Class-instance policy only; caller supplies its existing semantic query. */
+export function resolveClassInstanceFieldStorage(
+  ctx: CodegenContext,
+  semanticType: ts.Type,
+  nativeOverride: ValType | null,
+  voidCleared: boolean,
+  hasDynamicHostParent: boolean,
+): ClosedDataFieldStoragePlan {
+  const plan = resolveClosedDataFieldStorage(
+    ctx,
+    semanticType,
+    nativeOverride ?? resolveWasmType(ctx, semanticType),
+    nativeOverride,
+  );
+  let type = plan.type;
+  if (nativeOverride === null && voidCleared && (type.kind === "ref" || type.kind === "ref_null")) {
+    type = { kind: "externref" };
+  }
+  if (hasDynamicHostParent && (type.kind === "ref" || type.kind === "ref_null")) {
+    type = { kind: "externref" };
+  }
+  return { type, nativeBigIntReference: plan.nativeBigIntReference };
+}
+
+/** Logical allocation metadata, not part of structural hashing. */
+export function classFieldDefaultMetadata(
+  plan: ClosedDataFieldStoragePlan,
+  optionalUninitialized = false,
+): Pick<FieldDef, "undefinedDefault"> {
+  const numericUndefined = optionalUninitialized && plan.type.kind === "f64";
+  const bigintUndefined = plan.nativeBigIntReference && plan.type.kind === "externref";
+  return numericUndefined || bigintUndefined ? { undefinedDefault: true } : {};
+}
+
+/** Called immediately after layout commit, before any lazy/constructor capture. */
+export function prepareClassFieldDefaults(ctx: CodegenContext, fields: readonly FieldDef[]): void {
+  if (!fields.some((field) => field.undefinedDefault && field.type.kind === "externref")) return;
+  ensureAnyValueType(ctx);
+  if (ctx.undefinedGlobalIdx === undefined) throw new Error("Class undefined default was not prepared");
+}

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import type { FieldDef, Instr } from "../ir/types.js";
+import type { CodegenContext } from "./context/types.js";
+
+/** Read-only: safe in allocation assertion planners. No registration/fallback. */
+export function classReferenceDefaultInstrs(ctx: Pick<CodegenContext, "undefinedGlobalIdx">, field: FieldDef): Instr[] {
+  if (field.type.kind !== "externref") throw new Error("Expected class reference field");
+  if (!field.undefinedDefault) return [{ op: "ref.null.extern" }];
+  if (ctx.undefinedGlobalIdx === undefined) throw new Error("Class undefined default was not prepared");
+  return [{ op: "global.get", index: ctx.undefinedGlobalIdx }, { op: "extern.convert_any" }];
+}
diff --git a/src/codegen/context/emission-ownership.ts b/src/codegen/context/emission-ownership.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..924668d7bb

+++ b/src/codegen/context/emission-ownership.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,618 @@
+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+/**
+ * Context-local, metadata-only source-emission ownership arena.
+ *
+ * Not wired to CodegenContext, speculative compilation, IR or activation.
+ * Instantiate once per future compilation context; never key state by AST.
+ * Numeric ids here are arena metadata, NOT function/type/global/local indices.
+ *
+ * A completed provisional body is an immutable, unpublished candidate. Retain
+ * its exact graph BEFORE rolling back the probe that constructed it if later
+ * reuse is possible. Rollback cancels live edges/publications, not that retained
+ * candidate. Reuse requires a new publication/adoption edge to its exact epoch.
+ * This contract requires the future body owner to retain the corresponding
+ * instruction arena too; a function registry entry is not such evidence.
+ */
+
+declare const ownershipHandle: unique symbol;
+type Handle<Kind extends string> = Readonly<{ kind: Kind; id: number; [ownershipHandle]: true }>;
+export type EmissionTransaction = Handle<"transaction">;
+export type BodyEpoch = Handle<"body">;
+export type EmissionRegion = Handle<"region">;
+export type EmissionOccurrence = Handle<"occurrence">;
+export type OwnershipEdge = Handle<"edge">;
+export type SourcePublication = Handle<"publication">;
+export type CompletedBody = Handle<"completed-body">;
+export type EmissionOwner = BodyEpoch | EmissionRegion;
+type Node = EmissionOwner | EmissionOccurrence;
+
+/** Opaque staged-body and capture/frame version identities, not Wasm indices. */
+export interface BodyIdentity {
+  readonly bodyIdentity: object;
+  readonly bindingEnvironment: object;
+}
+export interface RetainedOccurrence {
+  readonly occurrence: EmissionOccurrence;
+  readonly owner: EmissionOwner;
+  readonly nodeIdentity: object;
+  readonly kind: "required-drive" | "protocol-choice" | "seed-edge";
+}
+export type PendingNodeBinding = Pick<RetainedOccurrence, "nodeIdentity" | "kind">;
+export interface RetainedAdoption {
+  readonly edge: OwnershipEdge;
+  readonly owner: EmissionOwner;
+  readonly body: BodyEpoch;
+  readonly useSiteIdentity: object;
+}
+/** Independently supplied staging census, never reconstructed from this ledger. */
+export interface RetainedBodyManifest extends BodyIdentity {
+  readonly body: BodyEpoch;
+  readonly regions: readonly EmissionRegion[];
+  readonly occurrences: readonly RetainedOccurrence[];
+  readonly adoptions: readonly RetainedAdoption[];
+}
+export interface SelectedBodyManifest {
+  readonly publications: readonly Readonly<{ publication: SourcePublication; receipt: CompletedBody }>[];
+  readonly bodies: readonly RetainedBodyManifest[];
+}
+export interface VerifiedSourceOwnership {
+  readonly ownership: SealedSourceOwnership;
+  readonly manifests: readonly RetainedBodyManifest[];
+  /** Only these witnesses may request activation; choices/seeds do not. */
+  readonly requiredOccurrences: readonly EmissionOccurrence[];
+}
+
+/** AST identity is opaque provenance only; repeated visits always mint ids. */
+export interface EmissionProvenance {
+  readonly sourceId: string;
+  readonly unitId: string;
+  readonly ast?: object;
+  readonly label?: string;
+}
+
+export interface SelectedOccurrence {
+  readonly occurrence: EmissionOccurrence;
+  readonly body: BodyEpoch;
+  readonly provenance: EmissionProvenance;
+}
+
+export interface SealedSourceOwnership {
+  readonly publications: readonly Readonly<{
+    publication: SourcePublication;
+    kind: "function" | "module-init";
+    body: BodyEpoch;
+    provenance: EmissionProvenance;
+  }>[];
+  readonly bodies: readonly BodyEpoch[];
+  readonly regions: readonly EmissionRegion[];
+  readonly occurrences: readonly SelectedOccurrence[];
+  /** Arena-only bodies are NOT roots or activation demand. */
+  readonly unselectedBodies: readonly BodyEpoch[];
+  readonly discardedBodies: readonly BodyEpoch[];
+}
+
+interface NodeRecord {
+  readonly body: BodyEpoch;
+  readonly provenance: EmissionProvenance;
+}
+interface EdgeRecord {
+  readonly from: EmissionOwner;
+  readonly to: Node;
+  active: boolean;
+  readonly useSiteIdentity?: object;
+}
+interface BodyRecord {
+  state: "provisional" | "retained" | "discarded";
+  readonly creationScopes: readonly EmissionTransaction[];
+  readonly identity?: BodyIdentity;
+  construction: "unstarted" | "building" | "complete" | "failed";
+  receipt?: CompletedBody;
+  manifest?: RetainedBodyManifest;
+  // Sticky audit taint: a caller-frame write is not body-owned even when that
+  // caller later rolls back, or commits its undo entries into a content frame.
+  // Do not journal/clear this flag. An adapter must rebuild a correctly scoped
+  // epoch rather than turn recovered chronology into a completion receipt.
+  unscopedContent: boolean;
+  /** Frozen body-local edges, including outbound adoptions, not publications. */
+  retained?: ReadonlyMap<EmissionOwner, readonly Node[]>;
+}
+interface TransactionFrame {
+  readonly token: EmissionTransaction;
+  readonly undo: (() => void)[];
+  domain: "caller" | "content" | "unbound-content";
+  body?: BodyEpoch;
+  construction?: boolean;
+}
+
+/** All mutable state is private to this instance; no compiler context import. */
+export class EmissionOwnership {
+  private nextId = 0;
+  private readonly nodes = new Map<Node, NodeRecord>();
+  private readonly bodies = new Map<BodyEpoch, BodyRecord>();
+  private readonly edges = new Map<OwnershipEdge, EdgeRecord>();
+  private readonly memberships = new Map<EmissionOccurrence, OwnershipEdge>();
+  private readonly pendingBindings = new Map<EmissionOccurrence, PendingNodeBinding>();
+  private readonly pendingNodes = new Set<object>();
+  private readonly completions = new Map<CompletedBody, BodyEpoch>();
+  private readonly publications = new Map<
+    SourcePublication,
+    {
+      kind: "function" | "module-init";
+      provenance: EmissionProvenance;
+    }
+  >();
+  private readonly roots = new Map<SourcePublication, BodyEpoch>();
+  private readonly transactions: TransactionFrame[] = [];
+  private sealed?: SealedSourceOwnership;
+  private verified?: VerifiedSourceOwnership;
+
+  private collecting(): void {
+    if (this.sealed) throw new Error("Source ownership is sealed");
+  }
+
+  private handle<K extends string>(kind: K): Handle<K> {
+    return Object.freeze({ kind, id: this.nextId++ }) as Handle<K>;
+  }
+
+  private node(node: Node): NodeRecord {
+    const record = this.nodes.get(node);
+    if (!record) throw new Error("Unknown or foreign emission node");
+    return record;
+  }
+
+  private body(body: BodyEpoch): BodyRecord {
+    const record = this.bodies.get(body);
+    if (!record) throw new Error("Unknown or foreign body epoch");
+    return record;
+  }
+
+  private editable(owner: EmissionOwner): BodyEpoch {
+    this.collecting();
+    const body = this.node(owner).body;
+    if (this.body(body).state !== "provisional") throw new Error("Body epoch is not editable");
+    return body;
+  }
+
+  private contentWrite(owner: EmissionOwner): BodyEpoch {
+    const body = this.editable(owner);
+    const frame = this.transactions[this.transactions.length - 1];
+    if (frame?.domain === "unbound-content") throw new Error("Bind the content transaction before emission");
+    if (frame?.domain === "content" && frame.body !== body) throw new Error("Wrong body transaction domain");
+    return body;
+  }
+
+  private journal(undo: () => void, body?: BodyEpoch): void {
+    const frame = this.transactions[this.transactions.length - 1];
+    if (frame?.domain === "unbound-content") throw new Error("Bind the content transaction before emission");
+    if (frame?.domain === "content" && frame.body !== body) throw new Error("Wrong body transaction domain");
+    frame?.undo.push(undo);
+  }
+
+  /** Legacy caller/publication scope; NOT an authenticated body construction. */
+  beginTransaction(): EmissionTransaction {
+    this.collecting();
+    const token = this.handle("transaction");
+    this.transactions.push({ token, undo: [], domain: "caller" });
+    return token;
+  }
+
+  /** First same-body scope owns construction; nested scopes are local probes. */
+  beginContentTransaction(body?: BodyEpoch): EmissionTransaction {
+    this.collecting();
+    const token = this.handle("transaction");
+    this.transactions.push({ token, undo: [], domain: "unbound-content" });
+    if (body !== undefined) {
+      try {
+        this.bindContentTransaction(token, body);
+      } catch (error) {
+        this.transactions.pop();
+        throw error;
+      }
+    }
+    return token;
+  }
+
+  /** Authenticates a construction token that was opened BEFORE its epoch. */
+  bindContentTransaction(token: EmissionTransaction, body: BodyEpoch): void {
+    const frame = this.top(token);
+    if (frame.domain !== "unbound-content") throw new Error("Content transaction is already bound");
+    this.editable(body);
+    const record = this.body(body);
+    if (record.construction === "failed") throw new Error("Body construction failed; create a new epoch");
+    frame.domain = "content";
+    frame.body = body;
+    frame.construction = record.construction === "unstarted";
+    if (frame.construction) record.construction = "building";
+  }
+
+  private top(token: EmissionTransaction): TransactionFrame {
+    this.collecting();
+    const frame = this.transactions[this.transactions.length - 1];
+    if (!frame || frame.token !== token) throw new Error("Transactions must close once in LIFO order");
+    return frame;
+  }
+
+  /** Inner commit merges the undo log, never publishes or activates anything. */
+  commitTransaction(token: EmissionTransaction): void {
+    const frame = this.top(token);
+    if (frame.domain === "unbound-content") throw new Error("Cannot commit unbound content transaction");
+    this.transactions.pop();
+    // Detached C settles into C, never into an unrelated open P probe.
+    const parent =
+      frame.domain === "content"
+        ? [...this.transactions].reverse().find((f) => f.domain === "content" && f.body === frame.body)
+        : this.transactions[this.transactions.length - 1];
+    if (parent) for (const undo of frame.undo) parent.undo.push(undo);
+    if (frame.construction) this.body(frame.body!).construction = "complete";
+  }
+
+  rollbackTransaction(token: EmissionTransaction): void {
+    const frame = this.top(token);
+    this.transactions.pop();
+    for (let i = frame.undo.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) frame.undo[i]!();
+    if (frame.construction) this.body(frame.body!).construction = "failed";
+  }
+
+  /** Definitions remain arena candidates after rollback, never implicit roots. */
+  createBodyEpoch(provenance: EmissionProvenance, identity?: BodyIdentity): BodyEpoch {
+    this.collecting();
+    const body = this.handle("body");
+    this.nodes.set(body, { body, provenance: Object.freeze({ ...provenance }) });
+    const record: BodyRecord = {
+      state: "provisional",
+      creationScopes: this.transactions.map((frame) => frame.token),
+      identity: identity && Object.freeze({ ...identity }),
+      construction: "unstarted",
+      unscopedContent: false,
+    };
+    this.bodies.set(body, record);
+    // An unfinished candidate has no reusable instruction-arena receipt.
+    // Completed candidates survive, but never inherit a publication root.
+    // Definitions are metadata even before an unbound token is authenticated.
+    this.transactions[this.transactions.length - 1]?.undo.push(() => {
+      if (record.state === "provisional") record.state = "discarded";
+    });
+    return body;
+  }
+
+  /** Detached region: attachment is separate so movement need not cancel it. */
+  createRegion(body: BodyEpoch): EmissionRegion {
+    this.contentWrite(body);
+    const region = this.handle("region");
+    this.nodes.set(region, { body, provenance: this.node(body).provenance });
+    return region;
+  }
+
+  private link(from: EmissionOwner, to: Node, useSiteIdentity?: object): OwnershipEdge {
+    const body = this.contentWrite(from);
+    this.node(to);
+    const edge = this.handle("edge");
+    const record: EdgeRecord = { from, to, active: true, useSiteIdentity };
+    this.journal(() => {
+      record.active = false;
+    }, body);
+    if (this.transactions[this.transactions.length - 1]?.domain !== "content") this.body(body).unscopedContent = true;
+    this.edges.set(edge, record);
+    return edge;
+  }
+
+  attachRegion(owner: EmissionOwner, region: EmissionRegion): OwnershipEdge {
+    const body = this.contentWrite(owner);
+    if (this.node(region).body !== body) throw new Error("Cross-body region attachment requires a new epoch");
+    // Only containment edges participate. Recursive body adoption remains legal.
+    const pending: EmissionOwner[] = [region];
+    const seen = new Set<EmissionOwner>();
+    while (pending.length) {
+      const cursor = pending.pop()!;
+      if (cursor === owner) throw new Error("Region containment cycle");
+      if (seen.has(cursor)) continue;
+      seen.add(cursor);
+      for (const edge of this.edges.values()) {
+        if (edge.active && edge.from === cursor && edge.to.kind === "region") pending.push(edge.to);
+      }
+    }
+    if ([...this.edges.values()].some((e) => e.active && e.to === region)) {
+      throw new Error("Region already has an owner; detach before moving");
+    }
+    return this.link(owner, region);
+  }
+
+  recordOccurrence(
+    owner: EmissionOwner,
+    provenance: EmissionProvenance,
+    binding?: PendingNodeBinding,
+  ): EmissionOccurrence {
+    const body = this.contentWrite(owner);
+    if (binding && this.pendingNodes.has(binding.nodeIdentity))
+      throw new Error("Pending node already has an occurrence");
+    const occurrence = this.handle("occurrence");
+    this.nodes.set(occurrence, { body, provenance: Object.freeze({ ...provenance }) });
+    this.memberships.set(occurrence, this.link(owner, occurrence));
+    if (binding) {
+      this.pendingBindings.set(occurrence, Object.freeze({ ...binding }));
+      this.pendingNodes.add(binding.nodeIdentity);
+    }
+    return occurrence;
+  }
+
+  /** Stable membership, available to staging movement/removal validation. */
+  occurrenceMembership(occurrence: EmissionOccurrence): OwnershipEdge {
+    this.node(occurrence);
+    const edge = this.memberships.get(occurrence);
+    if (!edge) throw new Error("Unknown occurrence membership");
+    return edge;
+  }
+
+  removeOccurrence(occurrence: EmissionOccurrence): void {
+    this.detachEdge(this.occurrenceMembership(occurrence));
+  }
+
+  /** Adopt exactly this candidate, never a function name or registry entry. */
+  adoptBody(owner: EmissionOwner, body: BodyEpoch, useSiteIdentity?: object): OwnershipEdge {
+    this.contentWrite(owner);
+    if (this.body(body).state === "discarded") throw new Error("Cannot adopt a discarded body epoch");
+    if (useSiteIdentity && [...this.edges.values()].some((e) => e.active && e.useSiteIdentity === useSiteIdentity)) {
+      throw new Error("Use site already has an adoption target");
+    }
+    return this.link(owner, body, useSiteIdentity);
+  }
+
+  detachEdge(edge: OwnershipEdge): void {
+    this.collecting();
+    const record = this.edges.get(edge);
+    if (!record) throw new Error("Unknown or foreign ownership edge");
+    const body = this.contentWrite(record.from);
+    if (!record.active) throw new Error("Ownership edge is already detached");
+    this.journal(() => {
+      record.active = true;
+    }, body);
+    if (this.transactions[this.transactions.length - 1]?.domain !== "content") this.body(body).unscopedContent = true;
+    record.active = false;
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * Freeze a completed provisional instruction arena for possible reuse.
+   * This is NOT transaction commit or publication. Intentionally survives an
+   * enclosing probe rollback. Call only once the candidate's internal scopes
+   * are settled; later edits/replacements must use a NEW epoch.
+   */
+  retainBodyEpoch(body: BodyEpoch): void {
+    this.editable(body);
+    if (this.transactions.some((f) => f.domain === "unbound-content" || (f.domain === "content" && f.body === body))) {
+      throw new Error("Close internal body transactions before retaining its epoch");
+    }
+    if (this.body(body).construction === "failed") throw new Error("Cannot retain failed body construction");
+    const current = this.transactions[this.transactions.length - 1]?.token;
+    // Compatibility-only conservative caller guard for the original 21 cases.
+    // It does NOT authenticate completion; adapters must use completeBodyEpoch.
+    if (this.body(body).construction !== "complete" && current && !this.body(body).creationScopes.includes(current)) {
+      throw new Error("Close internal body transactions before retaining its epoch");
+    }
+    const outgoing = new Map<EmissionOwner, Node[]>();
+    for (const edge of this.edges.values()) {
+      if (!edge.active || this.node(edge.from).body !== body) continue;
+      const children = outgoing.get(edge.from) ?? [];
+      children.push(edge.to);
+      outgoing.set(edge.from, children);
+    }
+    this.body(body).retained = new Map([...outgoing].map(([owner, children]) => [owner, Object.freeze(children)]));
+    this.body(body).state = "retained";
+  }
+
+  /** Checks both directions of a staging-supplied body census. */
+  private validateBodyManifest(manifest: RetainedBodyManifest): void {
+    const record = this.body(manifest.body);
+    if (
+      !record.identity ||
+      record.identity.bodyIdentity !== manifest.bodyIdentity ||
+      record.identity.bindingEnvironment !== manifest.bindingEnvironment
+    ) {
+      throw new Error("Stale body or binding-environment identity");
+    }
+    const seen = new Set<EmissionOwner>();
+    const regions = new Set<EmissionRegion>();
+    const occurrences = new Map<EmissionOccurrence, EmissionOwner>();
+    const adoptions = new Map<OwnershipEdge, EdgeRecord>();
+    const pending: EmissionOwner[] = [manifest.body];
+    while (pending.length) {
+      const owner = pending.pop()!;
+      if (seen.has(owner)) continue;
+      seen.add(owner);
+      for (const [id, edge] of this.edges) {
+        if (!edge.active || edge.from !== owner) continue;
+        if (edge.to.kind === "region") {
+          regions.add(edge.to);
+          pending.push(edge.to);
+        } else if (edge.to.kind === "occurrence") occurrences.set(edge.to, owner);
+        else adoptions.set(id, edge);
+      }
+    }
+    if (
+      manifest.regions.length !== regions.size ||
+      new Set(manifest.regions).size !== regions.size ||
+      manifest.regions.some((r) => !regions.has(r))
+    )
+      throw new Error("Retained region census mismatch");
+    if (manifest.occurrences.length !== occurrences.size) throw new Error("Pending node census mismatch");
+    const seenOccurrences = new Set<EmissionOccurrence>();
+    for (const item of manifest.occurrences) {
+      const binding = this.pendingBindings.get(item.occurrence);
+      if (
+        seenOccurrences.has(item.occurrence) ||
+        occurrences.get(item.occurrence) !== item.owner ||
+        !binding ||
+        binding.nodeIdentity !== item.nodeIdentity ||
+        binding.kind !== item.kind
+      ) {
+        throw new Error("Pending node census mismatch");
+      }
+      seenOccurrences.add(item.occurrence);
+    }
+    if (manifest.adoptions.length !== adoptions.size) throw new Error("Adoption census mismatch");
+    const seenEdges = new Set<OwnershipEdge>();
+    for (const item of manifest.adoptions) {
+      const edge = adoptions.get(item.edge);
+      if (
+        seenEdges.has(item.edge) ||
+        !edge ||
+        edge.from !== item.owner ||
+        edge.to !== item.body ||
+        edge.useSiteIdentity !== item.useSiteIdentity ||
+        !edge.useSiteIdentity
+      )
+        throw new Error("Adoption census mismatch");
+      seenEdges.add(item.edge);
+    }
+  }
+
+  /** Authenticated adapter entry; legacy retainBodyEpoch alone cannot mint it. */
+  completeBodyEpoch(body: BodyEpoch, manifest: RetainedBodyManifest): CompletedBody {
+    this.editable(body);
+    if (manifest.body !== body) throw new Error("Wrong body completion manifest");
+    const record = this.body(body);
+    if (record.construction !== "complete" || record.unscopedContent) {
+      throw new Error("Completion requires settled explicit body-owned construction");
+    }
+    if (this.transactions.some((f) => f.domain === "unbound-content" || (f.domain === "content" && f.body === body))) {
+      throw new Error("Close internal body transactions before completion");
+    }
+    this.validateBodyManifest(manifest);
+    this.retainBodyEpoch(body);
+    const receipt = this.handle("completed-body");
+    record.receipt = receipt;
+    record.manifest = Object.freeze({
+      body,
+      bodyIdentity: manifest.bodyIdentity,
+      bindingEnvironment: manifest.bindingEnvironment,
+      regions: Object.freeze([...manifest.regions]),
+      occurrences: Object.freeze(manifest.occurrences.map((o) => Object.freeze({ ...o }))),
+      adoptions: Object.freeze(manifest.adoptions.map((a) => Object.freeze({ ...a }))),
+    });
+    this.completions.set(receipt, body);
+    return receipt;
+  }
+
+  /** Explicit ordinary-owner discard. A selected edge to it makes seal fail. */
+  discardBodyEpoch(body: BodyEpoch): void {
+    this.collecting();
+    const record = this.body(body);
+    if (record.state === "discarded") throw new Error("Body epoch is already discarded");
+    const previous = record.state;
+    this.journal(() => {
+      record.state = previous;
+    }, body);
+    record.state = "discarded";
+  }
+
+  createPublication(kind: "function" | "module-init", provenance: EmissionProvenance): SourcePublication {
+    this.collecting();
+    const publication = this.handle("publication");
+    this.publications.set(publication, { kind, provenance: Object.freeze({ ...provenance }) });
+    return publication;
+  }
+
+  /** Ordinary published functions are roots BEFORE DCE, even if never called. */
+  publishBody(publication: SourcePublication, body: BodyEpoch): void {
+    this.collecting();
+    if (!this.publications.has(publication)) throw new Error("Unknown or foreign source publication");
+    if (this.body(body).state === "discarded") throw new Error("Cannot publish a discarded body epoch");
+    const previous = this.roots.get(publication);
+    this.journal(() => {
+      if (previous) this.roots.set(publication, previous);
+      else this.roots.delete(publication);
+    });
+    this.roots.set(publication, body);
+  }
+
+  unpublishBody(publication: SourcePublication): void {
+    this.collecting();
+    if (!this.publications.has(publication)) throw new Error("Unknown or foreign source publication");
+    const previous = this.roots.get(publication);
+    this.journal(() => {
+      if (previous) this.roots.set(publication, previous);
+    });
+    this.roots.delete(publication);
+  }
+
+  private computeSourceOwnership(): SealedSourceOwnership {
+    if (this.transactions.length !== 0) throw new Error("Cannot seal with open emission transactions");
+    const seen = new Set<Node>();
+    const bodies: BodyEpoch[] = [];
+    const regions: EmissionRegion[] = [];
+    const occurrences: SelectedOccurrence[] = [];
+    const pending: Node[] = [...this.roots.values()];
+    while (pending.length > 0) {
+      const node = pending.pop()!;
+      if (seen.has(node)) continue;
+      seen.add(node);
+      const record = this.node(node);
+      const body = this.body(record.body);
+      if (body.state !== "retained" || !body.retained) {
+        throw new Error("Selected ownership reaches an unfinished or discarded body epoch");
+      }
+      if (node.kind === "occurrence") {
+        occurrences.push(Object.freeze({ occurrence: node, body: record.body, provenance: record.provenance }));
+      } else {
+        if (node.kind === "body") bodies.push(node);
+        else regions.push(node);
+        for (const child of body.retained.get(node) ?? []) pending.push(child);
+      }
+    }
+    return Object.freeze({
+      publications: Object.freeze(
+        [...this.roots].map(([publication, body]) =>
+          Object.freeze({
+            publication,
+            body,
+            ...this.publications.get(publication)!,
+          }),
+        ),
+      ),
+      bodies: Object.freeze(bodies),
+      regions: Object.freeze(regions),
+      occurrences: Object.freeze(occurrences),
+      unselectedBodies: Object.freeze(
+        [...this.bodies].filter(([id, r]) => !seen.has(id) && r.state !== "discarded").map(([id]) => id),
+      ),
+      discardedBodies: Object.freeze([...this.bodies].filter(([, r]) => r.state === "discarded").map(([id]) => id)),
+    });
+  }
+
+  /** Raw ledger snapshot for metadata inspection/tests, NOT adapter approval. */
+  sealSourcePublication(): SealedSourceOwnership {
+    return (this.sealed ??= this.computeSourceOwnership());
+  }
+
+  /** Mandatory future adapter barrier. Failure never caches partial approval. */
+  sealWithManifest(manifest: SelectedBodyManifest): VerifiedSourceOwnership {
+    const ownership = this.sealed ?? this.computeSourceOwnership();
+    if (manifest.publications.length !== ownership.publications.length)
+      throw new Error("Selected root census mismatch");
+    const rootSlots = new Set<SourcePublication>();
+    for (const item of manifest.publications) {
+      const body = this.completions.get(item.receipt);
+      if (rootSlots.has(item.publication) || !body || this.roots.get(item.publication) !== body) {
+        throw new Error("Selected root census mismatch");
+      }
+      rootSlots.add(item.publication);
+    }
+    if (manifest.bodies.length !== ownership.bodies.length) throw new Error("Selected body census mismatch");
+    const selected = new Set(ownership.bodies);
+    for (const item of manifest.bodies) {
+      if (!selected.delete(item.body) || !this.body(item.body).receipt)
+        throw new Error("Selected body has no authenticated receipt");
+      this.validateBodyManifest(item);
+    }
+    if (this.verified) return this.verified;
+    const manifests = ownership.bodies.map((body) => this.body(body).manifest!);
+    this.verified = Object.freeze({
+      ownership,
+      manifests: Object.freeze(manifests),
+      requiredOccurrences: Object.freeze(
+        manifests.flatMap((m) => m.occurrences.filter((o) => o.kind === "required-drive").map((o) => o.occurrence)),
+      ),
+    });
+    this.sealed = ownership;
+    return this.verified;
+  }
+}

index 0000000000..f809b290a2

@@ -0,0 +1,88 @@
+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+/**
+ * Exact historical whole-SourceFile override facts. No receiver resolution,
+ * compiler context, module-wide admission, or runtime registration.
+ * Keep separate SourceFile/name caches and assignment-first short circuiting.
+ * SourceFile identity (not its filename) is the cache key; sources are immutable.
+ */
+import { ts, forEachChild } from "../../ts-api.js";
+
+const _reassignmentCache = new WeakMap<ts.SourceFile, Map<string, boolean>>();
+export function sourceMethodReassignmentFact(sf: ts.SourceFile | undefined, methodName: string): boolean {
+  if (!sf) return false;
+  let perFile = _reassignmentCache.get(sf);
+  if (perFile === undefined) {
+    perFile = new Map<string, boolean>();
+    _reassignmentCache.set(sf, perFile);
+  }
+  const cached = perFile.get(methodName);
+  if (cached !== undefined) return cached;
+
+  let found = false;
+  function visit(node: ts.Node): void {
+    if (found) return;
+    if (
+      ts.isBinaryExpression(node) &&
+      node.operatorToken.kind === ts.SyntaxKind.EqualsToken &&
+      ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(node.left) &&
+      ts.isIdentifier(node.left.name) &&
+      node.left.name.text === methodName
+    ) {
+      found = true;
+      return;
+    }
+    forEachChild(node, visit);
+  }
+  visit(sf);
+  perFile.set(methodName, found);
+  return found;
+}
+
+const _definePropertyCache = new WeakMap<ts.SourceFile, Map<string, boolean>>();
+export function sourceMethodDefinitionFact(sf: ts.SourceFile | undefined, methodName: string): boolean {
+  if (!sf) return false;
+  let perFile = _definePropertyCache.get(sf);
+  if (perFile === undefined) {
+    perFile = new Map<string, boolean>();
+    _definePropertyCache.set(sf, perFile);
+  }
+  const cached = perFile.get(methodName);
+  if (cached !== undefined) return cached;
+
+  let found = false;
+  function visit(node: ts.Node): void {
+    if (found) return;
+    if (ts.isCallExpression(node) && ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(node.expression)) {
+      const callee = node.expression.name.text;
+      if (callee === "defineProperty" && node.arguments.length >= 2) {
+        const key = node.arguments[1];
+        if (key !== undefined && ts.isStringLiteralLike(key) && key.text === methodName) {
+          found = true;
+          return;
+        }
+      }
+      if (callee === "defineProperties" && node.arguments.length >= 2) {
+        const descs = node.arguments[1];
+        if (descs !== undefined && ts.isObjectLiteralExpression(descs)) {
+          for (const p of descs.properties) {
+            const n = p.name;
+            if (n === undefined) continue;
+            if ((ts.isIdentifier(n) || ts.isStringLiteralLike(n)) && n.text === methodName) {
+              found = true;
+              return;
+            }
+          }
+        }
+      }
+    }
+    forEachChild(node, visit);
+  }
+  visit(sf);
+  perFile.set(methodName, found);
+  return found;
+}
+
+/** Pure assignment-or-definition fact; this alone does not admit any lowering. */
+export function sourceMethodOverrideFacts(sf: ts.SourceFile | undefined, methodName: string): boolean {
+  return sourceMethodReassignmentFact(sf, methodName) || sourceMethodDefinitionFact(sf, methodName);
+}

+/** Emit the canonical LHS before the RHS; retain both across user callbacks. */
+function protocolOverrideTarget(ctx: CodegenContext, fctx: FunctionContext, key: string): number {
+  const brand = ensureArrayNativeProtoGlue(ctx);
+  ensureLateImport(ctx, "__box_symbol", [{ kind: "i32" }], [{ kind: "externref" }]);
+  flushLateImportShifts(ctx, fctx);
+  if (brand === undefined || !emitLazyNativeProtoGet(ctx, fctx, brand)) {
+    throw new Error("Iterator override requires the canonical Array.prototype store");
+  }
+  const target = allocLocal(fctx, "__protocol_override_target", { kind: "externref" });
+  fctx.body.push({ op: "local.set", index: target });
+  // Intern while source registration is open; the actual key is pushed later.
+  if (key === "values") stringConstantExternrefInstrs(ctx, key);
+  flushLateImportShifts(ctx, fctx);
+  return target;
+}
+
+function protocolOverrideKey(ctx: CodegenContext, key: string): Instr[] {
+  return key === "@@iterator"
+    ? [
+        { op: "i32.const", value: 1 },
+        { op: "call", funcIdx: ctx.funcMap.get("__box_symbol")! },
+      ]
+    : stringConstantExternrefInstrs(ctx, "values");
+}

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { profilePhase } from "../compile-profile.js";
+import type { CodegenContext } from "./context/types.js";
+import { finalizeExternGetReceiverEntry } from "./object-runtime.js";
+import { fillNativeReflectOwnPropertyMop, fillNativeReflectTargetClassifier } from "./reflect-construct-native.js";
+
+/**
+ * Both pipelines call this after graph-wide type registration and all reader
+ * prependers, before late declared-function collection and dead elimination.
+ * Reflect guards compiled earlier may have missed a later NativeProto type.
+ * Finish their classifiers first, then capture the explicit Receiver at the
+ * true reader entry before an inherited getter/proxy can reenter Get.
+ */
+export function finalizeNativeReaderEntry(ctx: CodegenContext, profileReceiver = false): void {
+  fillNativeReflectTargetClassifier(ctx);
+  fillNativeReflectOwnPropertyMop(ctx);
+  if (profileReceiver) {
+    profilePhase("finalize-extern-get-receiver-entry", () => finalizeExternGetReceiverEntry(ctx));
+  } else {
+    finalizeExternGetReceiverEntry(ctx);
+  }
+}

+function prependIteratorRecordDelete(ctx: CodegenContext, lookupIdx: number, deletePropIdx: number | undefined): void {
+  // The generic carrier-delete helper deliberately has a narrower inventory
+  // than the instance predicate. Route only IterRec through its identity bag;

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import type { Instr, ValType } from "../ir/types.js";
+import type { CodegenContext } from "./context/types.js";
+import { canonicalUndefinedExternInstrs } from "./any-helpers.js";
+import { getStringToNumberProvider, getToPrimitiveProvider } from "./coercion-engine.js";
+import { mintDefinedFunc, pushDefinedFunc } from "./func-space.js";
+import {
+  ensureNativeIteratorRuntime,
+  getOrRegisterIterRecType,
+  ITER_FAMILY_ARRAY,
+  ITER_KIND_VEC,
+} from "./iterator-native.js";
+import { nativeStringLiteralInstrs } from "./native-strings.js";
+import { ensureObjectRuntime } from "./object-runtime.js";
+import { buildArrayLikeToLengthFromExternref } from "./object-runtime-enumeration.js";
+import { addFuncType, getOrRegisterVecType } from "./registry/types.js";
+import { addUnionImportsViaRegistry } from "./shared.js";
+
+const PAYLOAD = "__LiveArrayIterator";
+const PRODUCER = "__live_array_values";
+const STEP = "__live_array_next";
+const LENGTH = "__live_array_length";
+// WasmGC structurally canonicalizes types: the RTT alone is not a brand.
+const BRAND = 0x4c415252;
+const EXTERN: ValType = { kind: "externref" };
+
+/** Read-only fill-time lookup; never registers dependencies or caches indices. */
+export function liveArrayIteratorDeps(ctx: CodegenContext): { payloadTypeIdx: number; nextIdx: number } | undefined {
+  const payloadTypeIdx = ctx.structMap.get(PAYLOAD);
+  const nextIdx = ctx.funcMap.get(STEP);
+  return payloadTypeIdx === undefined || nextIdx === undefined ? undefined : { payloadTypeIdx, nextIdx };
+}
+
+/** Fresh predicate over IterRec.userIter; shape AND immutable nominal tag. */
+export function liveArrayPayloadTest(recTypeIdx: number, payloadTypeIdx: number, recLocal: number): Instr[] {
+  const payload = (): Instr[] => [
+    { op: "local.get", index: recLocal },
+    { op: "struct.get", typeIdx: recTypeIdx, fieldIdx: 3 },
+    { op: "any.convert_extern" },
+  ];
+  return [
+    { op: "local.get", index: recLocal },
+    { op: "struct.get", typeIdx: recTypeIdx, fieldIdx: 0 },
+    { op: "i32.const", value: ITER_KIND_VEC },
+    { op: "i32.eq" },
+    ...payload(),
+    { op: "ref.test", typeIdx: payloadTypeIdx },
+    {
+      op: "if",
+      blockType: { kind: "val", type: { kind: "i32" } },
+      then: [
+        ...payload(),
+        { op: "ref.cast", typeIdx: payloadTypeIdx },
+        { op: "struct.get", typeIdx: payloadTypeIdx, fieldIdx: 3 },
+        { op: "i32.const", value: BRAND },
+        { op: "i32.eq" },
+      ],
+      else: [{ op: "i32.const", value: 0 }],
+    },
+    { op: "i32.and" },
+  ];
+}
+
+/**
+ * Opt-in producer: (original source externref) -> IterRec externref.
+ * Call during registration/codegen, BEFORE iterator finalization. The caller
+ * must already have resolved the intrinsic Array.prototype.values method.
+ * This is NOT GetIterator: override lookup, receiver validation and next-method
+ * capture remain the caller's responsibility. No legacy producer opts in here.
+ */
+export function ensureNativeLiveArrayValues(ctx: CodegenContext): number {
+  const existing = ctx.funcMap.get(PRODUCER);
+  if (existing !== undefined) return existing;
+  if (!ctx.standalone && !ctx.wasi) throw new Error("Live array iterator requires the native runtime");
+  ensureNativeIteratorRuntime(ctx);
+  ensureObjectRuntime(ctx);
+  addUnionImportsViaRegistry(ctx);
+  if (
+    getToPrimitiveProvider(ctx) === undefined ||
+    getStringToNumberProvider(ctx) === undefined ||
+    !ctx.funcMap.has("__typeof_string") ||
+    !ctx.funcMap.has("__unbox_number") ||
+    !ctx.funcMap.has("__extern_get") ||
+    !ctx.funcMap.has("number_toString") ||
+    ctx.nativeStrTypeIdx < 0
+  )
+    throw new Error("Live array iterator requires semantic Get and complete ToLength providers");
+
+  // Establish conversion/error dependencies before capturing call handles.
+  const undef = canonicalUndefinedExternInstrs(ctx);
+  const toLength = buildArrayLikeToLengthFromExternref(ctx, ctx.symbolTypeIdx, "number");
+  const recTypeIdx = getOrRegisterIterRecType(ctx);
+  const vecTypeIdx = getOrRegisterVecType(ctx, "externref", EXTERN);
+  const payloadTypeIdx = ctx.mod.types.length;
+  const fields = [
+    { name: "source", type: EXTERN, mutable: false },
+    { name: "nextIndex", type: { kind: "f64" as const }, mutable: true },
+    { name: "exhausted", type: { kind: "i32" as const }, mutable: true },
+    { name: "brand", type: { kind: "i32" as const }, mutable: false },
+  ];
+  ctx.mod.types.push({ kind: "struct", name: PAYLOAD, fields });
+  ctx.structMap.set(PAYLOAD, payloadTypeIdx);
+  ctx.typeIdxToStructName.set(payloadTypeIdx, PAYLOAD);
+  ctx.structFields.set(PAYLOAD, fields);
+  const register = (name: string, results: ValType[], locals: { name: string; type: ValType }[], body: Instr[]) => {
+    const typeIdx = addFuncType(ctx, [EXTERN], results);
+    const funcIdx = mintDefinedFunc(ctx);
+    ctx.funcMap.set(name, funcIdx);
+    pushDefinedFunc(ctx, funcIdx, { name, typeIdx, locals, body, exported: false });
+  };
+  register(
+    LENGTH,
+    [{ kind: "f64" }],
+    [
+      { name: "pad", type: EXTERN },
+      { name: "length", type: { kind: "f64" } },
+      { name: "truncated", type: { kind: "f64" } },
+      { name: "primitive", type: EXTERN },
+    ],
+    [
+      { op: "local.get", index: 0 },
+      ...nativeStringLiteralInstrs(ctx, "length"),
+      { op: "extern.convert_any" },
+      { op: "call", funcIdx: ctx.funcMap.get("__extern_get")! },
+      ...toLength,
+    ],
+  );
+  const load = (): Instr[] => [{ op: "local.get", index: 1 }];
+  const source = (): Instr[] => [...load(), { op: "struct.get", typeIdx: payloadTypeIdx, fieldIdx: 0 }];
+  register(
+    STEP,
+    [{ kind: "i32" }, EXTERN],
+    [
+      { name: "payload", type: { kind: "ref_null", typeIdx: payloadTypeIdx } },
+      { name: "index", type: { kind: "f64" } },
+    ],
+    [
+      { op: "local.get", index: 0 },
+      { op: "any.convert_extern" },
+      { op: "ref.cast", typeIdx: payloadTypeIdx },
+      { op: "local.set", index: 1 },
+      ...load(),
+      { op: "struct.get", typeIdx: payloadTypeIdx, fieldIdx: 2 },
+      { op: "if", blockType: { kind: "empty" }, then: [{ op: "i32.const", value: 1 }, ...undef, { op: "return" }] },
+      // Capture index BEFORE observable length Get, as CreateArrayIterator does.
+      ...load(),
+      { op: "struct.get", typeIdx: payloadTypeIdx, fieldIdx: 1 },
+      { op: "local.tee", index: 2 },
+      ...source(),
+      { op: "call", funcIdx: ctx.funcMap.get(LENGTH)! },
+      { op: "f64.ge" },
+      {
+        op: "if",
+        blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+        then: [
+          ...load(),
+          { op: "i32.const", value: 1 },
+          { op: "struct.set", typeIdx: payloadTypeIdx, fieldIdx: 2 },
+          { op: "i32.const", value: 1 },
+          ...canonicalUndefinedExternInstrs(ctx),
+          { op: "return" },
+        ],
+      },
+      // Commit before indexed Get: throws and reentrant next consume this index.
+      ...load(),
+      { op: "local.get", index: 2 },
+      { op: "f64.const", value: 1 },
+      { op: "f64.add" },
+      { op: "struct.set", typeIdx: payloadTypeIdx, fieldIdx: 1 },
+      { op: "i32.const", value: 0 },
+      ...source(),
+      { op: "local.get", index: 2 },
+      // ArrayIterator next performs Get(source, ToString(index)), including
+      // callable receivers and descriptor/proxy accessors. The storage-index
+      // helper does not cover that full receiver domain.
+      { op: "call", funcIdx: ctx.funcMap.get("number_toString")! },
+      { op: "call", funcIdx: ctx.funcMap.get("__extern_get")! },
+    ],
+  );
+  register(
+    PRODUCER,
+    [EXTERN],
+    [],
+    [
+      { op: "i32.const", value: ITER_KIND_VEC },
+      { op: "ref.null", typeIdx: vecTypeIdx },
+      { op: "i32.const", value: 0 },
+      { op: "local.get", index: 0 },
+      { op: "f64.const", value: 0 },
+      { op: "i32.const", value: 0 },
+      { op: "i32.const", value: BRAND },
+      { op: "struct.new", typeIdx: payloadTypeIdx },
+      { op: "extern.convert_any" },
+      { op: "i32.const", value: ITER_FAMILY_ARRAY },
+      { op: "struct.new", typeIdx: recTypeIdx },
+      { op: "extern.convert_any" },
+    ],
+  );
+  ctx.nativeIteratorUserArmPending = true;
+  return ctx.funcMap.get(PRODUCER)!;
+}

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+/** Semantic Get for captured iterator protocols. No callability or iteration here. */
+import type { Instr, ValType } from "../ir/types.js";
+import type { CodegenContext, FunctionContext } from "./context/types.js";
+import { ensureObjectRuntime } from "./object-runtime.js";
+import { ensureIterRecPrototypeHelper } from "./iterator-proto-next.js";
+import { ensureArrayNativeProtoGlue } from "./array-object-proto.js";
+import { ensureNativeProtoCompanionSeeder } from "./native-proto.js";
+import { ensureNativeGeneratorProtocol } from "./generators-native-protocol.js";
+import { reserveProtoIndexStore } from "./proto-index-store.js";
+import { BUILTIN_BRAND_BASE } from "./builtin-brands.js";
+import { canonicalUndefinedExternInstrs, ensureExternStrictEqHelper } from "./any-helpers.js";
+import { addStringConstantGlobals } from "./registry/imports.js";
+import { stringConstantExternrefInstrs } from "./native-strings.js";
+import { reserveMemberGetDispatch } from "./member-get-dispatch.js";
+import { reserveVecOverlayHelpers } from "./vec-overlay.js";
+import { mintDefinedFunc, pushDefinedFunc, definedFuncAt } from "./func-space.js";
+import { addFuncType } from "./registry/types.js";
+import { flushLateImportShifts } from "./shared.js";
+
+const ER: ValType = { kind: "externref" };
+const GET = "__iterator_protocol_get";
+const TARGET = "__iterator_protocol_get_target";
+const KEY = "__iterator_protocol_key";
+const registering = new WeakSet<CodegenContext>();
+const load = (index: number): Instr => ({ op: "local.get", index });
+const call = (ctx: CodegenContext, name: string): Instr => {
+  const funcIdx = ctx.funcMap.get(name);
+  if (funcIdx === undefined) throw new Error(`Iterator protocol Get requires ${name}`);
+  return { op: "call", funcIdx };
+};
+const arm = (then: Instr[]): Instr => ({ op: "if", blockType: { kind: "empty" }, then });
+
+/**
+ * Call during dependency registration, BEFORE compiling source bodies/module
+ * init, so intercepted writes before the first Promise call are published too.
+ * funcMap reservations are also the opt-in flag and survive index shifting.
+ * Parent must finalizeExternGetReceiverEntry AFTER every property-read fill.
+ * IterRec own storage and general vector prototype writes remain prerequisites
+ * of the shared storage owners (see lane B handoff); no cache substitutes here.
+ */
+export function ensureIteratorProtocolGetRuntime(ctx: CodegenContext, fctx: FunctionContext): void {
+  flushLateImportShifts(ctx, fctx);
+  const existing = ctx.funcMap.get(GET);
+  if (existing !== undefined) {
+    const fn = definedFuncAt(ctx, existing);
+    if (!fn || (fn.body[0]?.op === "unreachable" && !registering.has(ctx))) {
+      throw new Error("Iterator protocol Get registration did not complete");
+    }
+    return;
+  }
+  if (!ctx.standalone) throw new Error("Captured iterator protocol Get requires the standalone property provider");
+  // Reserve both stable handles before recursive runtime registration. Bodies
+  // are emitted once, after all allocating dependencies have settled.
+  for (const [name, count] of [
+    [GET, 2],
+    [TARGET, 3],
+  ] as const) {
+    const typeIdx = addFuncType(
+      ctx,
+      Array.from({ length: count }, () => ER),
+      [ER],
+    );
+    const idx = mintDefinedFunc(ctx);
+    ctx.funcMap.set(name, idx);
+    pushDefinedFunc(ctx, idx, { name, typeIdx, locals: [], body: [{ op: "unreachable" }], exported: false });
+  }
+  registering.add(ctx);
+  try {
+    emitIteratorProtocolGetRuntime(ctx, fctx);
+  } finally {
+    registering.delete(ctx);
+  }
+}
+
+function emitIteratorProtocolGetRuntime(ctx: CodegenContext, fctx: FunctionContext): void {
+  ctx.protoMemberDirty = true;
+  ensureObjectRuntime(ctx);
+  reserveProtoIndexStore(ctx);
+  reserveVecOverlayHelpers(ctx);
+  const brand = ensureArrayNativeProtoGlue(ctx);
+  if (brand === undefined) throw new Error("Iterator protocol Get requires Array.prototype");
+  ensureNativeProtoCompanionSeeder(ctx, brand);
+  ensureIterRecPrototypeHelper(ctx);
+  ensureNativeGeneratorProtocol(ctx, fctx);
+  addStringConstantGlobals(ctx, ["next", "return", "done", "value", "values"]);
+  for (const key of ["next", "return", "done", "value"]) {
+    if (reserveMemberGetDispatch(ctx, key, fctx) === undefined) {
+      throw new Error(`Iterator protocol Get requires the finalized ${key} reader`);
+    }
+  }
+  if (ensureExternStrictEqHelper(ctx) === undefined) throw new Error("Missing protocol key equality provider");
+  canonicalUndefinedExternInstrs(ctx);
+  flushLateImportShifts(ctx, fctx);
+  const protoType = ctx.nativeProtoTypeIdx;
+  const recType = ctx.structMap.get("__IterRec");
+  if (protoType === undefined || recType === undefined) throw new Error("Missing iterator protocol carrier types");
+  const miss = (): Instr[] => canonicalUndefinedExternInstrs(ctx);
+  const keyType = addFuncType(ctx, [ER], [{ kind: "i32" }]);
+  const keyIdx = mintDefinedFunc(ctx);
+  ctx.funcMap.set(KEY, keyIdx);
+  const keyBody: Instr[] = [
+    load(0),
+    { op: "i32.const", value: 1 },
+    call(ctx, "__box_symbol"),
+    call(ctx, "__extern_strict_eq"),
+    arm([{ op: "i32.const", value: 1 }, { op: "return" }]),
+  ];
+  for (const key of ["next", "return", "done", "value", "values"]) {
+    keyBody.push(
+      load(0),
+      ...stringConstantExternrefInstrs(ctx, key),
+      call(ctx, "__extern_strict_eq"),
+      arm([{ op: "i32.const", value: 1 }, { op: "return" }]),
+    );
+  }
+  keyBody.push({ op: "i32.const", value: 0 });
+  pushDefinedFunc(ctx, keyIdx, { name: KEY, typeIdx: keyType, locals: [], body: keyBody, exported: false });
+  const target = definedFuncAt(ctx, ctx.funcMap.get(TARGET)!)!;
+  target.locals = [{ name: "prototype", type: ER }];
+  target.body.splice(
+    0,
+    1,
+    // Other keys retain the general reader (notably vector indices/length).
+    load(1),
+    call(ctx, KEY),
+    { op: "i32.eqz" },
+    arm([load(0), load(1), load(2), call(ctx, "__reflect_get_receiver"), { op: "return" }]),
+    // Presence, never a value/miss test: an own undefined getter shadows every
+    // inherited method. The bag is read with the original receiver.
+    load(0),
+    load(1),
+    call(ctx, "__carrier_bag_has"),
+    arm([
+      load(0),
+      call(ctx, "__carrier_bag_of"),
+      load(1),
+      load(2),
+      call(ctx, "__reflect_get_receiver"),
+      { op: "return" },
+    ]),
+    // Native prototype descriptors and tombstones share the ordinary writer's
+    // companion. Do not run the static method ladder after a missing entry:
+    // deletion must walk to the parent, never resurrect an intrinsic.
+    load(0),
+    { op: "any.convert_extern" },
+    { op: "ref.test", typeIdx: protoType },
+    arm([
+      load(0),
+      { op: "any.convert_extern" },
+      { op: "ref.cast", typeIdx: protoType },
+      { op: "struct.get", typeIdx: protoType, fieldIdx: 1 },
+      { op: "i32.eqz" },
+      arm([
+        load(2),
+        load(1),
+        call(ctx, "__protoidx_norm_key"),
+        load(0),
+        { op: "any.convert_extern" },
+        { op: "ref.cast", typeIdx: protoType },
+        { op: "struct.get", typeIdx: protoType, fieldIdx: 0 },
+        { op: "i32.const", value: BUILTIN_BRAND_BASE },
+        { op: "i32.sub" },
+        call(ctx, "__protoidx_get_k"),
+        { op: "return" },
+      ]),
+    ]),
+    load(0),
+    { op: "any.convert_extern" },
+    { op: "ref.test", typeIdx: recType },
+    arm([
+      load(0),
+      call(ctx, "__iter_rec_proto"),
+      { op: "local.set", index: 3 },
+      load(3),
+      { op: "ref.is_null" },
+      arm([...miss(), { op: "return" }]),
+      load(3),
+      load(1),
+      load(2),
+      call(ctx, TARGET),
+      { op: "return" },
+    ]),
+    load(0),
+    call(ctx, "__is_vec_prop_carrier"),
+    arm([
+      load(0),
+      call(ctx, "__getPrototypeOf"),
+      { op: "local.set", index: 3 },
+      load(3),
+      { op: "ref.is_null" },
+      arm([...miss(), { op: "return" }]),
+      load(3),
+      load(1),
+      load(2),
+      call(ctx, TARGET),
+      { op: "return" },
+    ]),
+    // The existing generator view maps a frame to its ordinary descriptor /
+    // prototype object and leaves every other carrier unchanged. Read that
+    // view with the explicit Receiver, including trap-absent Proxy forwarding.
+    load(0),
+    call(ctx, "__native_generator_proto_view"),
+    { op: "local.set", index: 3 },
+    load(0),
+    load(3),
+    call(ctx, "__extern_strict_eq"),
+    { op: "i32.eqz" },
+    arm([load(3), load(1), load(2), call(ctx, TARGET), { op: "return" }]),
+    // The finalized member readers include closed methods/accessors and native
+    // generator result fields (with sentinel-aware value boxing). They accept
+    // the target as Receiver, so only use them for the original object, never
+    // for a prototype walk carrying a distinct Receiver.
+    load(0),
+    load(2),
+    call(ctx, "__extern_strict_eq"),
+    arm(
+      ["next", "return", "done", "value"].flatMap((key): Instr[] => [
+        load(1),
+        ...stringConstantExternrefInstrs(ctx, key),
+        call(ctx, "__extern_strict_eq"),
+        arm([load(0), call(ctx, `__get_member_${key}`), { op: "return" }]),
+      ]),
+    ),
+    // Closed shapes, ordinary objects, callable objects, native generators and
+    // proxies retain the finalized reader and its explicit-receiver bridge.
+    load(0),
+    load(1),
+    load(2),
+    call(ctx, "__reflect_get_receiver"),
+  );
+  const get = definedFuncAt(ctx, ctx.funcMap.get(GET)!)!;
+  get.body.splice(0, 1, load(0), load(1), load(0), call(ctx, TARGET));
+}
+
+/** Overlay lookup is minted by the existing late vec fill; splice only then. */
+export function fillIteratorProtocolGetRuntime(ctx: CodegenContext): void {
+  const fn = ctx.mod.functions.find((candidate) => candidate.name === TARGET);
+  if (!fn || fn.locals.some((local) => local.name === "__protocol_overlay")) return;
+  const lookup = ctx.funcMap.get("__vec_overlay_lookup");
+  const object = ctx.objectRuntimeTypes?.objectTypeIdx;
+  if (lookup === undefined || object === undefined) return;
+  const bag = 3 + fn.locals.length;
+  fn.locals.push({ name: "__protocol_overlay", type: { kind: "ref_null", typeIdx: object } });
+  // Named/symbol definitions use the overlay; assignments use the expando bag
+  // unless an overlay entry already exists. Match the existing overlay-first
+  // read precedence, but carry the true Receiver through getter invocation.
+  fn.body.splice(
+    4,
+    0,
+    load(0),
+    call(ctx, "__is_vec_prop_carrier"),
+    arm([
+      load(0),
+      { op: "any.convert_extern" },
+      { op: "call", funcIdx: lookup },
+      { op: "local.tee", index: bag },
+      { op: "ref.is_null" },
+      { op: "i32.eqz" },
+      arm([
+        load(bag),
+        { op: "ref.as_non_null" },
+        load(1),
+        call(ctx, "__obj_find"),
+        { op: "ref.is_null" },
+        { op: "i32.eqz" },
+        arm([
+          load(bag),
+          { op: "extern.convert_any" },
+          load(1),
+          load(2),
+          call(ctx, "__reflect_get_receiver"),
+          { op: "return" },
+        ]),
+      ]),
+    ]),
+  );
+}

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import type { Instr, ValType } from "../ir/types.js";
+import type { CodegenContext } from "./context/types.js";
+import { mintDefinedFunc, pushDefinedFunc } from "./func-space.js";
+import { buildThrowJsErrorInstrs } from "./js-errors.js";
+import { ensureObjectRuntime } from "./object-runtime.js";
+import { addFuncType } from "./registry/types.js";
+import { addUnionImportsViaRegistry } from "./shared.js";
+import { ensureSymbolCarrier } from "./symbol-native.js";
+
+const NAME = "__iterator_receiver_to_object";
+const EXT: ValType = { kind: "externref" };
+
+/**
+ * Runtime ToObject for an already-evaluated reflective iterator receiver.
+ * Unlike Object(value), null/undefined THROW; unlike the static Object(...)
+ * lowering, an unknown primitive is classified and wrapped at runtime.
+ * Objects (including wrappers, arrays, closures and proxies) return unchanged.
+ *
+ * Register before emitting the calling function's instructions. All providers
+ * are native and all dependency registration precedes call-handle capture.
+ * No Get(length), Get(index), ToPrimitive or user constructor is invoked here.
+ */
+export function ensureNativeIteratorReceiverToObject(ctx: CodegenContext): number {
+  const existing = ctx.funcMap.get(NAME);
+  if (existing !== undefined) return existing;
+  if (!ctx.standalone && !ctx.wasi) throw new Error("Native iterator ToObject requires standalone/WASI");
+  ensureObjectRuntime(ctx);
+  addUnionImportsViaRegistry(ctx);
+  const symbolTypeIdx = ensureSymbolCarrier(ctx);
+  const nullishThrow = buildThrowJsErrorInstrs(ctx, "TypeError", "Array.prototype.values called on null or undefined", {
+    forceInModuleCtor: true,
+  });
+  const required = (name: string): number => {
+    const handle = ctx.funcMap.get(name);
+    if (handle === undefined) throw new Error(`Missing native iterator ToObject dependency: ${name}`);
+    return handle;
+  };
+  // Capture only after every ensure above. Missing native dependencies fail
+  // registration rather than adding a host import or treating a primitive as
+  // an object. Each arm owns its instruction objects.
+  const primitiveArm = (predicate: string, wrapper: string, convert: Instr[] = []): Instr[] => [
+    { op: "local.get", index: 0 },
+    { op: "call", funcIdx: required(predicate) },
+    {
+      op: "if",
+      blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+      then: [{ op: "local.get", index: 0 }, ...convert, { op: "call", funcIdx: required(wrapper) }, { op: "return" }],
+    },
+  ];
+  const body: Instr[] = [
+    { op: "local.get", index: 0 },
+    { op: "ref.is_null" },
+    { op: "local.get", index: 0 },
+    { op: "call", funcIdx: required("__extern_is_undefined") },
+    { op: "i32.or" },
+    { op: "if", blockType: { kind: "empty" }, then: nullishThrow },
+    ...primitiveArm("__typeof_number", "__new_Number", [{ op: "call", funcIdx: required("__unbox_number") }]),
+    ...primitiveArm("__typeof_boolean", "__new_Boolean", [
+      { op: "call", funcIdx: required("__unbox_boolean") },
+      { op: "f64.convert_i32_s" },
+    ]),
+    ...primitiveArm("__typeof_string", "__new_String"),
+    // __new_BigInt takes i64 and would narrow a wide primitive. __new_String
+    // is the canonical raw externref wrapper builder (also __new_Symbol's
+    // alias): it stores the value unchanged under WRAPPER_PRIMITIVE_KEY,
+    // without string conversion. Wrapper brand/prototype recovery consults
+    // that stored primitive, not the builder name. Preserve BigInt identity
+    // and precision through that same allocation body.
+    ...primitiveArm("__typeof_bigint", "__new_String"),
+    { op: "local.get", index: 0 },
+    { op: "any.convert_extern" },
+    { op: "ref.test", typeIdx: symbolTypeIdx },
+    {
+      op: "if",
+      blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+      then: [{ op: "local.get", index: 0 }, { op: "call", funcIdx: required("__new_Symbol") }, { op: "return" }],
+    },
+    // Runtime primitive discriminators do not admit wrapper objects. Returning
+    // the original reference preserves identity and avoids observable Gets.
+    { op: "local.get", index: 0 },
+  ];
+  const typeIdx = addFuncType(ctx, [EXT], [EXT]);
+  const funcIdx = mintDefinedFunc(ctx);
+  ctx.funcMap.set(NAME, funcIdx);
+  pushDefinedFunc(ctx, funcIdx, { name: NAME, typeIdx, locals: [], body, exported: false });
+  return funcIdx;
+}

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+/** Metadata only. No CodegenContext, provider registration, or Wasm indices. */
+import { EmissionOwnership } from "./context/emission-ownership.js";
+import type { EmissionOccurrence, SelectedBodyManifest } from "./context/emission-ownership.js";
+
+export type NativeProtoDemandReason = "mixed-generator-then" | "observable-vector";
+export type NativeProtoDemandState = "collecting" | "inactive" | "selected-awaiting-registration";
+export interface NativeProtoDemandRecipe {
+  readonly brand: "Promise";
+  readonly requiredMembers: readonly ["then"];
+  readonly reason: NativeProtoDemandReason;
+  readonly occurrence: EmissionOccurrence;
+}
+export interface SelectedNativeProtoDemand {
+  readonly brand: "Promise";
+  /** Full canonical initializer, never a then-only inventory approximation. */
+  readonly initializer: "full-Promise-brand";
+  readonly requiredMembers: readonly ["then"];
+  readonly receiverAwareGet: true;
+  readonly consumers: readonly NativeProtoDemandRecipe[];
+}
+/** Specification for a future registration receipt, NOT proof minted here.
+ * A provider owner must authenticate actual body/installation identities and
+ * all semantic Get dependencies before exposing a frozen ready capability.
+ * CSV membership, reserved function names, refusal bodies and booleans alone
+ * cannot constitute this receipt. This foundation accepts no such receipts.
+ */
+export interface RequiredNativeProtoMemberReceiptContract {
+  readonly brand: "Promise";
+  readonly member: "then";
+  readonly evidence: "installed-canonical-descriptor-and-supported-body";
+  readonly dependencies: readonly [
+    "semantic-receiver-get",
+    "canonical-companion-initializer",
+    "full-callable-predicate",
+    "apply-driver",
+    "native-TypeError",
+  ];
+}
+
+/** One collection per compilation; the supplied arena is the shared owner.
+ * Transition: collecting -> inactive OR selected-awaiting-registration.
+ * Registration -> ready -> frozen belongs to the future provider owner.
+ * There is deliberately no ready setter, registration callback, or fake body.
+ */
+export class NativeProtoDemandCollection {
+  readonly #ownership: EmissionOwnership;
+  #state: NativeProtoDemandState = "collecting";
+  readonly #recipes: NativeProtoDemandRecipe[] = [];
+  #selected: readonly SelectedNativeProtoDemand[] = Object.freeze([]);
+
+  constructor(ownership: EmissionOwnership) {
+    if (!(ownership instanceof EmissionOwnership)) throw new Error("Shared emission ownership required");
+    this.#ownership = ownership;
+  }
+
+  get state(): NativeProtoDemandState {
+    return this.#state;
+  }
+
+  prepare(recipe: NativeProtoDemandRecipe): NativeProtoDemandRecipe {
+    if (this.#state !== "collecting") throw new Error("Native prototype demand collection is closed");
+    if (recipe.brand !== "Promise" || recipe.requiredMembers.length !== 1 || recipe.requiredMembers[0] !== "then")
+      throw new Error("Unsupported native prototype demand");
+    if (recipe.reason !== "mixed-generator-then" && recipe.reason !== "observable-vector")
+      throw new Error("Unknown native prototype demand reason");
+    // Read-only arena query rejects forged/foreign occurrences. Preparation
+    // does not attach edges, complete bodies, publish roots, or seal ownership.
+    this.#ownership.occurrenceMembership(recipe.occurrence);
+    const old = this.#recipes.find((r) => r.occurrence === recipe.occurrence && r.reason === recipe.reason);
+    if (old) return old;
+    const prepared = Object.freeze({ ...recipe, requiredMembers: Object.freeze(["then"] as const) });
+    this.#recipes.push(prepared);
+    return prepared;
+  }
+
+  /** Verify independent selected-body census using the SAME shared arena.
+   * Never accept raw seal snapshots, AST nodes, booleans, or caller-created
+   * VerifiedSourceOwnership objects as activation authority.
+   */
+  select(manifest: SelectedBodyManifest): readonly SelectedNativeProtoDemand[] {
+    if (this.#state !== "collecting") throw new Error("Native prototype demand selection is closed");
+    const verified = this.#ownership.sealWithManifest(manifest);
+    const required = new Set(verified.requiredOccurrences);
+    const consumers = this.#recipes.filter((r) => required.has(r.occurrence));
+    this.#selected = Object.freeze(
+      consumers.length
+        ? [
+            Object.freeze({
+              brand: "Promise" as const,
+              initializer: "full-Promise-brand" as const,
+              requiredMembers: Object.freeze(["then"] as const),
+              receiverAwareGet: true as const,
+              consumers: Object.freeze(consumers),
+            }),
+          ]
+        : [],
+    );
+    this.#state = consumers.length ? "selected-awaiting-registration" : "inactive";
+    return this.#selected;
+  }
+
+  /** Selected metadata is NOT a ready provider manifest or call admission. */
+  get selected(): readonly SelectedNativeProtoDemand[] {
+    return this.#selected;
+  }
+}
