+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+//
+// (#5197 R3-2) Observable intrinsic Promise.all/race routes.
+//
+// This deliberately sits beside, rather than inside, promise-combinators.ts.
+// #5759 freezes that legacy adapter's source-preservation bridge; the static
+// call dispatcher enters this module only after its observable source gate has
+// admitted a direct literal or vector input.
+
+import { buildTargetTaggedTry } from "../ir/try-table.js";
+import type { FieldDef, Instr, ValType } from "../ir/types.js";
+import {
+  PROMISE_STATE_PENDING,
+  type PromiseExecutorClosures,
+  buildPromiseSettleClosureInstrs,
+  ensureAsyncDriveRuntime,
+  ensurePromiseExecutorClosures,
+} from "./async-scheduler.js";
+import { ensureBuiltinFnMetaType } from "./builtin-fn-meta.js";
+import { emitBuiltinConstructorIdentity } from "./builtin-static-globals.js";
+import { ensureStandaloneBuiltinStaticMethodClosure } from "./builtin-value-read.js";
+import { reserveCarrierBagVisibility } from "./carrier-bag-visibility.js";
+import { buildClosureRefTestArms } from "./closure-classifier.js";
+import { closureBagInitInstr, getOrCreateFuncRefWrapperTypes } from "./closures/funcref-wrapper-types.js";
+import { allocLocal } from "./context/locals.js";
+import type { CodegenContext, FunctionContext } from "./context/types.js";
+import { mintDefinedFunc, pushDefinedFunc } from "./func-space.js";
+import { stringConstantExternrefInstrs } from "./native-strings.js";
+import { ensureObjVecBuilders, ensureObjectRuntime, reserveApplyClosure } from "./object-runtime.js";
+import { ensureCombinatorFunctions } from "./promise-combinators.js";
+import { resolveF64VecArg as resolveObservableF64VecArg } from "./promise-combinator-observable-protocol.js";
+import { emitWasiErrorConstructor } from "./registry/error-types.js";
+import { ensureExnTag } from "./registry/imports.js";
+import { addStringConstantGlobal } from "./registry/imports.js";
+
+const EXTERNREF: ValType = { kind: "externref" };
+
+type AsyncDriveRuntimeT = ReturnType<typeof ensureAsyncDriveRuntime>;
+type CombinatorRuntime = ReturnType<typeof ensureCombinatorFunctions>;
+
+/** `$__combinator_all_resolve_cap` resources registered once per module. */
+interface ObservableCombinatorRuntime {
+  allResolveCapTypeIdx: number;
+  allResolveElemCapsFieldIdx: number;
+  allResolveCalledFieldIdx: number;
+  allResolveMetaTypeIdx: number;
+  exnTagIdx: number;
+  settleClosures: PromiseExecutorClosures;
+}
+
+type CtxWithObservableCombinators = CodegenContext & {
+  __promiseObservableCombinators?: ObservableCombinatorRuntime | null;
+};
+
+interface ObservableCombinatorPreparation {
+  resultLocal: number;
+  ctorLocal: number;
+  resolveLocal: number;
+  abortedLocal: number;
+  raceFulfillLocal: number;
+  rejectLocal: number;
+}
+
+/** Register observable-only plumbing before any detached argument buffer is spliced. */
+function ensureObservableCombinatorRuntime(ctx: CodegenContext, ids: CombinatorRuntime): ObservableCombinatorRuntime {
+  const cache = ctx as CtxWithObservableCombinators;
+  if (cache.__promiseObservableCombinators === null) {
+    throw new Error(
+      "observable Promise combinator pipeline requires closure invocation, property reads, and TypeError runtime support",
+    );
+  }
+  if (cache.__promiseObservableCombinators !== undefined) return cache.__promiseObservableCombinators;
+
+  ensureObjectRuntime(ctx);
+  ensureObjVecBuilders(ctx);
+  reserveCarrierBagVisibility(ctx);
+  const applyClosureIdx = reserveApplyClosure(ctx);
+  ensureStandaloneBuiltinStaticMethodClosure(ctx, "Promise", "resolve");
+  const executorClosures = ensurePromiseExecutorClosures(ctx);
+  emitWasiErrorConstructor(ctx, "TypeError", 1);
+  addStringConstantGlobal(ctx, "Promise resolve is not callable");
+  addStringConstantGlobal(ctx, "Promise then is not callable");
+
+  const externGetIdx = ctx.funcMap.get("__extern_get");
+  const newTypeErrorIdx = ctx.funcMap.get("__new_TypeError");
+  if (
+    executorClosures === null ||
+    externGetIdx === undefined ||
+    applyClosureIdx === undefined ||
+    newTypeErrorIdx === undefined
+  ) {
+    throw new Error(
+      "observable Promise combinator pipeline requires closure invocation, property reads, and TypeError runtime support",
+    );
+  }
+
+  const wrapper = getOrCreateFuncRefWrapperTypes(ctx, [EXTERNREF], []);
+  if (!wrapper) {
+    throw new Error("observable Promise combinator pipeline requires a one-argument closure wrapper");
+  }
+  const allResolveMetaTypeIdx = ensureBuiltinFnMetaType(
+    ctx,
+    wrapper.structTypeIdx,
+    wrapper.closureInfo,
+    "promise:all-resolve-element",
+    "",
+    1,
+  );
+  const metaFields = (ctx.mod.types[allResolveMetaTypeIdx] as { fields: FieldDef[] }).fields;
+  const allResolveElemCapsFieldIdx = metaFields.length;
+  const allResolveCalledFieldIdx = allResolveElemCapsFieldIdx + 1;
+  const allResolveCapTypeIdx = ctx.mod.types.length;
+  const allResolveFields: FieldDef[] = [
+    ...metaFields.map((field) => ({ ...field })),
+    {
+      name: "$elemCaps",
+      type: { kind: "ref", typeIdx: ids.elemCapsTypeIdx },
+      mutable: false,
+    },
+    { name: "$called", type: { kind: "i32" }, mutable: true },
+  ];
+  ctx.mod.types.push({
+    kind: "struct",
+    name: "$__combinator_all_resolve_cap",
+    fields: allResolveFields,
+    superTypeIdx: allResolveMetaTypeIdx,
+  });
+  ctx.structMap.set("$__combinator_all_resolve_cap", allResolveCapTypeIdx);
+  ctx.typeIdxToStructName.set(allResolveCapTypeIdx, "$__combinator_all_resolve_cap");
+  ctx.structFields.set(
+    "$__combinator_all_resolve_cap",
+    allResolveFields.map((field) => ({ ...field })),
+  );
+
+  const allResolveFuncIdx = mintDefinedFunc(ctx);
+  pushDefinedFunc(ctx, allResolveFuncIdx, {
+    name: "__combinator_all_resolve_element",
+    typeIdx: wrapper.liftedFuncTypeIdx,
+    locals: [{ name: "$cap", type: { kind: "ref", typeIdx: allResolveCapTypeIdx } }],
+    body: [
+      { op: "local.get", index: 0 },
+      { op: "ref.cast", typeIdx: allResolveCapTypeIdx },
+      { op: "local.set", index: 2 },
+      { op: "local.get", index: 2 },
+      {
+        op: "struct.get",
+        typeIdx: allResolveCapTypeIdx,
+        fieldIdx: allResolveCalledFieldIdx,
+      },
+      {
+        op: "if",
+        blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+        then: [],
+        else: [
+          { op: "local.get", index: 2 },
+          { op: "i32.const", value: 1 },
+          {
+            op: "struct.set",
+            typeIdx: allResolveCapTypeIdx,
+            fieldIdx: allResolveCalledFieldIdx,
+          },
+          { op: "local.get", index: 2 },
+          {
+            op: "struct.get",
+            typeIdx: allResolveCapTypeIdx,
+            fieldIdx: allResolveElemCapsFieldIdx,
+          },
+          { op: "extern.convert_any" },
+          { op: "local.get", index: 1 },
+          { op: "call", funcIdx: ids.allFulfillFuncIdx },
+          { op: "drop" },
+        ],
+      },
+    ],
+    exported: false,
+  });
+  ctx.funcMap.set("__combinator_all_resolve_element", allResolveFuncIdx);
+
+  const result: ObservableCombinatorRuntime = {
+    allResolveCapTypeIdx,
+    allResolveElemCapsFieldIdx,
+    allResolveCalledFieldIdx,
+    allResolveMetaTypeIdx,
+    exnTagIdx: ensureExnTag(ctx),
+    settleClosures: executorClosures,
+  };
+  cache.__promiseObservableCombinators = result;
+  return result;
+}
+
+function observableReactionFns(
+  ids: CombinatorRuntime,
+  method: "all" | "race",
+): { fulfillIdx: number; rejectIdx: number } {
+  return {
+    fulfillIdx: method === "all" ? ids.allFulfillFuncIdx : ids.raceFulfillFuncIdx,
+    rejectIdx: ids.rejectFuncIdx,
+  };
+}
+
+/** Build `RejectPromise(result, reason)` plus the local abrupt-completion bit. */
+function buildObservableRejectInstrs(
+  rt: AsyncDriveRuntimeT,
+  preparation: ObservableCombinatorPreparation,
+  reasonInstrs: readonly Instr[],
+): Instr[] {
+  return [
+    { op: "local.get", index: preparation.resultLocal },
+    ...reasonInstrs,
+    { op: "call", funcIdx: rt.rejectFuncIdx },
+    { op: "drop" },
+    { op: "i32.const", value: 1 },
+    { op: "local.set", index: preparation.abortedLocal },
+  ];
+}
+
+/** The observable path's `IsCallable` predicate over the shared closure set. */
+function buildObservableCallableCheckInstrs(
+  ctx: CodegenContext,
+  valueLocal: number,
+  callableLocal: number,
+  anyLocal: number,
+): Instr[] {
+  return [
+    { op: "i32.const", value: 0 },
+    { op: "local.set", index: callableLocal },
+    { op: "local.get", index: valueLocal },
+    { op: "ref.is_null" },
+    {
+      op: "if",
+      blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+      then: [],
+      else: [
+        { op: "local.get", index: valueLocal },
+        { op: "any.convert_extern" },
+        { op: "local.set", index: anyLocal },
+        ...buildClosureRefTestArms(ctx, anyLocal, [
+          { op: "i32.const", value: 1 },
+          { op: "local.set", index: callableLocal },
+        ]),
+      ],
+    },
+  ];
+}
+
+/** Emit a native TypeError rejection for a non-callable observable method. */
+function buildObservableTypeErrorRejectInstrs(
+  ctx: CodegenContext,
+  rt: AsyncDriveRuntimeT,
+  preparation: ObservableCombinatorPreparation,
+  message: string,
+): Instr[] {
+  return buildObservableRejectInstrs(rt, preparation, [
+    ...stringConstantExternrefInstrs(ctx, message),
+    { op: "call", funcIdx: ctx.funcMap.get("__new_TypeError")! },
+  ]);
+}
+
+/**
+ * Set up the result carrier and the sole observable `Get(Promise, "resolve")`.
+ * The captured method remains in a local for every element in this invocation.
+ */
+function emitObservableCombinatorPreparation(
+  ctx: CodegenContext,
+  fctx: FunctionContext,
+  ids: CombinatorRuntime,
+  rt: AsyncDriveRuntimeT,
+  observable: ObservableCombinatorRuntime,
+  method: "all" | "race",
+): ObservableCombinatorPreparation {
+  const resultLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_result_${fctx.locals.length}`, {
+    kind: "ref",
+    typeIdx: ids.promiseTypeIdx,
+  });
+  const ctorLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_ctor_${fctx.locals.length}`, EXTERNREF);
+  const resolveLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_resolve_${fctx.locals.length}`, EXTERNREF);
+  const abortedLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_abrupt_${fctx.locals.length}`, { kind: "i32" });
+  const reasonLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_reason_${fctx.locals.length}`, EXTERNREF);
+  const callableLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_callable_${fctx.locals.length}`, { kind: "i32" });
+  const callableAnyLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_callable_any_${fctx.locals.length}`, {
+    kind: "anyref",
+  });
+  const raceFulfillLocal =
+    method === "race" ? allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_race_fulfill_${fctx.locals.length}`, EXTERNREF) : -1;
+  const rejectLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_reject_${fctx.locals.length}`, EXTERNREF);
+  const preparation = {
+    resultLocal,
+    ctorLocal,
+    resolveLocal,
+    abortedLocal,
+    raceFulfillLocal,
+    rejectLocal,
+  };
+
+  fctx.body.push(
+    { op: "i32.const", value: PROMISE_STATE_PENDING },
+    { op: "ref.null.extern" },
+    { op: "ref.null.extern" },
+    closureBagInitInstr(),
+    { op: "struct.new", typeIdx: ids.promiseTypeIdx },
+    { op: "local.set", index: resultLocal },
+    { op: "i32.const", value: 0 },
+    { op: "local.set", index: abortedLocal },
+  );
+
+  if (method === "race") {
+    fctx.body.push(
+      ...buildPromiseSettleClosureInstrs(observable.settleClosures, ctx.funcMap.get("__promise_resolve_cl")!, [
+        { op: "local.get", index: resultLocal },
+      ]),
+      { op: "extern.convert_any" },
+      { op: "local.set", index: raceFulfillLocal },
+    );
+  }
+  fctx.body.push(
+    ...buildPromiseSettleClosureInstrs(observable.settleClosures, ctx.funcMap.get("__promise_reject_cl")!, [
+      { op: "local.get", index: resultLocal },
+    ]),
+    { op: "extern.convert_any" },
+    { op: "local.set", index: rejectLocal },
+  );
+
+  emitBuiltinConstructorIdentity(ctx, fctx, "Promise");
+  fctx.body.push({ op: "local.set", index: ctorLocal });
+  fctx.body.push(
+    buildTargetTaggedTry(
+      ctx,
+      { kind: "empty" },
+      [
+        { op: "local.get", index: ctorLocal },
+        ...stringConstantExternrefInstrs(ctx, "resolve"),
+        { op: "call", funcIdx: ctx.funcMap.get("__extern_get")! },
+        { op: "local.set", index: resolveLocal },
+      ],
+      [
+        {
+          tagIdx: observable.exnTagIdx,
+          body: [
+            { op: "local.set", index: reasonLocal },
+            ...buildObservableRejectInstrs(rt, preparation, [{ op: "local.get", index: reasonLocal }]),
+          ],
+        },
+      ],
+    ),
+  );
+  fctx.body.push(
+    { op: "local.get", index: abortedLocal },
+    { op: "i32.eqz" },
+    {
+      op: "if",
+      blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+      then: [
+        ...buildObservableCallableCheckInstrs(ctx, resolveLocal, callableLocal, callableAnyLocal),
+        { op: "local.get", index: callableLocal },
+        { op: "i32.eqz" },
+        {
+          op: "if",
+          blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+          then: buildObservableTypeErrorRejectInstrs(ctx, rt, preparation, "Promise resolve is not callable"),
+        },
+      ],
+    },
+  );
+  return preparation;
+}
+
+/** Allocate aggregate state after literal inputs have all been materialized. */
+function emitObservableCombinatorState(
+  fctx: FunctionContext,
+  ids: CombinatorRuntime,
+  preparation: ObservableCombinatorPreparation,
+  method: "all" | "race",
+  lengthInstrs: readonly Instr[],
+): { stateLocal: number; arrLocal: number; lengthLocal: number } {
+  const arrLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_arr_${fctx.locals.length}`, {
+    kind: "ref",
+    typeIdx: ids.arrTypeIdx,
+  });
+  const stateLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_state_${fctx.locals.length}`, {
+    kind: "ref",
+    typeIdx: ids.stateTypeIdx,
+  });
+  const lengthLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_length_${fctx.locals.length}`, { kind: "i32" });
+  fctx.body.push(
+    ...lengthInstrs,
+    { op: "local.set", index: lengthLocal },
+    { op: "local.get", index: lengthLocal },
+    { op: "array.new_default", typeIdx: ids.arrTypeIdx },
+    { op: "local.set", index: arrLocal },
+    { op: "local.get", index: preparation.resultLocal },
+    { op: "local.get", index: arrLocal },
+    { op: "local.get", index: lengthLocal },
+    ...(method === "all"
+      ? ([{ op: "i32.const", value: 1 }] satisfies Instr[])
+      : ([{ op: "local.get", index: lengthLocal }] satisfies Instr[])),
+    { op: "struct.new", typeIdx: ids.stateTypeIdx },
+    { op: "local.set", index: stateLocal },
+  );
+  return { stateLocal, arrLocal, lengthLocal };
+}
+
+/** Drop Promise.all's iteration-completion sentinel after every admitted element ran. */
+function emitObservableAllIterationComplete(
+  fctx: FunctionContext,
+  ids: CombinatorRuntime,
+  rt: AsyncDriveRuntimeT,
+  method: "all" | "race",
+  preparation: ObservableCombinatorPreparation,
+  state: { stateLocal: number; arrLocal: number; lengthLocal: number },
+): void {
+  if (method !== "all") return;
+  const remainingLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_remaining_${fctx.locals.length}`, { kind: "i32" });
+  fctx.body.push(
+    { op: "local.get", index: preparation.abortedLocal },
+    { op: "i32.eqz" },
+    {
+      op: "if",
+      blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+      then: [
+        { op: "local.get", index: state.stateLocal },
+        { op: "local.get", index: state.stateLocal },
+        { op: "struct.get", typeIdx: ids.stateTypeIdx, fieldIdx: 3 },
+        { op: "i32.const", value: 1 },
+        { op: "i32.sub" },
+        { op: "local.tee", index: remainingLocal },
+        { op: "struct.set", typeIdx: ids.stateTypeIdx, fieldIdx: 3 },
+        { op: "local.get", index: remainingLocal },
+        { op: "i32.eqz" },
+        {
+          op: "if",
+          blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+          then: [
+            { op: "local.get", index: preparation.resultLocal },
+            { op: "local.get", index: state.lengthLocal },
+            { op: "local.get", index: state.arrLocal },
+            { op: "struct.new", typeIdx: ids.vecTypeIdx },
+            { op: "extern.convert_any" },
+            { op: "call", funcIdx: rt.fulfillFuncIdx },
+            { op: "drop" },
+          ],
+        },
+      ],
+    },
+  );
+}
+
+function buildObservableAllResolveClosureInstrs(
+  ctx: CodegenContext,
+  observable: ObservableCombinatorRuntime,
+  elemCapsLocal: number,
+): Instr[] {
+  return [
+    {
+      op: "ref.func",
+      funcIdx: ctx.funcMap.get("__combinator_all_resolve_element")!,
+    },
+    { op: "i32.const", value: 1 },
+    closureBagInitInstr(),
+    { op: "i32.const", value: 0 },
+    { op: "i32.const", value: observable.allResolveMetaTypeIdx },
+    { op: "local.get", index: elemCapsLocal },
+    { op: "i32.const", value: 0 },
+    { op: "struct.new", typeIdx: observable.allResolveCapTypeIdx },
+    { op: "extern.convert_any" },
+  ];
+}
+
+/** Append one `Call(resolve, C, value)` followed by the one-Get `then` Invoke. */
+function emitObservableCombinatorElement(
+  ctx: CodegenContext,
+  fctx: FunctionContext,
+  ids: CombinatorRuntime,
+  rt: AsyncDriveRuntimeT,
+  observable: ObservableCombinatorRuntime,
+  preparation: ObservableCombinatorPreparation,
+  method: "all" | "race",
+  reaction: { fulfillIdx: number; rejectIdx: number },
+  stateLocal: number,
+  indexInstrs: readonly Instr[],
+  inputInstrs: readonly Instr[],
+): void {
+  const inputLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_input_${fctx.locals.length}`, EXTERNREF);
+  const resolveArgsLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_resolve_args_${fctx.locals.length}`, EXTERNREF);
+  const thenArgsLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_then_args_${fctx.locals.length}`, EXTERNREF);
+  const nextLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_next_${fctx.locals.length}`, EXTERNREF);
+  const errorLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_error_${fctx.locals.length}`, EXTERNREF);
+  const thenLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_then_${fctx.locals.length}`, EXTERNREF);
+  const callableLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_then_callable_${fctx.locals.length}`, { kind: "i32" });
+  const callableAnyLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_then_any_${fctx.locals.length}`, { kind: "anyref" });
+  const nextAnyLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_next_any_${fctx.locals.length}`, { kind: "anyref" });
+  const elemCapsLocal =
+    method === "all"
+      ? allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_elem_caps_${fctx.locals.length}`, {
+          kind: "ref",
+          typeIdx: ids.elemCapsTypeIdx,
+        })
+      : -1;
+
+  fctx.body.push(...inputInstrs, { op: "local.set", index: inputLocal });
+
+  const buildRejectFromError = (): Instr[] => [
+    { op: "local.set", index: errorLocal },
+    ...buildObservableRejectInstrs(rt, preparation, [{ op: "local.get", index: errorLocal }]),
+  ];
+  const buildFulfilHandler = (): Instr[] => {
+    if (method === "all") {
+      return [
+        { op: "local.get", index: stateLocal },
+        ...indexInstrs,
+        { op: "struct.new", typeIdx: ids.elemCapsTypeIdx },
+        { op: "local.set", index: elemCapsLocal },
+        ...buildObservableAllResolveClosureInstrs(ctx, observable, elemCapsLocal),
+      ];
+    }
+    return [{ op: "local.get", index: preparation.raceFulfillLocal }];
+  };
+  const buildThenArgs = (): Instr[] => [
+    { op: "call", funcIdx: ctx.funcMap.get("__objvec_new")! },
+    { op: "local.set", index: thenArgsLocal },
+    { op: "local.get", index: thenArgsLocal },
+    ...buildFulfilHandler(),
+    { op: "call", funcIdx: ctx.funcMap.get("__objvec_push")! },
+    { op: "local.get", index: thenArgsLocal },
+    { op: "local.get", index: preparation.rejectLocal },
+    { op: "call", funcIdx: ctx.funcMap.get("__objvec_push")! },
+  ];
+  const buildLegacySubscribe = (): Instr[] => [
+    { op: "local.get", index: nextLocal },
+    { op: "local.get", index: stateLocal },
+    { op: "extern.convert_any" },
+    ...indexInstrs,
+    { op: "ref.func", funcIdx: reaction.fulfillIdx },
+    { op: "ref.func", funcIdx: reaction.rejectIdx },
+    { op: "call", funcIdx: ids.subscribeFuncIdx },
+  ];
+  const buildCapturedInvoke = (): Instr[] => [
+    { op: "local.get", index: thenLocal },
+    { op: "local.get", index: nextLocal },
+    { op: "local.get", index: thenArgsLocal },
+    { op: "call", funcIdx: ctx.funcMap.get("__apply_closure")! },
+    { op: "drop" },
+  ];
+  const buildNativeInvoke = (): Instr[] => {
+    const carrierBagHasIdx = ctx.funcMap.get("__carrier_bag_has");
+    if (carrierBagHasIdx === undefined) return buildLegacySubscribe();
+    return [
+      { op: "local.get", index: nextLocal },
+      ...stringConstantExternrefInstrs(ctx, "then"),
+      { op: "call", funcIdx: carrierBagHasIdx },
+      {
+        op: "if",
+        blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+        then: [
+          buildTargetTaggedTry(
+            ctx,
+            { kind: "empty" },
+            [
+              { op: "local.get", index: nextLocal },
+              ...stringConstantExternrefInstrs(ctx, "then"),
+              { op: "call", funcIdx: ctx.funcMap.get("__extern_get")! },
+              { op: "local.set", index: thenLocal },
+              ...buildObservableCallableCheckInstrs(ctx, thenLocal, callableLocal, callableAnyLocal),
+              { op: "local.get", index: callableLocal },
+              {
+                op: "if",
+                blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+                then: buildCapturedInvoke(),
+                else: buildObservableTypeErrorRejectInstrs(ctx, rt, preparation, "Promise then is not callable"),
+              },
+            ],
+            [{ tagIdx: observable.exnTagIdx, body: buildRejectFromError() }],
+          ),
+        ],
+        else: buildLegacySubscribe(),
+      },
+    ];
+  };
+  // Ordinary values and native promises with an own `then` both use this
+  // captured getter path. The no-own-native case retains the legacy fast path.
+  const buildNonNativeInvoke = (): Instr[] => [
+    buildTargetTaggedTry(
+      ctx,
+      { kind: "empty" },
+      [
+        { op: "local.get", index: nextLocal },
+        ...stringConstantExternrefInstrs(ctx, "then"),
+        { op: "call", funcIdx: ctx.funcMap.get("__extern_get")! },
+        { op: "local.set", index: thenLocal },
+        ...buildObservableCallableCheckInstrs(ctx, thenLocal, callableLocal, callableAnyLocal),
+        { op: "local.get", index: callableLocal },
+        {
+          op: "if",
+          blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+          then: buildCapturedInvoke(),
+          else: buildObservableTypeErrorRejectInstrs(ctx, rt, preparation, "Promise then is not callable"),
+        },
+      ],
+      [{ tagIdx: observable.exnTagIdx, body: buildRejectFromError() }],
+    ),
+  ];
+  const buildAllRemainingIncrement = (): Instr[] =>
+    method === "all"
+      ? [
+          { op: "local.get", index: stateLocal },
+          { op: "local.get", index: stateLocal },
+          { op: "struct.get", typeIdx: ids.stateTypeIdx, fieldIdx: 3 },
+          { op: "i32.const", value: 1 },
+          { op: "i32.add" },
+          { op: "struct.set", typeIdx: ids.stateTypeIdx, fieldIdx: 3 },
+        ]
+      : [];
+
+  const pipeline: Instr[] = [
+    buildTargetTaggedTry(
+      ctx,
+      { kind: "empty" },
+      [
+        { op: "call", funcIdx: ctx.funcMap.get("__objvec_new")! },
+        { op: "local.set", index: resolveArgsLocal },
+        { op: "local.get", index: resolveArgsLocal },
+        { op: "local.get", index: inputLocal },
+        { op: "call", funcIdx: ctx.funcMap.get("__objvec_push")! },
+        ...buildThenArgs(),
+        { op: "local.get", index: preparation.resolveLocal },
+        { op: "local.get", index: preparation.ctorLocal },
+        { op: "local.get", index: resolveArgsLocal },
+        { op: "call", funcIdx: ctx.funcMap.get("__apply_closure")! },
+        { op: "local.set", index: nextLocal },
+      ],
+      [{ tagIdx: observable.exnTagIdx, body: buildRejectFromError() }],
+    ),
+    { op: "local.get", index: preparation.abortedLocal },
+    { op: "i32.eqz" },
+    {
+      op: "if",
+      blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+      then: [
+        ...buildAllRemainingIncrement(),
+        { op: "local.get", index: nextLocal },
+        { op: "ref.is_null" },
+        {
+          op: "if",
+          blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+          then: buildNonNativeInvoke(),
+          else: [
+            { op: "local.get", index: nextLocal },
+            { op: "any.convert_extern" },
+            { op: "local.set", index: nextAnyLocal },
+            { op: "local.get", index: nextAnyLocal },
+            { op: "ref.test", typeIdx: ids.promiseTypeIdx },
+            {
+              op: "if",
+              blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+              then: buildNativeInvoke(),
+              else: buildNonNativeInvoke(),
+            },
+          ],
+        },
+      ],
+    },
+  ];
+  fctx.body.push(
+    { op: "local.get", index: preparation.abortedLocal },
+    { op: "i32.eqz" },
+    { op: "if", blockType: { kind: "empty" }, then: pipeline },
+  );
+}
+
+/**
+ * Lower the admitted literal route. Input expressions execute completely before
+ * `Get(Promise, "resolve")`; the retained locals are then consumed in order.
+ */
+export function emitObservableStandalonePromiseCombinatorLiteral(
+  ctx: CodegenContext,
+  fctx: FunctionContext,
+  method: "all" | "race",
+  elementInstrs: Instr[][],
+): ValType {
+  const ids = ensureCombinatorFunctions(ctx);
+  const rt = ensureAsyncDriveRuntime(ctx);
+  const observable = ensureObservableCombinatorRuntime(ctx, ids);
+
+  const reaction = observableReactionFns(ids, method);
+  const inputLocals: number[] = [];
+  for (const element of elementInstrs) {
+    const inputLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_literal_input_${fctx.locals.length}`, EXTERNREF);
+    fctx.body.push(...element, { op: "local.set", index: inputLocal });
+    inputLocals.push(inputLocal);
+  }
+  const preparation = emitObservableCombinatorPreparation(ctx, fctx, ids, rt, observable, method);
+  const state = emitObservableCombinatorState(fctx, ids, preparation, method, [
+    { op: "i32.const", value: elementInstrs.length },
+  ]);
+  for (let i = 0; i < inputLocals.length; i++) {
+    emitObservableCombinatorElement(
+      ctx,
+      fctx,
+      ids,
+      rt,
+      observable,
+      preparation,
+      method,
+      reaction,
+      state.stateLocal,
+      [{ op: "i32.const", value: i }],
+      [{ op: "local.get", index: inputLocals[i]! }],
+    );
+  }
+  emitObservableAllIterationComplete(fctx, ids, rt, method, preparation, state);
+  fctx.body.push({ op: "local.get", index: preparation.resultLocal }, { op: "extern.convert_any" });
+  return EXTERNREF;
+}
+
+/**
+ * The separate route keeps its admission entrypoint; main's relocated protocol
+ * owns the identical f64 carrier classifier used by this production selector.
+ * Delegate once, without probing/emitting or broadening the observable gate.
+ */
+export function resolveF64VecArg(
+  ctx: CodegenContext,
+  argType: ValType | null,
+): { vecTypeIdx: number; arrTypeIdx: number } | null {
+  return resolveObservableF64VecArg(ctx, argType);
+}
+
+/** Lower an admitted direct vector without eager element copying. */
+export function emitObservableStandalonePromiseCombinatorRuntime(
+  ctx: CodegenContext,
+  fctx: FunctionContext,
+  method: "all" | "race",
+  argVecLocal: number,
+  argVecTypeIdx: number,
+  argArrTypeIdx: number,
+  opts?: { boxF64Elements?: boolean },
+): ValType {
+  const ids = ensureCombinatorFunctions(ctx);
+  const rt = ensureAsyncDriveRuntime(ctx);
+  const observable = ensureObservableCombinatorRuntime(ctx, ids);
+
+  const reaction = observableReactionFns(ids, method);
+  const preparation = emitObservableCombinatorPreparation(ctx, fctx, ids, rt, observable, method);
+  const boxNumberIdx = opts?.boxF64Elements === true ? ctx.funcMap.get("__box_number") : undefined;
+  if (opts?.boxF64Elements === true && boxNumberIdx === undefined) {
+    throw new Error("observable f64 Promise combinator requires __box_number");
+  }
+  const state = emitObservableCombinatorState(fctx, ids, preparation, method, [
+    { op: "local.get", index: argVecLocal },
+    { op: "ref.as_non_null" },
+    { op: "struct.get", typeIdx: argVecTypeIdx, fieldIdx: 0 },
+  ]);
+  const iLocal = allocLocal(fctx, `__comb_observable_i_${fctx.locals.length}`, {
+    kind: "i32",
+  });
+
+  fctx.body.push(
+    { op: "i32.const", value: 0 },
+    { op: "local.set", index: iLocal },
+    {
+      op: "block",
+      blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+      body: [
+        {
+          op: "loop",
+          blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+          body: [
+            { op: "local.get", index: iLocal },
+            { op: "local.get", index: state.lengthLocal },
+            { op: "i32.ge_s" },
+            { op: "br_if", depth: 1 },
+            { op: "local.get", index: preparation.abortedLocal },
+            { op: "br_if", depth: 1 },
+          ],
+        },
+      ],
+    },
+  );
+  const outerBlock = fctx.body[fctx.body.length - 1] as Extract<Instr, { op: "block" }>;
+  const loop = outerBlock.body[0] as Extract<Instr, { op: "loop" }>;
+  const loopBody = loop.body;
+  const savedBody = fctx.body;
+  fctx.body = loopBody;
+  fctx.savedBodies.push(savedBody);
+  try {
+    emitObservableCombinatorElement(
+      ctx,
+      fctx,
+      ids,
+      rt,
+      observable,
+      preparation,
+      method,
+      reaction,
+      state.stateLocal,
+      [{ op: "local.get", index: iLocal }],
+      [
+        { op: "local.get", index: argVecLocal },
+        { op: "ref.as_non_null" },
+        { op: "struct.get", typeIdx: argVecTypeIdx, fieldIdx: 1 },
+        { op: "local.get", index: iLocal },
+        { op: "array.get", typeIdx: argArrTypeIdx },
+        ...(opts?.boxF64Elements === true ? ([{ op: "call", funcIdx: boxNumberIdx! }] satisfies Instr[]) : []),
+      ],
+    );
+    loopBody.push(
+      { op: "local.get", index: iLocal },
+      { op: "i32.const", value: 1 },
+      { op: "i32.add" },
+      { op: "local.set", index: iLocal },
+      { op: "br", depth: 0 },
+    );
+  } finally {
+    fctx.savedBodies.pop();
+    fctx.body = savedBody;
+  }
+  emitObservableAllIterationComplete(fctx, ids, rt, method, preparation, state);
+  fctx.body.push({ op: "local.get", index: preparation.resultLocal }, { op: "extern.convert_any" });
+  return EXTERNREF;
+}
diff --git a/src/codegen/promise-vector-iterator.ts b/src/codegen/promise-vector-iterator.ts

--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/codegen/promise-vector-iterator.ts

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+/** Private GetIterator/IteratorStepValue/IteratorClose(throw) for the opt-in vector drive. */
+import type { Instr, ValType } from "../ir/types.js";
+import { buildTargetTaggedTry } from "../ir/try-table.js";
+import type { CodegenContext, FunctionContext } from "./context/types.js";
+import { definedFuncAt, mintDefinedFunc, pushDefinedFunc } from "./func-space.js";
+import { addFuncType } from "./registry/types.js";
+import { addStringConstantGlobals, ensureExnTag } from "./registry/imports.js";
+import { ensureObjectRuntime, ensureObjVecBuilders, reserveApplyClosure } from "./object-runtime.js";
+import { externIsObjectInstrs } from "./iterator-native.js";
+import { ensureIteratorProtocolGetRuntime } from "./iterator-protocol-get.js";
+import { stringConstantExternrefInstrs } from "./native-strings.js";
+import { canonicalUndefinedExternInstrs } from "./any-helpers.js";
+import { buildThrowJsErrorInstrs } from "./js-errors.js";
+import { emitToBoolean } from "./coercion-engine.js";
+import { ensureLateImport, flushLateImportShifts } from "./shared.js";
+
+const ER: ValType = { kind: "externref" };
+const I32: ValType = { kind: "i32" };
+const RECORD = "__PromiseVectorIteratorRecord";
+const ACQUIRE = "__promise_vector_iterator_acquire";
+const STEP = "__promise_vector_iterator_step";
+const CLOSE = "__promise_vector_iterator_close_throw";
+const ERROR = "Invalid iterator protocol";
+type Phase = "registering" | "reserved" | "emitted" | "failed";
+const states = new WeakMap<CodegenContext, { phase: Phase; error?: unknown }>();
+const get = (index: number): Instr => ({ op: "local.get", index });
+const set = (index: number): Instr => ({ op: "local.set", index });
+const int = (value: number): Instr => ({ op: "i32.const", value });
+const when = (then: Instr[]): Instr => ({ op: "if", blockType: { kind: "empty" }, then, else: [] });
+function call(ctx: CodegenContext, name: string): Instr {
+  const funcIdx = ctx.funcMap.get(name);
+  if (funcIdx === undefined) throw new Error(`Promise vector iterator requires ${name}`);
+  return { op: "call", funcIdx };
+}
+
+/**
+ * Register while source/type population is open, before constructing caller IR.
+ * B owns semantic Get and its late shape arms; this module never rebuilds bodies
+ * at finalization, dispatches an IterRec, or substitutes an intrinsic method.
+ */
+export function ensurePromiseVectorIteratorRuntime(ctx: CodegenContext, fctx: FunctionContext): void {
+  const previous = states.get(ctx);
+  if (previous) {
+    if (previous.phase === "failed") throw previous.error;
+    // A dependency cycle before reservation cannot safely expose missing handles.
+    // Producer/Get dependencies must not depend on Promise acquisition.
+    if (previous.phase === "registering") {
+      throw new Error("Promise vector iterator dependency reentered before helper reservation");
+    }
+    return;
+  }
+  const state: { phase: Phase; error?: unknown } = { phase: "registering" };
+  states.set(ctx, state);
+  try {
+    ensureObjectRuntime(ctx);
+    ensureObjVecBuilders(ctx);
+    reserveApplyClosure(ctx);
+    ensureIteratorProtocolGetRuntime(ctx, fctx);
+    addStringConstantGlobals(ctx, ["next", "done", "value", "return", ERROR]);
+    for (const name of [
+      "__typeof_object",
+      "__typeof_function",
+      "__is_callable",
+      "__is_truthy",
+      "__extern_is_undefined",
+    ]) {
+      ensureLateImport(ctx, name, [ER], [I32]);
+    }
+    // Warm allocating providers BEFORE retaining any detached instructions.
+    emitToBoolean(ctx, ER, []);
+    canonicalUndefinedExternInstrs(ctx);
+    buildThrowJsErrorInstrs(ctx, "TypeError", ERROR, { flush: fctx });
+    ensureExnTag(ctx);
+    for (const key of ["next", "done", "value", "return"]) stringConstantExternrefInstrs(ctx, key);
+    flushLateImportShifts(ctx, fctx);
+    for (const name of [
+      "__iterator_protocol_get",
+      "__apply_closure",
+      "__objvec_new",
+      "__box_symbol",
+      "__typeof_object",
+      "__typeof_function",
+      "__is_callable",
+      "__is_truthy",
+      "__extern_is_undefined",
+      "__new_TypeError",
+    ])
+      call(ctx, name);
+
+    const recordType = ctx.mod.types.length;
+    ctx.mod.types.push({
+      kind: "struct",
+      name: RECORD,
+      fields: [
+        { name: "iterator", type: ER, mutable: false },
+        { name: "nextMethod", type: ER, mutable: false },
+        { name: "done", type: I32, mutable: true },
+      ],
+    });
+    // Deliberately absent from generic JS property/carrier field registries.
+    ctx.structMap.set(RECORD, recordType);
+    const rec: ValType = { kind: "ref", typeIdx: recordType };
+    const reserve = (name: string, params: ValType[], results: ValType[], locals: ValType[]) => {
+      const typeIdx = addFuncType(ctx, params, results);
+      const handle = mintDefinedFunc(ctx);
+      ctx.funcMap.set(name, handle);
+      pushDefinedFunc(ctx, handle, {
+        name,
+        typeIdx,
+        exported: false,
+        locals: locals.map((type, i) => ({ name: `scratch${i}`, type })),
+        body: [{ op: "unreachable" }],
+      });
+    };
+    reserve(ACQUIRE, [ER], [ER], [ER, ER, ER]);
+    reserve(STEP, [ER], [I32, ER], [rec, ER, ER, ER, ER]);
+    reserve(CLOSE, [ER, ER], [ER], [rec, ER]);
+    state.phase = "reserved";
+
+    // All factories below are read-only and create fresh instruction trees.
+    const tagIdx = ensureExnTag(ctx);
+    const fail = (): Instr[] => [
+      ...stringConstantExternrefInstrs(ctx, ERROR),
+      call(ctx, "__new_TypeError"),
+      { op: "throw", tagIdx },
+    ];
+    const callable = (local: number): Instr[] => [
+      get(local),
+      call(ctx, "__is_callable"),
+      { op: "i32.eqz" },
+      when(fail()),
+    ];
+    const object = (local: number): Instr[] => {
+      const predicate = externIsObjectInstrs(ctx, local);
+      if (!predicate) throw new Error("Promise vector iterator object predicate unavailable");
+      return [...predicate, { op: "i32.eqz" }, when(fail())];
+    };
+    const property = (local: number, key: string): Instr[] => [
+      get(local),
+      ...stringConstantExternrefInstrs(ctx, key),
+      call(ctx, "__iterator_protocol_get"),
+    ];
+    const invoke = (method: number, receiver: number): Instr[] => [
+      ...callable(method),
+      get(method),
+      get(receiver),
+      call(ctx, "__objvec_new"),
+      call(ctx, "__apply_closure"),
+    ];
+    const cast = (local: number): Instr[] => [
+      get(0),
+      { op: "any.convert_extern" },
+      { op: "ref.cast", typeIdx: recordType },
+      set(local),
+    ];
+    const field = (local: number, fieldIdx: number): Instr[] => [
+      get(local),
+      { op: "struct.get", typeIdx: recordType, fieldIdx },
+    ];
+    const markDone = (local: number): Instr[] => [
+      get(local),
+      int(1),
+      { op: "struct.set", typeIdx: recordType, fieldIdx: 2 },
+    ];
+    const terminal = (): Instr[] => [int(1), ...canonicalUndefinedExternInstrs(ctx), { op: "return" }];
+    const emit = (name: string, body: Instr[]) => {
+      const fn = definedFuncAt(ctx, ctx.funcMap.get(name)!);
+      if (!fn) throw new Error(`Missing reserved Promise vector iterator helper ${name}`);
+      fn.body = body;
+    };
+
+    emit(ACQUIRE, [
+      // Well-known symbol id 1 is Symbol.iterator, never the string alias.
+      get(0),
+      int(1),
+      call(ctx, "__box_symbol"),
+      call(ctx, "__iterator_protocol_get"),
+      set(1),
+      ...invoke(1, 0),
+      set(2),
+      ...object(2),
+      ...property(2, "next"),
+      set(3),
+      // GetIterator captures even a noncallable next; IteratorNext checks it.
+      get(2),
+      get(3),
+      int(0),
+      { op: "struct.new", typeIdx: recordType },
+      { op: "extern.convert_any" },
+    ]);
+    emit(STEP, [
+      ...cast(1),
+      ...field(1, 2),
+      when(terminal()),
+      buildTargetTaggedTry(
+        ctx,
+        { kind: "empty" },
+        [
+          ...field(1, 0),
+          set(2),
+          ...field(1, 1),
+          set(3),
+          ...invoke(3, 2),
+          set(4),
+          ...object(4),
+          ...property(4, "done"),
+          call(ctx, "__is_truthy"),
+          when([...markDone(1), ...terminal()]),
+          int(0),
+          ...property(4, "value"),
+          { op: "return" },
+        ],
+        [{ tagIdx, body: [set(5), ...markDone(1), get(5), { op: "throw", tagIdx }] }],
+      ),
+      { op: "unreachable" },
+    ]);
+    emit(CLOSE, [
+      ...cast(2),
+      ...field(2, 2),
+      when([get(1), { op: "return" }]),
+      ...markDone(2),
+      // Param 1 retains the original completion across nested calls. Only the
+      // JS tag is caught; a trap/compiler defect cannot become successful close.
+      buildTargetTaggedTry(
+        ctx,
+        { kind: "empty" },
+        [
+          ...field(2, 0),
+          ...stringConstantExternrefInstrs(ctx, "return"),
+          call(ctx, "__iterator_protocol_get"),
+          set(3),
+          get(3),
+          { op: "ref.is_null" },
+          get(3),
+          call(ctx, "__extern_is_undefined"),
+          { op: "i32.or" },
+          when([get(1), { op: "return" }]),
+          ...callable(3),
+          get(3),
+          ...field(2, 0),
+          call(ctx, "__objvec_new"),
+          call(ctx, "__apply_closure"),
+          // Throw completion wins before validating the return result.
+          { op: "drop" },
+        ],
+        [{ tagIdx, body: [{ op: "drop" }] }],
+      ),
+      get(1),
+    ]);
+    state.phase = "emitted";
+  } catch (error) {
+    // Never reuse a partially registered graph as though emission succeeded.
+    state.phase = "failed";
+    state.error = error;
+    throw error;
+  }
+}

diff --git a/src/codegen/reference-storage-operand.ts b/src/codegen/reference-storage-operand.ts

index 0000000000..52876d0fcd
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/codegen/reference-storage-operand.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,25 @@
+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import type { ValType } from "../ir/types.js";
+import type { ts } from "../ts-api.js";
+import { prepareClosedFieldValue } from "./bigint-field-carrier.js";
+import type { CodegenContext, FunctionContext } from "./context/types.js";
+import { compileExpression } from "./shared.js";
+
+/** Preserve a proven BigInt before emitting an already-selected reference boundary. */
+export function compileReferenceStorageOperand(
+  ctx: CodegenContext,
+  fctx: FunctionContext,
+  value: ts.Expression,
+  expectedType?: ValType,
+): ValType | null {
+  const nativeBigInt =
+    ctx.standalone &&
+    ctx.nativeStrings &&
+    (expectedType === undefined || expectedType.kind === "externref" || expectedType.kind === "anyref") &&
+    ctx.oracle.staticJsTypeOf(value) === "bigint";
+  const hint = expectedType ?? (nativeBigInt ? { kind: "externref" as const } : undefined);
+  if (nativeBigInt) prepareClosedFieldValue(ctx, fctx, value, hint!);
+  // Other values keep their original hint. This neither changes a scalar ABI
+  // nor reconstructs limbs already lost by the operand's producer.
+  return compileExpression(ctx, fctx, value, hint);
+}
diff --git a/src/codegen/reference-wrapper-brand.ts b/src/codegen/reference-wrapper-brand.ts

+++ b/src/codegen/reference-wrapper-brand.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,34 @@
+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import type { Instr } from "../ir/types.js";
+import { builtinBrandOffsetOf } from "./builtin-brands.js";
+import type { CodegenContext } from "./context/types.js";
+
+/**
+ * Classify an existing [[PrimitiveValue]] slot without registering providers.
+ * Symbol wrappers select Symbol.prototype, including its symbol-keyed entries.
+ * BigIntWide is a subtype of the base BigInt carrier, so testing that base
+ * preserves all limbs without introducing a separate wide-carrier dependency.
+ * Missing carriers emit nothing; this finalizer never mints types or helpers.
+ */
+export function buildReferenceWrapperBrandArms(
+  ctx: Pick<CodegenContext, "symbolTypeIdx" | "nativeBigIntTypeIdx">,
+  slotValue: () => Instr[],
+): Instr[] {
+  const result: Instr[] = [];
+  for (const [typeIdx, brand] of [
+    [ctx.symbolTypeIdx, "Symbol"],
+    [ctx.nativeBigIntTypeIdx, "BigInt"],
+  ] as const) {
+    if (typeIdx < 0) continue;
+    result.push(
+      ...slotValue(),
+      { op: "ref.test", typeIdx },
+      {
+        op: "if",
+        blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+        then: [{ op: "i32.const", value: builtinBrandOffsetOf(brand)! }, { op: "return" }],
+      },
+    );
+  }
+  return result;
+}
diff --git a/src/codegen/staging-census.ts b/src/codegen/staging-census.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..9adad47d9c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/codegen/staging-census.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,181 @@
+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import type {
+  BodyEpoch,
+  EmissionOccurrence,
+  EmissionRegion,
+  OwnershipEdge,
+  RetainedAdoption,
+  RetainedBodyManifest,
+  RetainedOccurrence,
+} from "./context/emission-ownership.js";
+import {
+  isExecutableStageLeaf,
+  materializedStageChildren,
+  type StageBody,
+  type StageChildSlot,
+  type StageNode,
+  type StageRegion,
+} from "./staging-instr.js";
+
+export type StageParent =
+  | { readonly kind: "body"; readonly body: BodyEpoch }
+  | { readonly kind: "node"; readonly nodeIdentity: object; readonly slot: StageChildSlot };
+export interface StagePlacement {
+  readonly region: EmissionRegion;
+  readonly parent: StageParent;
+  /** Exact physical sibling order, which the ledger's region set cannot prove. */
+  readonly nodeIdentities: readonly object[];
+}
+export interface StageVisit {
+  readonly node: StageNode;
+  readonly region: EmissionRegion;
+  readonly index: number;
+  readonly labelDepth: number;
+}
+export interface StageCensus {
+  readonly manifest: RetainedBodyManifest;
+  readonly layout: readonly StagePlacement[];
+  /** Preorder; siblings, then/else, try body/catches/catchAll stay lexical. */
+  readonly visits: readonly StageVisit[];
+}
+
+/**
+ * Independent census of actual retained materialized nodes, NOT ledger edges.
+ * No EmissionOwnership instance is accepted: the caller subsequently supplies
+ * manifest to completeBodyEpoch / sealWithManifest, never raw seal fallback.
+ *
+ * Reject physical aliases/cycles, repeated handles, wrong-body regions and
+ * repeated node/witness/use-site identities. This is a tree check, not a
+ * deduplicating relocation walk. Detached regions not in this root are absent.
+ * It does not authenticate source root selection, binding equivalence, payload
+ * semantics, pending recipes, or structural equality to an unprovided tree.
+ */
+export function inspectStageBody(body: StageBody): StageCensus {
+  const regions: EmissionRegion[] = [];
+  const occurrences: RetainedOccurrence[] = [];
+  const adoptions: RetainedAdoption[] = [];
+  const layout: StagePlacement[] = [];
+  const visits: StageVisit[] = [];
+  const physicalRegions = new Set<StageRegion>();
+  const regionHandles = new Set<EmissionRegion>();
+  const arrays = new Set<readonly StageNode[]>();
+  const physicalNodes = new Set<StageNode>();
+  const nodeIdentities = new Set<object>();
+  const instructions = new Set<object>();
+  const occurrenceHandles = new Set<EmissionOccurrence>();
+  const adoptionEdges = new Set<OwnershipEdge>();
+  const useSites = new Set<object>();
+  type Work =
+    | { kind: "region"; region: StageRegion; parent: StageParent; depth: number }
+    | { kind: "node"; node: StageNode; region: StageRegion; index: number; depth: number };
+  const work: Work[] = [{ kind: "region", region: body.root, parent: { kind: "body", body: body.body }, depth: 0 }];
+  while (work.length) {
+    const next = work.pop()!;
+    const region = next.region;
+    if (next.kind === "region") {
+      if (region.body !== body.body) throw new Error("Stage region belongs to a different body epoch");
+      if (physicalRegions.has(region)) throw new Error("Stage region cycle or multiple physical parents");
+      if (regionHandles.has(region.region)) throw new Error("Stage region handle has multiple physical versions");
+      if (arrays.has(region.nodes)) throw new Error("Stage node array has multiple physical parents");
+      physicalRegions.add(region);
+      regionHandles.add(region.region);
+      arrays.add(region.nodes);
+      regions.push(region.region);
+      layout.push(
+        Object.freeze({
+          region: region.region,
+          parent: Object.freeze(next.parent),
+          nodeIdentities: Object.freeze(region.nodes.map((node) => node.nodeIdentity)),
+        }),
+      );
+      for (let index = region.nodes.length - 1; index >= 0; index--) {
+        work.push({ kind: "node", node: region.nodes[index]!, region, index, depth: next.depth });
+      }
+      continue;
+    }
+    const node = next.node;
+    if (physicalNodes.has(node) || nodeIdentities.has(node.nodeIdentity)) {
+      throw new Error("Stage node identity has multiple physical positions");
+    }
+    if (instructions.has(node.instruction)) throw new Error("Concrete instruction has multiple physical positions");
+    if (node.kind === "leaf" && !isExecutableStageLeaf(node.instruction)) {
+      throw new Error("Recursive instruction hidden in a staged leaf");
+    }
+    physicalNodes.add(node);
+    nodeIdentities.add(node.nodeIdentity);
+    instructions.add(node.instruction);
+    visits.push(Object.freeze({ node, region: region.region, index: next.index, labelDepth: next.depth }));
+    if (node.occurrence) {
+      if (occurrenceHandles.has(node.occurrence.occurrence)) throw new Error("Occurrence has multiple physical nodes");
+      occurrenceHandles.add(node.occurrence.occurrence);
+      occurrences.push(
+        Object.freeze({
+          ...node.occurrence,
+          owner: region.region,
+          nodeIdentity: node.nodeIdentity,
+        }),
+      );
+    }
+    for (const adoption of node.adoptions ?? []) {
+      if (adoptionEdges.has(adoption.edge) || useSites.has(adoption.useSiteIdentity)) {
+        throw new Error("Adoption edge or use site has multiple physical positions");
+      }
+      adoptionEdges.add(adoption.edge);
+      useSites.add(adoption.useSiteIdentity);
+      adoptions.push(Object.freeze({ ...adoption, owner: region.region }));
+    }
+    const children = materializedStageChildren(node);
+    for (let i = children.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
+      const child = children[i]!;
+      work.push({
+        kind: "region",
+        region: child.region,
+        parent: { kind: "node", nodeIdentity: node.nodeIdentity, slot: Object.freeze({ ...child.slot }) },
+        depth: next.depth + child.labelDepthDelta,
+      });
+    }
+  }
+  return Object.freeze({
+    manifest: Object.freeze({
+      body: body.body,
+      bodyIdentity: body.bodyIdentity,
+      bindingEnvironment: body.bindingEnvironment,
+      regions: Object.freeze(regions),
+      occurrences: Object.freeze(occurrences),
+      adoptions: Object.freeze(adoptions),
+    }),
+    layout: Object.freeze(layout),
+    visits: Object.freeze(visits),
+  });
+}
+
+function sameSlot(a: StageChildSlot, b: StageChildSlot): boolean {
+  return a.kind === b.kind && (a.kind !== "catch" || (b.kind === "catch" && a.index === b.index));
+}
+function sameParent(a: StageParent, b: StageParent): boolean {
+  if (a.kind === "body") return b.kind === "body" && a.body === b.body;
+  return b.kind === "node" && a.nodeIdentity === b.nodeIdentity && sameSlot(a.slot, b.slot);
+}
+
+/**
+ * Check an independently retained/expected physical layout, including sibling
+ * order and parent slot. Passing a layout freshly echoed from the same tree is
+ * not independent evidence. Intentional edits require an explicit new layout.
+ * Does not claim the ledger checked these invariants or infer source semantics.
+ */
+export function assertStageLayout(actual: StageCensus, expected: readonly StagePlacement[]): void {
+  if (actual.layout.length !== expected.length) throw new Error("Stage layout region count mismatch");
+  for (let i = 0; i < actual.layout.length; i++) {
+    const a = actual.layout[i]!;
+    const b = expected[i]!;
+    if (a.region !== b.region || !sameParent(a.parent, b.parent)) {
+      throw new Error("Stage layout parent or region order mismatch");
+    }
+    if (
+      a.nodeIdentities.length !== b.nodeIdentities.length ||
+      a.nodeIdentities.some((node, index) => node !== b.nodeIdentities[index])
+    ) {
+      throw new Error("Stage layout node order mismatch");
+    }
+  }
+}
diff --git a/src/codegen/staging-edit.ts b/src/codegen/staging-edit.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..e89ce5fc1d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/codegen/staging-edit.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,265 @@
+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+/**
+ * First isolated editor slice: materialized leaf removal and sequence reorder.
+ * Uses the existing arena/tokens; owns physical snapshots, not another ledger.
+ * No pending recipe, operand edit, provider readiness, lowering or caller wiring.
+ */
+import type {
+  CompletedBody,
+  EmissionOwnership,
+  EmissionRegion,
+  EmissionTransaction,
+} from "./context/emission-ownership.js";
+import { assertStageLayout, inspectStageBody, type StagePlacement } from "./staging-census.js";
+import {
+  materializedStageChildren,
+  stageContainer,
+  stageRegion,
+  type StageBody,
+  type StageChildSlot,
+  type StageContainerNode,
+  type StageNode,
+  type StageRegion,
+} from "./staging-instr.js";
+
+interface PhysicalVersion {
+  readonly body: StageBody;
+  readonly expected: readonly StagePlacement[];
+}
+interface EditFrame {
+  readonly token: EmissionTransaction;
+  readonly before: PhysicalVersion;
+}
+
+function copyLayout(layout: readonly StagePlacement[]): readonly StagePlacement[] {
+  return Object.freeze(
+    layout.map((placement) =>
+      Object.freeze({
+        region: placement.region,
+        parent:
+          placement.parent.kind === "body"
+            ? Object.freeze({ ...placement.parent })
+            : Object.freeze({ ...placement.parent, slot: Object.freeze({ ...placement.parent.slot }) }),
+        nodeIdentities: Object.freeze([...placement.nodeIdentities]),
+      }),
+    ),
+  );
+}
+
+/** Reorder only the independent expected layout, not the observed physical tree. */
+function changeExpectedSequence(
+  expected: readonly StagePlacement[],
+  region: EmissionRegion,
+  order: readonly object[],
+): readonly StagePlacement[] {
+  const updated = expected.map((p) => (p.region === region ? { ...p, nodeIdentities: order } : p));
+  const children = new Map<object, StagePlacement[]>();
+  const roots: StagePlacement[] = [];
+  for (const p of updated) {
+    if (p.parent.kind === "body") roots.push(p);
+    else {
+      const entries = children.get(p.parent.nodeIdentity) ?? [];
+      entries.push(p); // Existing child-slot order is unchanged by these edits.
+      children.set(p.parent.nodeIdentity, entries);
+    }
+  }
+  const ordered: StagePlacement[] = [];
+  const stack = [...roots].reverse();
+  while (stack.length) {
+    const p = stack.pop()!;
+    ordered.push(p);
+    for (let i = p.nodeIdentities.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
+      const descendants = children.get(p.nodeIdentities[i]!) ?? [];
+      for (let j = descendants.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) stack.push(descendants[j]!);
+    }
+  }
+  if (ordered.length !== updated.length) throw new Error("Edit would lose a materialized child region");
+  return copyLayout(ordered);
+}
+
+function replaceChild(node: StageContainerNode, slot: StageChildSlot, child: StageRegion): StageContainerNode {
+  const instruction = node.instruction;
+  switch (instruction.op) {
+    case "block":
+    case "loop":
+    case "try_table":
+      if (slot.kind === "body") return stageContainer({ ...instruction, body: child }, node);
+      break;
+    case "if":
+      if (slot.kind === "then") return stageContainer({ ...instruction, then: child }, node);
+      if (slot.kind === "else" && instruction.else) return stageContainer({ ...instruction, else: child }, node);
+      break;
+    case "try":
+      if (slot.kind === "body") return stageContainer({ ...instruction, body: child }, node);
+      if (slot.kind === "catchAll" && instruction.catchAll) {
+        return stageContainer({ ...instruction, catchAll: child }, node);
+      }
+      if (slot.kind === "catch" && instruction.catches[slot.index]) {
+        return stageContainer(
+          {
+            ...instruction,
+            catches: instruction.catches.map((c, i) => (i === slot.index ? { ...c, body: child } : c)),
+          },
+          node,
+        );
+      }
+  }
+  throw new Error("Stale materialized child slot");
+}
+
+/** Iterative path copying; no mutation of frozen containers or old snapshots. */
+function replaceSequence(body: StageBody, target: EmissionRegion, nodes: readonly StageNode[]): StageBody {
+  const census = inspectStageBody(body);
+  const regions = new Map<EmissionRegion, StageRegion>([[body.root.region, body.root]]);
+  const placements = new Map(census.layout.map((p) => [p.region, p]));
+  const visits = new Map(census.visits.map((v) => [v.node.nodeIdentity, v]));
+  for (const visit of census.visits) {
+    for (const child of materializedStageChildren(visit.node)) regions.set(child.region.region, child.region);
+  }
+  const original = regions.get(target);
+  if (!original) throw new Error("Unknown physical region");
+  let replacement = stageRegion(body.body, target, nodes);
+  for (;;) {
+    const placement = placements.get(replacement.region)!;
+    if (placement.parent.kind === "body") return Object.freeze({ ...body, root: replacement });
+    const parent = visits.get(placement.parent.nodeIdentity)!;
+    if (parent.node.kind !== "container") throw new Error("Physical parent is not a container");
+    const container = replaceChild(parent.node, placement.parent.slot, replacement);
+    const parentRegion = regions.get(parent.region)!;
+    replacement = stageRegion(
+      body.body,
+      parent.region,
+      parentRegion.nodes.map((node, index) => (index === parent.index ? container : node)),
+    );
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Transfer exclusive close authority for an OPEN construction token to this
+ * editor. The adapter must route all subsequent body edits/token closes here.
+ * Current ledger API cannot enforce that discipline against external callers.
+ * Expected layout must be supplied independently, not echoed from this class.
+ */
+export class StagingBodyEditor {
+  private current: PhysicalVersion;
+  private readonly frames: EditFrame[];
+  private closed = false;
+
+  constructor(
+    private readonly ownership: EmissionOwnership,
+    body: StageBody,
+    constructionToken: EmissionTransaction,
+    expected: readonly StagePlacement[],
+  ) {
+    const copy = copyLayout(expected);
+    assertStageLayout(inspectStageBody(body), copy);
+    this.current = Object.freeze({ body: Object.freeze({ ...body }), expected: copy });
+    this.frames = [{ token: constructionToken, before: this.current }];
+  }
+
+  snapshot(): StageBody {
+    return this.current.body;
+  }
+  expectedLayout(): readonly StagePlacement[] {
+    return this.current.expected;
+  }
+
+  private editable(): void {
+    if (this.closed) throw new Error("Staging editor is closed");
+    assertStageLayout(inspectStageBody(this.current.body), this.current.expected);
+  }
+  private top(token: EmissionTransaction): EditFrame {
+    this.editable();
+    const top = this.frames[this.frames.length - 1];
+    if (!top || top.token !== token) throw new Error("Editor scopes must close in LIFO order");
+    return top;
+  }
+  beginEdit(): EmissionTransaction {
+    this.editable();
+    const token = this.ownership.beginContentTransaction(this.current.body.body);
+    this.frames.push({ token, before: this.current });
+    return token;
+  }
+  commitEdit(token: EmissionTransaction): void {
+    this.top(token);
+    if (this.frames.length === 1) throw new Error("Use complete for the construction scope");
+    this.ownership.commitTransaction(token);
+    this.frames.pop(); // Parent's saved physical version still owns rollback.
+  }
+  rollbackEdit(token: EmissionTransaction): void {
+    const frame = this.top(token);
+    this.ownership.rollbackTransaction(token);
+    this.current = frame.before;
+    this.frames.pop();
+    if (this.frames.length === 0) this.closed = true;
+  }
+
+  /** No generic rewrite callback: exact removal of one existing concrete leaf. */
+  removeLeaf(nodeIdentity: object): void {
+    this.editable();
+    const census = inspectStageBody(this.current.body);
+    const location = census.visits.find((visit) => visit.node.nodeIdentity === nodeIdentity);
+    if (!location) throw new Error("Unknown physical node");
+    if (location.node.kind !== "leaf") throw new Error("Subtree removal requires an attachment-receipt contract");
+    const nodes = census.visits
+      .filter((visit) => visit.region === location.region)
+      .map((visit) => visit.node)
+      .filter((node) => node.nodeIdentity !== nodeIdentity);
+    const next = this.prepareSequence(location.region, nodes);
+    const token = this.ownership.beginContentTransaction(this.current.body.body);
+    try {
+      if (location.node.occurrence) this.ownership.removeOccurrence(location.node.occurrence.occurrence);
+      for (const adoption of location.node.adoptions ?? []) this.ownership.detachEdge(adoption.edge);
+    } catch (error) {
+      this.ownership.rollbackTransaction(token);
+      throw error;
+    }
+    // Both roots and journal data are prepared before closing the local scope.
+    // No source callbacks or fallible preparation between commit and install.
+    this.ownership.commitTransaction(token);
+    this.current = next;
+  }
+
+  /** Bijection only: cannot insert, remove or reparent a node under this name. */
+  reorderSequence(region: EmissionRegion, order: readonly object[]): void {
+    this.editable();
+    const census = inspectStageBody(this.current.body);
+    if (!census.layout.some((p) => p.region === region)) throw new Error("Unknown physical region");
+    const old = census.visits.filter((visit) => visit.region === region).map((visit) => visit.node);
+    const byIdentity = new Map(old.map((node) => [node.nodeIdentity, node]));
+    if (order.length !== old.length || new Set(order).size !== old.length || order.some((id) => !byIdentity.has(id))) {
+      throw new Error("Reorder requires an exact identity permutation");
+    }
+    const next = this.prepareSequence(
+      region,
+      order.map((id) => byIdentity.get(id)!),
+    );
+    const token = this.ownership.beginContentTransaction(this.current.body.body);
+    this.ownership.commitTransaction(token); // Membership unchanged; paired physical scope remains rollbackable.
+    this.current = next;
+  }
+
+  private prepareSequence(region: EmissionRegion, nodes: readonly StageNode[]): PhysicalVersion {
+    const expected = changeExpectedSequence(
+      this.current.expected,
+      region,
+      nodes.map((node) => node.nodeIdentity),
+    );
+    const body = replaceSequence(this.current.body, region, nodes);
+    assertStageLayout(inspectStageBody(body), expected);
+    return Object.freeze({ body, expected });
+  }
+
+  complete(): Readonly<{ body: StageBody; receipt: CompletedBody }> {
+    this.editable();
+    if (this.frames.length !== 1) throw new Error("Close nested editor scopes before completion");
+    const census = inspectStageBody(this.current.body);
+    assertStageLayout(census, this.current.expected);
+    this.ownership.commitTransaction(this.frames[0]!.token);
+    this.frames.pop();
+    this.closed = true;
+    // A mismatch is an integration defect. No raw seal, retry, or receipt stub.
+    const receipt = this.ownership.completeBodyEpoch(this.current.body.body, census.manifest);
+    return Object.freeze({ body: this.current.body, receipt });
+  }
+}

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+/**
+ * Isolated materialized staging tree. No new ownership arena or executable op.
+ * All body/region/occurrence/edge handles come from EmissionOwnership.
+ *
+ * Pending recipes are deliberately NOT represented yet: a node annotation is
+ * evidence about this physical node, never an executable placeholder, lowering
+ * recipe, or permission to activate. This module has no executable projection.
+ */
+import type { CatchClause, Instr, TryTableCatch } from "../wasm/model/instructions.js";
+import type {
+  BodyEpoch,
+  BodyIdentity,
+  EmissionOccurrence,
+  EmissionRegion,
+  OwnershipEdge,
+  PendingNodeBinding,
+} from "./context/emission-ownership.js";
+
+export type StageContainerOp = "block" | "loop" | "if" | "try" | "try_table";
+export type ExecutableStageLeaf = Exclude<Instr, { op: StageContainerOp }>;
+
+// Explicit replacements, including nested catches; never a shallow Omit of
+// the full Instr union. try_table catch entries are labels, not child bodies.
+export type StageContainerInstruction =
+  | (Readonly<Omit<Extract<Instr, { op: "block" }>, "body">> & { readonly body: StageRegion })
+  | (Readonly<Omit<Extract<Instr, { op: "loop" }>, "body">> & { readonly body: StageRegion })
+  | (Readonly<Omit<Extract<Instr, { op: "if" }>, "then" | "else">> & {
+      readonly then: StageRegion;
+      readonly else?: StageRegion;
+    })
+  | (Readonly<Omit<Extract<Instr, { op: "try" }>, "body" | "catches" | "catchAll">> & {
+      readonly body: StageRegion;
+      readonly catches: readonly StageCatch[];
+      readonly catchAll?: StageRegion;
+    })
+  | (Readonly<Omit<Extract<Instr, { op: "try_table" }>, "body" | "catches">> & {
+      readonly body: StageRegion;
+      readonly catches: readonly Readonly<TryTableCatch>[];
+    });
+
+export interface StageCatch extends Readonly<Omit<CatchClause, "body">> {
+  readonly body: StageRegion;
+}
+export interface StageOccurrence {
+  readonly occurrence: EmissionOccurrence;
+  readonly kind: PendingNodeBinding["kind"];
+}
+export interface StageAdoption {
+  readonly edge: OwnershipEdge;
+  readonly body: BodyEpoch;
+  readonly useSiteIdentity: object;
+}
+export interface StageNodeIdentity {
+  /** Exact staged-node version identity, not an AST or instruction index. */
+  readonly nodeIdentity: object;
+  readonly occurrence?: StageOccurrence;
+  readonly adoptions?: readonly StageAdoption[];
+}
+export type StageLeafNode = StageNodeIdentity & {
+  readonly kind: "leaf";
+  readonly instruction: ExecutableStageLeaf;
+};
+export type StageContainerNode = StageNodeIdentity & {
+  readonly kind: "container";
+  readonly instruction: StageContainerInstruction;
+};
+export type StageNode = StageLeafNode | StageContainerNode;
+
+export interface StageRegion {
+  readonly kind: "stage-region";
+  readonly body: BodyEpoch;
+  readonly region: EmissionRegion;
+  readonly nodes: readonly StageNode[];
+}
+export interface StageBody extends BodyIdentity {
+  readonly body: BodyEpoch;
+  readonly root: StageRegion;
+}
+
+export function isExecutableStageLeaf(instruction: Instr): instruction is ExecutableStageLeaf {
+  switch (instruction.op) {
+    case "block":
+    case "loop":
+    case "if":
+    case "try":
+    case "try_table":
+      return false;
+    default:
+      return true;
+  }
+}
+
+function identityFields(identity: StageNodeIdentity): StageNodeIdentity {
+  return {
+    nodeIdentity: identity.nodeIdentity,
+    occurrence: identity.occurrence && Object.freeze({ ...identity.occurrence }),
+    adoptions: identity.adoptions && Object.freeze(identity.adoptions.map((a) => Object.freeze({ ...a }))),
+  };
+}
+
+/** Own structural collections; do not clone concrete instruction identities. */
+export function stageLeaf(instruction: ExecutableStageLeaf, identity: StageNodeIdentity): StageLeafNode {
+  if (!isExecutableStageLeaf(instruction)) throw new Error("Recursive instruction requires staged children");
+  return Object.freeze({ kind: "leaf", instruction, ...identityFields(identity) });
+}
+
+export function stageContainer(
+  instruction: StageContainerInstruction,
+  identity: StageNodeIdentity,
+): StageContainerNode {
+  const fields = identityFields(identity);
+  switch (instruction.op) {
+    case "block":
+    case "loop":
+    case "if":
+      return Object.freeze({ kind: "container", instruction: Object.freeze({ ...instruction }), ...fields });
+    case "try":
+      return Object.freeze({
+        kind: "container",
+        instruction: Object.freeze({
+          ...instruction,
+          catches: Object.freeze(instruction.catches.map((c) => Object.freeze({ ...c }))),
+        }),
+        ...fields,
+      });
+    case "try_table":
+      return Object.freeze({
+        kind: "container",
+        instruction: Object.freeze({
+          ...instruction,
+          catches: Object.freeze(instruction.catches.map((c) => Object.freeze({ ...c }))),
+        }),
+        ...fields,
+      });
+  }
+}
+
+/** Caller supplies the existing ledger handle; no second id/epoch allocator. */
+export function stageRegion(body: BodyEpoch, region: EmissionRegion, nodes: readonly StageNode[]): StageRegion {
+  return Object.freeze({ kind: "stage-region", body, region, nodes: Object.freeze([...nodes]) });
+}
+
+export type StageChildSlot =
+  | { readonly kind: "body" | "then" | "else" | "catchAll" }
+  | { readonly kind: "catch"; readonly index: number };
+export interface StageChild {
+  readonly slot: StageChildSlot;
+  readonly region: StageRegion;
+  /** Materialized Wasm containers add one label. No pending wrappers exist yet. */
+  readonly labelDepthDelta: 1;
+}
+
+/** Immediate concrete children in lexical order. No callbacks/recipe traversal. */
+export function materializedStageChildren(node: StageNode): readonly StageChild[] {
+  if (node.kind === "leaf") return [];
+  const instruction = node.instruction;
+  switch (instruction.op) {
+    case "block":
+    case "loop":
+    case "try_table":
+      return [{ slot: { kind: "body" }, region: instruction.body, labelDepthDelta: 1 }];
+    case "if":
+      return [
+        { slot: { kind: "then" }, region: instruction.then, labelDepthDelta: 1 },
+        ...(instruction.else
+          ? [{ slot: { kind: "else" as const }, region: instruction.else, labelDepthDelta: 1 as const }]
+          : []),
+      ];
+    case "try":
+      return [
+        { slot: { kind: "body" }, region: instruction.body, labelDepthDelta: 1 },
+        ...instruction.catches.map((c, index) => ({
+          slot: { kind: "catch" as const, index },
+          region: c.body,
+          labelDepthDelta: 1 as const,
+        })),
+        ...(instruction.catchAll
+          ? [{ slot: { kind: "catchAll" as const }, region: instruction.catchAll, labelDepthDelta: 1 as const }]
+          : []),
+      ];
+  }
+}

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import type { ValType } from "../ir/types.js";
+import type { ts } from "../ts-api.js";
+import type { CodegenContext, FunctionContext } from "./context/types.js";
+import { flushLateImportShifts } from "./expressions/late-imports.js";
+import { usesHostBigIntCarrier } from "./host-bigint-carrier.js";
+import { addUnionImports } from "./registry/imports.js";
+import { compileExpression } from "./shared.js";
+
+/** Compile String's operand without narrowing a proven native BigInt first. */
+export function compileStringConversionArgument(
+  ctx: CodegenContext,
+  fctx: FunctionContext,
+  argument: ts.Expression,
+): ValType | null {
+  const isBigIntArg = ctx.oracle.staticJsTypeOf(argument) === "bigint";
+  const hostBigIntArg = usesHostBigIntCarrier(ctx) && isBigIntArg;
+  const nativeBigIntArg = ctx.standalone && ctx.nativeStrings && isBigIntArg;
+  if (nativeBigIntArg) {
+    // Wide lowering needs its carrier types before emission. Boxing an i64
+    // afterward cannot recover the upper limbs already discarded by lowering.
+    addUnionImports(ctx);
+    flushLateImportShifts(ctx, fctx);
+  }
+  return compileExpression(ctx, fctx, argument, hostBigIntArg || nativeBigIntArg ? { kind: "externref" } : undefined);
+}

diff --git a/src/codegen/vec-pop-body.ts b/src/codegen/vec-pop-body.ts

+++ b/src/codegen/vec-pop-body.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,104 @@
+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import type { FuncHandle, Instr, ValType } from "../ir/types.js";
+
+/** Physical facts prepared and validated by the existing vec bridge owner. */
+export interface VecPopEntry {
+  readonly elemKey: string;
+  readonly vecTypeIdx: number;
+  readonly arrTypeIdx: number;
+  readonly isNativeStr: boolean;
+  readonly storageGet: FuncHandle | undefined;
+}
+
+/** Closed pop body construction; no module allocation or callable publication. */
+export function buildVecPopBody(
+  entries: readonly (Readonly<VecPopEntry> | undefined)[],
+  boxNumHandle: FuncHandle | undefined,
+): { locals: { name: string; type: ValType }[]; body: Instr[] } {
+  const locals: { name: string; type: ValType }[] = [{ name: "__any", type: { kind: "anyref" } }];
+  const body: Instr[] = [{ op: "local.get", index: 0 }, { op: "any.convert_extern" }, { op: "local.set", index: 1 }];
+  let current: Instr[] = [{ op: "ref.null.extern" }, { op: "return" }];
+  for (let i = entries.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
+    const entry = entries[i];
+    if (entry === undefined) continue;
+    const { elemKey, vecTypeIdx, arrTypeIdx, isNativeStr, storageGet } = entry;
+    const base = 1 + locals.length; // 1 param + locals so far
+    const vecL = base;
+    const lenL = base + 1;
+    locals.push(
+      { name: `__vpop_vec_${vecTypeIdx}`, type: { kind: "ref_null", typeIdx: vecTypeIdx } },
+      { name: `__vpop_len_${vecTypeIdx}`, type: { kind: "i32" } },
+    );
+    // (#2593) Packed i8/i16 elements need array.get_u and unsigned→f64; plain
+    // `array.get` is invalid Wasm on a packed array. Generic dynamic path reads
+    // zero-extended (the per-view signedness is at the typed `a[i]` site).
+    // (#2835) `i32_byte` (ArrayBuffer/DataView byte buffer) is now packed i8 too
+    // — same unsigned read/box. `i32_elem` (Int32/Uint32 element storage) stays
+    // full-width signed (plain `array.get`), preserving its pre-split behaviour.
+    const isPackedByte = elemKey === "i8_byte" || elemKey === "i16_byte" || elemKey === "i32_byte";
+    const boxInstrs: Instr[] =
+      elemKey === "externref"
+        ? []
+        : // (#3311) native-string element (`ref null $AnyString`) → externref via
+          // the plain anyref→externref box (no `__box_number`).
+          // (#4531/#4527) struct-ref elements box the same way.
+          isNativeStr || elemKey === "structref"
+          ? [{ op: "extern.convert_any" }]
+          : elemKey === "f64"
+            ? [{ op: "call", funcIdx: boxNumHandle! }]
+            : isPackedByte
+              ? [{ op: "f64.convert_i32_u" }, { op: "call", funcIdx: boxNumHandle! }]
+              : [{ op: "f64.convert_i32_s" }, { op: "call", funcIdx: boxNumHandle! }];
+    const thenBranch: Instr[] = [
+      { op: "local.get", index: 1 },
+      { op: "ref.cast", typeIdx: vecTypeIdx },
+      { op: "local.set", index: vecL },
+      { op: "local.get", index: vecL },
+      { op: "struct.get", typeIdx: vecTypeIdx, fieldIdx: 0 },
+      { op: "local.set", index: lenL },
+      // empty → undefined
+      { op: "local.get", index: lenL },
+      { op: "i32.eqz" },
+      {
+        op: "if",
+        blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+        then: [{ op: "ref.null.extern" }, { op: "return" }],
+      },
+      // value = data[len-1] (boxed)
+      { op: "local.get", index: vecL },
+      ...(storageGet === undefined
+        ? [{ op: "struct.get", typeIdx: vecTypeIdx, fieldIdx: 1 } as Instr]
+        : [{ op: "extern.convert_any" } as Instr]),
+      { op: "local.get", index: lenL },
+      { op: "i32.const", value: 1 },
+      { op: "i32.sub" },
+      ...(storageGet === undefined
+        ? [{ op: isPackedByte ? "array.get_u" : "array.get", typeIdx: arrTypeIdx } as Instr]
+        : [{ op: "call", funcIdx: storageGet } as Instr]),
+      ...boxInstrs,
+      // vec.length = len - 1 (value stays beneath on the stack)
+      { op: "local.get", index: vecL },
+      { op: "local.get", index: lenL },
+      { op: "i32.const", value: 1 },
+      { op: "i32.sub" },
+      { op: "struct.set", typeIdx: vecTypeIdx, fieldIdx: 0 },
+      { op: "return" },
+    ];
+    current = [
+      { op: "local.get", index: 1 },
+      { op: "ref.test", typeIdx: vecTypeIdx },
+      {
+        op: "if",
+        blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+        then: thenBranch,
+        else: current,
+      },
+    ];
+  }
+  body.push(...current);
+  // Every carrier arm and the unsupported fallback return. Make that terminal
+  // contract explicit after the empty-typed dispatch ladder: no result value
+  // may be invented for a fallthrough that cannot execute.
+  body.push({ op: "unreachable" });
+  return { locals, body };
+}

+   * sentinel rather than Wasm's numeric zero default. Marked externref fields
+   * use the prepared canonical undefined singleton; unmarked references retain
+   * their existing defaults. This logical marker is not a physical layout key.
    */
   undefinedDefault?: true;

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
+import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { runInNewContext } from "node:vm";
+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { compile } from "../src/index.js";
+import { ts } from "../src/ts-api.js";
+
+// Original September 15 sources and expectations are immutable. Historical
+// compiler answers are evidence only, never the expected result of this test.
+const bytes = readFileSync(
+  new URL("../plan/agent-context/5883-observable-vector-review-20260915.jsonl", import.meta.url),
+);
+if (
+  createHash("sha256").update(bytes).digest("hex") !==
+  "d9d792883c09853383845195ca9868ed97e506c6526b2434384c49a7f2d5f806"
+) {
+  throw new Error("Original observable-vector review receipt changed");
+}
+interface ReviewCase {
+  method: "all" | "race";
+  variant: string;
+  source: string;
+  reference: number | { calls: number; length: number; trace: string };
+}
+const cases: ReviewCase[] = bytes
+  .toString("utf8")
+  .trim()
+  .split("\n")
+  .map((line) => JSON.parse(line));
+if (cases.length !== 12 || new Set(cases.map((row) => `${row.method}/${row.variant}`)).size !== 12) {
+  throw new Error("Expected all twelve unique original review cases");
+}
+
+for (const row of cases) {
+  it(`original review: Promise.${row.method} ${row.variant}`, async () => {
+    const expected =
+      typeof row.reference === "number"
+        ? row.reference
+        : row.reference.calls * 1_000_000 + Number(row.reference.trace) * 10 + row.reference.length;
+    // A fresh realm prevents the fixture's Promise.resolve replacement from
+    // modifying Vitest or another case. Only types/module syntax are removed.
+    const nativeSource = ts.transpileModule(row.source, {
+      compilerOptions: { target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2022, module: ts.ModuleKind.CommonJS },
+    }).outputText;
+    const native = runInNewContext(
+      `${nativeSource}\nexports.test();`,
+      { exports: {} },
+      {
+        timeout: 5000,
+        microtaskMode: "afterEvaluate",
+      },
+    );
+    expect(native).toBe(expected);
+    const result = await compile(row.source, {
+      fileName: `5197-original-${row.method}-${row.variant}.ts`,
+      target: "standalone",
+      nativeStrings: true,
+      emitWat: true,
+    });
+    expect(result.success, JSON.stringify(result.errors)).toBe(true);
+    const module = new WebAssembly.Module(result.binary);
+    const imports = WebAssembly.Module.imports(module);
+    expect(imports).toEqual([]);
+    const instance = new WebAssembly.Instance(module, {});
+    const actual = (instance.exports.test as () => number)();
+    console.log(
+      JSON.stringify({
+        method: row.method,
+        variant: row.variant,
+        sourceSha256: createHash("sha256").update(row.source).digest("hex"),
+        native,
+        expected,
+        actual,
+        imports,
+        observableBody: result.wat?.includes("__comb_observable_") ?? false,
+      }),
+    );
+    expect(actual).toBe(expected);
+  });
+}
