+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { EmissionOwnership } from "../src/codegen/context/emission-ownership.js";
+import type { RetainedBodyManifest, SelectedBodyManifest } from "../src/codegen/context/emission-ownership.js";
+import { NativeProtoDemandCollection } from "../src/codegen/native-proto-demand.js";
+
+const provenance = { sourceId: "pure-test", unitId: "body" };
+function body(arena: EmissionOwnership, kind: "required-drive" | "protocol-choice" = "required-drive") {
+  const transaction = arena.beginContentTransaction();
+  const identity = { bodyIdentity: {}, bindingEnvironment: {} };
+  const epoch = arena.createBodyEpoch(provenance, identity);
+  arena.bindContentTransaction(transaction, epoch);
+  const nodeIdentity = {};
+  const occurrence = arena.recordOccurrence(epoch, provenance, { nodeIdentity, kind });
+  arena.commitTransaction(transaction);
+  const manifest: RetainedBodyManifest = {
+    body: epoch,
+    ...identity,
+    regions: [],
+    occurrences: [{ occurrence, owner: epoch, nodeIdentity, kind }],
+    adoptions: [],
+  };
+  const receipt = arena.completeBodyEpoch(epoch, manifest);
+  return { epoch, occurrence, manifest, receipt };
+}
+function publish(arena: EmissionOwnership, value: ReturnType<typeof body>): SelectedBodyManifest {
+  const publication = arena.createPublication("function", provenance);
+  arena.publishBody(publication, value.epoch);
+  return { publications: [{ publication, receipt: value.receipt }], bodies: [value.manifest] };
+}
+const recipe = (
+  occurrence: ReturnType<typeof body>["occurrence"],
+  reason: "mixed-generator-then" | "observable-vector" = "mixed-generator-then",
+) => ({ brand: "Promise", requiredMembers: ["then"], reason, occurrence }) as const;
+
+describe("metadata-only native prototype demand", () => {
+  it("preparation publishes no capability and does not seal the shared arena", () => {
+    const arena = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const value = body(arena);
+    const demands = new NativeProtoDemandCollection(arena);
+    const prepared = demands.prepare(recipe(value.occurrence));
+    expect(Object.isFrozen(prepared)).toBe(true);
+    expect(Object.isFrozen(prepared.requiredMembers)).toBe(true);
+    expect(demands.state).toBe("collecting");
+    expect(demands.selected).toEqual([]);
+    // Continued ordinary ownership construction is legal after preparation.
+    body(arena);
+    expect(demands.select(publish(arena, value))[0]?.consumers).toEqual([prepared]);
+    expect(demands.state).toBe("selected-awaiting-registration");
+  });
+
+  it("does not activate an authenticated but unpublished orphan", () => {
+    const arena = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const orphan = body(arena);
+    const demands = new NativeProtoDemandCollection(arena);
+    demands.prepare(recipe(orphan.occurrence));
+    expect(demands.select({ publications: [], bodies: [] })).toEqual([]);
+    expect(demands.state).toBe("inactive");
+  });
+
+  it("a selected protocol choice is not a required operation", () => {
+    const arena = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const value = body(arena, "protocol-choice");
+    const demands = new NativeProtoDemandCollection(arena);
+    demands.prepare(recipe(value.occurrence));
+    expect(demands.select(publish(arena, value))).toEqual([]);
+  });
+
+  it("keeps reasons independent while deduplicating each exact consumer", () => {
+    const arena = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const generator = body(arena);
+    const vector = body(arena);
+    const demands = new NativeProtoDemandCollection(arena);
+    const first = demands.prepare(recipe(generator.occurrence));
+    expect(demands.prepare(recipe(generator.occurrence))).toBe(first);
+    demands.prepare(recipe(vector.occurrence, "observable-vector"));
+    const selected = demands.select(publish(arena, generator));
+    expect(selected[0]?.consumers.map((r) => r.reason)).toEqual(["mixed-generator-then"]);
+    expect(selected[0]?.initializer).toBe("full-Promise-brand");
+  });
+
+  it("vector-only selection does not invent a generator consumer", () => {
+    const arena = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const vector = body(arena);
+    const demands = new NativeProtoDemandCollection(arena);
+    demands.prepare(recipe(vector.occurrence, "observable-vector"));
+    expect(demands.select(publish(arena, vector))[0]?.consumers.map((r) => r.reason)).toEqual(["observable-vector"]);
+  });
+
+  it("rejects foreign occurrences without changing collection state", () => {
+    const demands = new NativeProtoDemandCollection(new EmissionOwnership());
+    expect(() => demands.prepare(recipe(body(new EmissionOwnership()).occurrence))).toThrow();
+    expect(demands.state).toBe("collecting");
+    expect(demands.selected).toEqual([]);
+  });
+
+  it("rejects an incomplete census and allows a corrected verified selection", () => {
+    const arena = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const value = body(arena);
+    const demands = new NativeProtoDemandCollection(arena);
+    demands.prepare(recipe(value.occurrence));
+    const selected = publish(arena, value);
+    expect(() => demands.select({ ...selected, bodies: [] })).toThrow();
+    expect(demands.state).toBe("collecting");
+    expect(demands.select(selected)).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(() => demands.select(selected)).toThrow();
+    expect(() => demands.prepare(recipe(value.occurrence))).toThrow();
+  });
+
+  it("rejects a legacy-retained child despite authenticated parent and matching census", () => {
+    const arena = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const childIdentity = { bodyIdentity: {}, bindingEnvironment: {} };
+    const child = arena.createBodyEpoch(provenance, childIdentity);
+    const childTransaction = arena.beginContentTransaction(child);
+    const nodeIdentity = {};
+    const occurrence = arena.recordOccurrence(child, provenance, { nodeIdentity, kind: "required-drive" });
+    arena.commitTransaction(childTransaction);
+    const childManifest: RetainedBodyManifest = {
+      body: child,
+      ...childIdentity,
+      regions: [],
+      adoptions: [],
+      occurrences: [{ occurrence, owner: child, nodeIdentity, kind: "required-drive" }],
+    };
+    // Every child field is valid except the deliberately absent completion receipt.
+    arena.retainBodyEpoch(child);
+    const parentIdentity = { bodyIdentity: {}, bindingEnvironment: {} };
+    const parent = arena.createBodyEpoch(provenance, parentIdentity);
+    const parentTransaction = arena.beginContentTransaction(parent);
+    const useSiteIdentity = {};
+    const edge = arena.adoptBody(parent, child, useSiteIdentity);
+    arena.commitTransaction(parentTransaction);
+    const parentManifest: RetainedBodyManifest = {
+      body: parent,
+      ...parentIdentity,
+      regions: [],
+      occurrences: [],
+      adoptions: [{ edge, owner: parent, body: child, useSiteIdentity }],
+    };
+    const receipt = arena.completeBodyEpoch(parent, parentManifest);
+    const publication = arena.createPublication("function", provenance);
+    arena.publishBody(publication, parent);
+    const demands = new NativeProtoDemandCollection(arena);
+    demands.prepare(recipe(occurrence));
+    const selected: SelectedBodyManifest = {
+      publications: [{ publication, receipt }],
+      bodies: [parentManifest, childManifest],
+    };
+    expect(() => demands.select(selected)).toThrow("Selected body has no authenticated receipt");
+    expect(demands.state).toBe("collecting");
+    expect(demands.selected).toEqual([]);
+  });
+});

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
+import { runInNewContext } from "node:vm";
+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { compile } from "../src/index.js";
+
+// Count and raw output are separate observations, not a combined assertion.
+// Existing String13/raw5/valueOf31 fixtures remain unchanged.
+const cases: { name: string; body: string; expected: number | string }[] = [
+  {
+    name: "comma left increment count only",
+    body: "let count=0;const result=String((count++,18446744073709551617n));return count;",
+    expected: 1,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "comma wide positive raw result only",
+    body: "let count=0;return String((count++,18446744073709551617n));",
+    expected: "18446744073709551617",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "comma wide negative raw result only",
+    body: "let count=0;return String((count++,-18446744073709551617n));",
+    expected: "-18446744073709551617",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "nested comma evaluation trace only",
+    body: "let trace=0;const result=String((trace=trace*10+1,(trace=trace*10+2,18446744073709551617n)));return trace;",
+    expected: 12,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "nested comma wide raw result only",
+    body: "let trace=0;return String((trace=trace*10+1,(trace=trace*10+2,18446744073709551617n)));",
+    expected: "18446744073709551617",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "comma left throw prevents right evaluation",
+    body: "let left=0;let right=0;const reason={};function fail(){left++;throw reason;}try{String((fail(),(right++,18446744073709551617n)));return -1;}catch(e){if(e!==reason)return -2;if(right!==0)return -3;return left;}",
+    expected: 1,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "comma number raw result control",
+    body: "let count=0;return String((count++,1.5));",
+    expected: "1.5",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "comma void right retains both effects",
+    body: "let trace=0;function left(){trace=trace*10+1;}function right(){trace=trace*10+2;}(left(),right());return trace;",
+    expected: 12,
+  },
+];
+
+for (const { name, body, expected } of cases) {
+  it(name, async () => {
+    const output = `function output(){${body}}`;
+    const isText = typeof expected === "string";
+    const source =
+      output +
+      (isText
+        ? "\nexport function length(){return output().length;}\nexport function unit(index:number){return output().charCodeAt(index);}"
+        : "\nexport function test(){return output();}");
+    const sha256 = createHash("sha256").update(source).digest("hex");
+    const native = runInNewContext(output + ";output()", {}, { timeout: 5000 });
+    const receipt: Record<string, unknown> = { name, source, sha256, expected, native };
+    try {
+      const result = await compile(source, {
+        fileName: "5883-bigint-string-comma.ts",
+        skipSemanticDiagnostics: true,
+        target: "standalone",
+        nativeStrings: true,
+      });
+      receipt.success = result.success;
+      receipt.errors = result.errors;
+      expect(result.success, JSON.stringify(result.errors)).toBe(true);
+      const module = new WebAssembly.Module(result.binary);
+      receipt.imports = WebAssembly.Module.imports(module);
+      expect(receipt.imports).toEqual([]);
+      const instance = new WebAssembly.Instance(module, {});
+      if (isText) {
+        const length = (instance.exports.length as () => number)();
+        receipt.length = length;
+        expect(Number.isInteger(length) && length >= 0 && length <= 128).toBe(true);
+        const units: number[] = [];
+        for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
+          const unit = (instance.exports.unit as (index: number) => number)(i);
+          units.push(unit);
+          expect(Number.isInteger(unit) && unit >= 0 && unit <= 65535).toBe(true);
+        }
+        receipt.units = units;
+        receipt.actual = String.fromCharCode(...units);
+      } else {
+        receipt.actual = (instance.exports.test as () => number)();
+      }
+      expect(native).toBe(expected);
+      expect(receipt.actual).toBe(expected);
+    } catch (error) {
+      receipt.failure = String(error);
+      throw error;
+    } finally {
+      console.log(JSON.stringify(receipt));
+    }
+  });
+}

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
+import { runInNewContext } from "node:vm";
+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { compile } from "../src/index.js";
+const cases: [string, string][] = [
+  ["wide positive literal", 'return String(18446744073709551617n) === "18446744073709551617" ? 1 : -1;'],
+  ["wide negative literal", 'return String(-18446744073709551617n) === "-18446744073709551617" ? 1 : -1;'],
+  ["wide unsigned64 edge", 'return String(18446744073709551615n) === "18446744073709551615" ? 1 : -1;'],
+  ["wide constant expression", 'return String(18446744073709551616n + 3n) === "18446744073709551619" ? 1 : -1;'],
+  ["narrow bigint", 'return String(7n) === "7" && String(-7n) === "-7" && String(0n) === "0" ? 1 : -1;'],
+  ["exact signed64 edge", 'return String(9223372036854775807n) === "9223372036854775807" ? 1 : -1;'],
+  ["number controls", 'return String(1.5) === "1.5" && String(-0) === "0" && String(NaN) === "NaN" ? 1 : -1;'],
+  ["symbol control", 'return String(Symbol("x")) === "Symbol(x)" ? 1 : -1;'],
+  [
+    "boolean nullish and string controls",
+    'return String(true) === "true" && String(false) === "false" && String(null) === "null" && String(undefined) === "undefined" && String("ok") === "ok" ? 1 : -1;',
+  ],
+  [
+    "string hint and single evaluation",
+    'let evaluations=0;let reads=0;let hint;function source(){evaluations++;return {[Symbol.toPrimitive]:function(h){reads++;hint=h;return 7n;}};}const result=String(source());return result==="7" && evaluations===1 && reads===1 && hint==="string" ? 1 : -1;',
+  ],
+  [
+    "throwing conversion preserved",
+    "const reason={};const source={[Symbol.toPrimitive]:function(){throw reason;}};try{String(source);return -1;}catch(e){return e===reason?1:-2;}",
+  ],
+  [
+    "argument side effect once",
+    'let count=0;const result=String((count++,18446744073709551617n));return count===1 && result==="18446744073709551617" ? 1 : -1;',
+  ],
+  [
+    "ordinary conversion preference",
+    'let trace=0;const source={toString:function(){trace=trace*10+1;return "ok";},valueOf:function(){trace=trace*10+2;return 7n;}};return String(source)==="ok" && trace===1 ? 1 : -1;',
+  ],
+];
+for (const [name, body] of cases) {
+  it(name, async () => {
+    const source = `export function test() { ${body} }`;
+    const sha256 = createHash("sha256").update(source).digest("hex");
+    const native = runInNewContext(`(function () { ${body} })()`, {}, { timeout: 5000 });
+    const result = await compile(source, {
+      fileName: "5883-bigint-string.js",
+      allowJs: true,
+      skipSemanticDiagnostics: true,
+      target: "standalone",
+      nativeStrings: true,
+    });
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({ name, source, sha256, native, success: result.success, errors: result.errors }));
+    expect(result.success, JSON.stringify(result.errors)).toBe(true);
+    const module = new WebAssembly.Module(result.binary);
+    const imports = WebAssembly.Module.imports(module);
+    expect(imports).toEqual([]);
+    const instance = new WebAssembly.Instance(module, {});
+    const actual = (instance.exports.test as () => number)();
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({ name, source, sha256, native, actual, imports }));
+    expect(native).toBe(1);
+    expect(actual).toBe(1);
+  });
+}

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
+import { runInNewContext } from "node:vm";
+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { compile } from "../src/index.js";
+
+// Additive coverage; original fixtures and their expectations remain frozen.
+const cases: [string, string][] = [];
+for (const [label, receiver, expected] of [
+  ["primitive", "7n", "7"],
+  ["narrow wrapper", "Object(7n)", "7"],
+  ["wide primitive", "18446744073709551617n", "18446744073709551617"],
+  // Keep this control even if static Object(wide) narrows before valueOf.
+  ["wide wrapper", "Object(18446744073709551617n)", "18446744073709551617"],
+] as const) {
+  for (const extracted of [false, true]) {
+    for (const invocation of ["call", "apply"]) {
+      const method = extracted ? "method" : "BigInt.prototype.valueOf";
+      const call = invocation === "call" ? `${method}.call(receiver)` : `${method}.apply(receiver, [])`;
+      cases.push([
+        `${label}: ${extracted ? "extracted" : "direct"} ${invocation}`,
+        `
+        const receiver = ${receiver};
+        ${extracted ? "const method = BigInt.prototype.valueOf;" : ""}
+        const value = ${call};
+        if (typeof value !== "bigint") return -1;
+        return String(value) === "${expected}" ? 1 : -2;
+      `,
+      ]);
+    }
+  }
+}
+cases.push([
+  "wide runtime ToObject wrapper retains all limbs",
+  `
+  let wrapper;
+  Object.defineProperty(BigInt.prototype, "length", {
+    configurable: true, get: function () { "use strict"; wrapper = this; return 0; }
+  });
+  const iterator = Array.prototype.values.call(18446744073709551617n);
+  iterator.next();
+  if (typeof wrapper !== "object") return -1;
+  const method = BigInt.prototype.valueOf;
+  const value = method.call(wrapper);
+  return typeof value === "bigint" && String(value) === "18446744073709551617" ? 1 : -2;
+`,
+]);
+for (const expression of [
+  "null",
+  "undefined",
+  "7",
+  '"7"',
+  "true",
+  'Symbol("wrong")',
+  "{}",
+  "Object(7)",
+  '({ "[[PrimitiveValue]]": 7n })',
+  "BigInt.prototype",
+]) {
+  cases.push([
+    `rejects incompatible ${expression}`,
+    `
+    const method = BigInt.prototype.valueOf;
+    let rejected = 0;
+    try { BigInt.prototype.valueOf.call(${expression}); } catch (e) { if (e instanceof TypeError) rejected++; }
+    try { method.apply(${expression}, []); } catch (e) { if (e instanceof TypeError) rejected++; }
+    return rejected === 2 ? 1 : -1;
+  `,
+  ]);
+}
+cases.push([
+  "prototype override is observed and prior extraction retained",
+  `
+  const saved = BigInt.prototype.valueOf;
+  BigInt.prototype.valueOf = function () { return 19n; };
+  const direct = BigInt.prototype.valueOf.call(Object(7n));
+  const extracted = saved.call(Object(7n));
+  return direct === 19n && extracted === 7n ? 1 : -1;
+`,
+]);
+cases.push([
+  "local BigInt spelling does not select intrinsic",
+  `
+  const BigInt = { prototype: { valueOf: function () { "use strict"; return this.answer; } } };
+  return BigInt.prototype.valueOf.call({answer: 23}) === 23 ? 1 : -1;
+`,
+]);
+cases.push([
+  "reassigned extracted method uses current value",
+  `
+  let method = BigInt.prototype.valueOf;
+  method = function () { return 29n; };
+  return method.call(Object(7n)) === 29n ? 1 : -1;
+`,
+]);
+cases.push([
+  "wide primitive formatting control without valueOf",
+  `
+  return String(18446744073709551617n) === "18446744073709551617" ? 1 : -1;
+`,
+]);
+for (const [name, body] of cases) {
+  it(name, async () => {
+    const source = `export function test() { ${body} }`;
+    const sha256 = createHash("sha256").update(source).digest("hex");
+    const native = runInNewContext(`(function () { ${body} })()`, {}, { timeout: 5000 });
+    const result = await compile(source, {
+      fileName: "5883-bigint-valueof.js",
+      allowJs: true,
+      skipSemanticDiagnostics: true,
+      target: "standalone",
+      nativeStrings: true,
+    });
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({ name, source, sha256, native, success: result.success, errors: result.errors }));
+    expect(result.success, JSON.stringify(result.errors)).toBe(true);
+    const module = new WebAssembly.Module(result.binary);
+    const imports = WebAssembly.Module.imports(module);
+    expect(imports).toEqual([]);
+    const instance = new WebAssembly.Instance(module, {});
+    const actual = (instance.exports.test as () => number)();
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({ name, source, sha256, native, actual, imports }));
+    expect(native).toBe(1);
+    expect(actual).toBe(1);
+  });
+}

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
+import { runInNewContext } from "node:vm";
+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { compile } from "../src/index.js";
+
+// Supplemental localization controls. Original acceptance fixtures remain unchanged.
+const cases = [
+  {
+    name: "wide high-bit observable without valueOf",
+    source:
+      'export function test(){try{const box={v:18446744073709551617n};const value=Reflect.get(box,"v"); return (value >> 64n) === 1n && (value & 4294967295n) === 1n ? 1 : -1;}catch(e){return -90;}}',
+    expectedNative: 1,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "narrow negative control for high-bit observable",
+    source:
+      'export function test(){try{const box={v:1n};const value=Reflect.get(box,"v"); return (value >> 64n) === 0n && (value & 4294967295n) === 1n ? 1 : -1;}catch(e){return -90;}}',
+    expectedNative: 1,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "wide reference primitive through extracted valueOf",
+    source:
+      'export function test(){try{const box={v:18446744073709551617n};const input=Reflect.get(box,"v");const method=BigInt.prototype.valueOf;const value=method.call(input); return (value >> 64n) === 1n && (value & 4294967295n) === 1n ? 1 : -1;}catch(e){return -90;}}',
+    expectedNative: 1,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "wide reference primitive through direct valueOf",
+    source:
+      'export function test(){try{const box={v:18446744073709551617n};const input=Reflect.get(box,"v");const value=BigInt.prototype.valueOf.call(input); return (value >> 64n) === 1n && (value & 4294967295n) === 1n ? 1 : -1;}catch(e){return -90;}}',
+    expectedNative: 1,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "wide runtime wrapper through extracted valueOf",
+    source:
+      'export function test(){try{let wrapper;Object.defineProperty(BigInt.prototype,"length",{configurable:true,get:function(){"use strict";wrapper=this;return 0;}});Array.prototype.values.call(18446744073709551617n).next();const method=BigInt.prototype.valueOf;const value=method.call(wrapper);return (value >> 64n) === 1n && (value & 4294967295n) === 1n ? 1 : -1;}catch(e){return -90;}}',
+    expectedNative: 1,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "wide static wrapper separate constructor boundary",
+    source:
+      "export function test(){try{const wrapper=Object(18446744073709551617n);const method=BigInt.prototype.valueOf;const value=method.call(wrapper);return (value >> 64n) === 1n && (value & 4294967295n) === 1n ? 1 : -1;}catch(e){return -90;}}",
+    expectedNative: 1,
+  },
+];
+for (const { name, source } of cases) {
+  it(name, async () => {
+    const sha256 = createHash("sha256").update(source).digest("hex");
+    const native = runInNewContext(
+      source.replace("export function test", "function test") + ";test()",
+      {},
+      { timeout: 5000 },
+    );
+    const receipt: Record<string, unknown> = { name, source, sha256, native };
+    try {
+      const result = await compile(source, {
+        fileName: "5883-bigint-width-discriminator.js",
+        allowJs: true,
+        skipSemanticDiagnostics: true,
+        target: "standalone",
+        nativeStrings: true,
+      });
+      receipt.success = result.success;
+      receipt.errors = result.errors;
+      expect(result.success, JSON.stringify(result.errors)).toBe(true);
+      const module = new WebAssembly.Module(result.binary);
+      receipt.imports = WebAssembly.Module.imports(module);
+      expect(receipt.imports).toEqual([]);
+      const instance = new WebAssembly.Instance(module, {});
+      receipt.actual = (instance.exports.test as () => number)();
+      expect(native).toBe(1);
+      expect(receipt.actual).toBe(1);
+    } catch (error) {
+      receipt.failure = String(error);
+      throw error;
+    } finally {
+      console.log(JSON.stringify(receipt));
+    }
+  });
+}

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
+import ts from "typescript";
+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { compile } from "../src/index.js";
+
+const fixtures = [
+  {
+    name: "initialized reference field evaluates once and preserves wide value",
+    source: `
+    let calls = 0;
+    function decimal() { calls++; return "18446744073709551617"; }
+    class Box { value = BigInt(decimal()); }
+    export function test() { const b = new Box(); return calls === 1 && String(b.value) === "18446744073709551617" ? 1 : -1; }`,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "uninitialized bigint is undefined before direct runtime-wide write",
+    source: `
+    class Box { value!: bigint; }
+    export function test() {
+      const b = new Box();
+      if (b.value !== undefined || b.value === null) return -1;
+      b.value = BigInt("18446744073709551617");
+      return String(b.value) === "18446744073709551617" ? 1 : -2;
+    }`,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "inherited field defaults and initialization order retain reference values",
+    source: `
+    let order = 0;
+    function decimal() { order = order * 10 + 1; return "18446744073709551617"; }
+    class Base { value = BigInt(decimal()); missing!: bigint; constructor() { order = order * 10 + 2; } }
+    class Child extends Base { own = BigInt("36893488147419103233"); constructor() { super(); order = order * 10 + 3; } }
+    export function test() {
+      const b = new Child();
+      if (order !== 123 || b.missing !== undefined || b.missing === null) return -1;
+      return String(b.value) === "18446744073709551617" && String(b.own) === "36893488147419103233" ? 1 : -2;
+    }`,
+  },
+] as const;
+for (const fixture of fixtures)
+  for (const optimize of [0, 2] as const) {
+    it(`${fixture.name} O${optimize}`, async () => {
+      const sourceHash = createHash("sha256").update(fixture.source).digest("hex");
+      const js = ts.transpileModule(fixture.source.replace("export function", "function"), {
+        compilerOptions: { target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2022, module: ts.ModuleKind.None },
+      }).outputText;
+      const native = new Function(js + "; return test();")();
+      const result = await compile(fixture.source, {
+        fileName: "class-bigint-default.ts",
+        target: "standalone",
+        optimize,
+      });
+      console.log(
+        JSON.stringify({
+          name: fixture.name,
+          source: fixture.source,
+          sourceHash,
+          optimize,
+          native,
+          success: result.success,
+          errors: result.errors,
+        }),
+      );
+      expect(native).toBe(1);
+      expect(result.success).toBe(true);
+      const module = new WebAssembly.Module(result.binary);
+      const imports = WebAssembly.Module.imports(module);
+      console.log(JSON.stringify({ sourceHash, imports }));
+      expect(imports).toEqual([]);
+      const actual = (new WebAssembly.Instance(module, {}).exports.test as () => number)();
+      console.log(JSON.stringify({ sourceHash, optimize, actual }));
+      expect(actual).toBe(1);
+    });
+  }

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { beforeEach, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import type { ts } from "../src/ts-api.js";
+import type { FieldDef, ValType } from "../src/ir/types.js";
+import type { CodegenContext } from "../src/codegen/context/types.js";
+
+const mocks = vi.hoisted(() => ({ resolve: vi.fn(), storage: vi.fn(), ensure: vi.fn() }));
+vi.mock("../src/codegen/index.js", () => ({ resolveWasmType: mocks.resolve }));
+vi.mock("../src/codegen/bigint-field-carrier.js", () => ({ resolveClosedDataFieldStorage: mocks.storage }));
+vi.mock("../src/codegen/any-helpers.js", () => ({ ensureAnyValueType: mocks.ensure }));
+import {
+  classFieldDefaultMetadata,
+  prepareClassFieldDefaults,
+  resolveClassInstanceFieldStorage,
+} from "../src/codegen/class-field-storage.js";
+
+// Only opaque identity crosses the mocked mapper boundary; no real checker or
+// compiler context is constructed and no compiler/provider runs in these tests.
+const semantic = Object.freeze({}) as ts.Type;
+const ctx = Object.freeze({}) as CodegenContext;
+const ref: ValType = { kind: "ref_null", typeIdx: 17 };
+const marked: FieldDef = { name: "value", type: { kind: "externref" }, mutable: true, undefinedDefault: true };
+beforeEach(() => {
+  vi.resetAllMocks();
+});
+
+it("explicit native i64 bypasses scalar resolution and retains its default", () => {
+  const native: ValType = { kind: "i64" };
+  mocks.resolve.mockImplementation(() => {
+    throw new Error("must not resolve override");
+  });
+  mocks.storage.mockReturnValue({ type: native, nativeBigIntReference: false });
+  const result = resolveClassInstanceFieldStorage(ctx, semantic, native, true, true);
+  expect(mocks.resolve).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  expect(mocks.storage).toHaveBeenCalledExactlyOnceWith(ctx, semantic, native, native);
+  expect(result.type).toBe(native);
+  expect(classFieldDefaultMetadata(result)).toEqual({});
+});
+
+it("resolves scalar before storage policy, then applies void-cleared widening", () => {
+  const events: string[] = [];
+  mocks.resolve.mockImplementation(() => {
+    events.push("resolve");
+    return ref;
+  });
+  mocks.storage.mockImplementation((_ctx, _semantic, scalar, override) => {
+    events.push("storage");
+    expect(scalar).toBe(ref);
+    expect(override).toBeNull();
+    return Object.freeze({ type: ref, nativeBigIntReference: false });
+  });
+  const result = resolveClassInstanceFieldStorage(ctx, semantic, null, true, false);
+  expect(events).toEqual(["resolve", "storage"]);
+  expect(result).toEqual({ type: { kind: "externref" }, nativeBigIntReference: false });
+  expect(ref).toEqual({ kind: "ref_null", typeIdx: 17 });
+});
+
+it("void-cleared does not widen an explicit reference override", () => {
+  mocks.storage.mockReturnValue({ type: ref, nativeBigIntReference: false });
+  expect(resolveClassInstanceFieldStorage(ctx, semantic, ref, true, false).type).toBe(ref);
+  expect(mocks.resolve).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+});
+
+it("host-parent widening still applies after an explicit reference override", () => {
+  mocks.storage.mockReturnValue({ type: ref, nativeBigIntReference: false });
+  expect(resolveClassInstanceFieldStorage(ctx, semantic, ref, true, true).type).toEqual({ kind: "externref" });
+  expect(mocks.resolve).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+});
+
+it("widening uses the storage-policy result rather than the original scalar mapping", () => {
+  mocks.resolve.mockReturnValue(ref);
+  const scalar: ValType = { kind: "i64" };
+  mocks.storage.mockReturnValue({ type: scalar, nativeBigIntReference: false });
+  expect(resolveClassInstanceFieldStorage(ctx, semantic, null, true, true).type).toBe(scalar);
+});
+
+it("same physical externref does not transfer logical BigInt default intent", () => {
+  const type: ValType = Object.freeze({ kind: "externref" });
+  expect(classFieldDefaultMetadata({ type, nativeBigIntReference: true })).toEqual({ undefinedDefault: true });
+  expect(classFieldDefaultMetadata({ type, nativeBigIntReference: false })).toEqual({});
+  expect(classFieldDefaultMetadata({ type: ref, nativeBigIntReference: true })).toEqual({});
+});
+
+it("preparation is inert for no marker, unmarked reference and marked numeric fields", () => {
+  const numeric: FieldDef = { name: "optional", type: { kind: "f64" }, mutable: true, undefinedDefault: true };
+  prepareClassFieldDefaults(ctx, []);
+  prepareClassFieldDefaults(ctx, [{ name: "plain", type: { kind: "externref" }, mutable: true }, numeric]);
+  expect(mocks.ensure).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+});
+
+it("prepares once for multiple marked fields and accepts canonical global zero", () => {
+  const state = { undefinedGlobalIdx: undefined as number | undefined };
+  mocks.ensure.mockImplementation((received) => {
+    expect(received).toBe(state);
+    state.undefinedGlobalIdx = 0;
+  });
+  prepareClassFieldDefaults(state as CodegenContext, [marked, { ...marked, name: "inherited" }]);
+  expect(mocks.ensure).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+  expect(state.undefinedGlobalIdx).toBe(0);
+});
+
+it("missing singleton after preparation is an invariant, never null success", () => {
+  expect(() => prepareClassFieldDefaults(ctx, [marked])).toThrow("Class undefined default was not prepared");
+  expect(mocks.ensure).toHaveBeenCalledExactlyOnceWith(ctx);
+});
+
+it("retains optional-uninitialized numeric defaults independently of BigInt intent", () => {
+  const numeric = { type: { kind: "f64" } as ValType, nativeBigIntReference: false };
+  expect(classFieldDefaultMetadata(numeric, true)).toEqual({ undefinedDefault: true });
+  expect(classFieldDefaultMetadata(numeric, false)).toEqual({});
+  expect(classFieldDefaultMetadata(numeric)).toEqual({});
+});
+it("optional alone does not mark scalar overrides or unrelated reference fields", () => {
+  for (const type of [{ kind: "i64" }, { kind: "i32" }, { kind: "externref" }] as ValType[]) {
+    expect(classFieldDefaultMetadata({ type, nativeBigIntReference: false }, true)).toEqual({});
+  }
+});
+it("BigInt reference default remains marked independently of optional syntax", () => {
+  const plan = { type: { kind: "externref" } as ValType, nativeBigIntReference: true };
+  expect(classFieldDefaultMetadata(plan, false)).toEqual({ undefinedDefault: true });
+  expect(classFieldDefaultMetadata(plan, true)).toEqual({ undefinedDefault: true });
+});

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { classReferenceDefaultInstrs } from "../src/codegen/class-reference-default.js";
+import type { FieldDef } from "../src/ir/types.js";
+
+const marked: FieldDef = { name: "value", type: { kind: "externref" }, mutable: true, undefinedDefault: true };
+it("uses prepared canonical undefined including global zero without mutating inputs", () => {
+  const ctx = Object.freeze({ undefinedGlobalIdx: 0 });
+  const field = Object.freeze({ ...marked });
+  expect(classReferenceDefaultInstrs(ctx, field)).toEqual([
+    { op: "global.get", index: 0 },
+    { op: "extern.convert_any" },
+  ]);
+  expect(ctx).toEqual({ undefinedGlobalIdx: 0 });
+});
+it("emits fresh instruction identities for repeated allocation/assertion planning", () => {
+  const a = classReferenceDefaultInstrs({ undefinedGlobalIdx: 7 }, marked);
+  const b = classReferenceDefaultInstrs({ undefinedGlobalIdx: 7 }, marked);
+  expect(a).toEqual(b);
+  expect(a).not.toBe(b);
+  expect(a[0]).not.toBe(b[0]);
+  expect(a[1]).not.toBe(b[1]);
+});
+it("fails on missing preparation rather than returning null", () => {
+  expect(() => classReferenceDefaultInstrs({}, marked)).toThrow("Class undefined default was not prepared");
+});
+it("leaves unmarked externref defaults unchanged without a provider", () => {
+  expect(classReferenceDefaultInstrs({}, { name: "ordinary", type: { kind: "externref" }, mutable: true })).toEqual([
+    { op: "ref.null.extern" },
+  ]);
+});
+it("rejects accidental scalar use", () => {
+  expect(() => classReferenceDefaultInstrs({}, { name: "native", type: { kind: "i64" }, mutable: true })).toThrow(
+    "Expected class reference field",
+  );
+});

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+// Hume corrections review item 3. Separate from frozen40; authored, NOT executed.
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import {
+  EmissionOwnership,
+  type BodyEpoch,
+  type CompletedBody,
+  type EmissionOwner,
+  type RetainedAdoption,
+  type RetainedBodyManifest,
+  type RetainedOccurrence,
+} from "../src/codegen/context/emission-ownership.js";
+
+const provenance = (unitId: string) => ({ sourceId: "authenticated.ts", unitId });
+function candidate(graph: EmissionOwnership, name: string) {
+  const identity = { bodyIdentity: {}, bindingEnvironment: {} };
+  const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance(name), identity);
+  const manifest: RetainedBodyManifest = { body, ...identity, regions: [], occurrences: [], adoptions: [] };
+  return { body, manifest };
+}
+function pending(graph: EmissionOwnership, owner: EmissionOwner, name: string): RetainedOccurrence {
+  const binding = { nodeIdentity: {}, kind: "required-drive" as const };
+  return { occurrence: graph.recordOccurrence(owner, provenance(name), binding), owner, ...binding };
+}
+function adopt(graph: EmissionOwnership, owner: EmissionOwner, body: BodyEpoch): RetainedAdoption {
+  const useSiteIdentity = {};
+  return { edge: graph.adoptBody(owner, body, useSiteIdentity), owner, body, useSiteIdentity };
+}
+function publish(graph: EmissionOwnership, body: BodyEpoch, receipt: CompletedBody) {
+  const publication = graph.createPublication("function", provenance("root"));
+  graph.publishBody(publication, body);
+  return { publication, receipt };
+}
+
+describe("authenticated emission ownership review controls", () => {
+  it.each(["rollback", "commit"] as const)("individual removal %s has an exact completed census", (exit) => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const c = candidate(graph, "child");
+    const construction = graph.beginContentTransaction(c.body);
+    const region = graph.createRegion(c.body);
+    graph.attachRegion(c.body, region);
+    const x = pending(graph, region, "x");
+    const y = pending(graph, region, "y");
+    const membership = graph.occurrenceMembership(y.occurrence);
+    const probe = graph.beginContentTransaction(c.body);
+    graph.removeOccurrence(y.occurrence);
+    if (exit === "rollback") graph.rollbackTransaction(probe);
+    else graph.commitTransaction(probe);
+    graph.commitTransaction(construction);
+    const occurrences = exit === "rollback" ? [x, y] : [x];
+    const cm = { ...c.manifest, regions: [region], occurrences };
+    // A stale physical-node census must fail before the correct census succeeds.
+    expect(() => graph.completeBodyEpoch(c.body, { ...cm, occurrences: exit === "rollback" ? [x] : [x, y] })).toThrow(
+      "census mismatch",
+    );
+    graph.completeBodyEpoch(c.body, cm);
+    expect(graph.occurrenceMembership(y.occurrence)).toBe(membership);
+
+    // Verify through later adoption, not just direct publication of the child.
+    const p = candidate(graph, "parent");
+    const pt = graph.beginContentTransaction(p.body);
+    const edge = adopt(graph, p.body, c.body);
+    graph.commitTransaction(pt);
+    const pm = { ...p.manifest, adoptions: [edge] };
+    const receipt = graph.completeBodyEpoch(p.body, pm);
+    const result = graph.sealWithManifest({ publications: [publish(graph, p.body, receipt)], bodies: [pm, cm] });
+    expect(new Set(result.requiredOccurrences)).toEqual(new Set(occurrences.map((o) => o.occurrence)));
+    expect(result.requiredOccurrences).toHaveLength(occurrences.length);
+    expect(result.manifests.find((m) => m.body === c.body)?.occurrences).toEqual(occurrences);
+  });
+
+  it("within-body movement preserves the occurrence and binding through completion and verified seal", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const c = candidate(graph, "moving");
+    const construction = graph.beginContentTransaction(c.body);
+    const left = graph.createRegion(c.body);
+    const right = graph.createRegion(c.body);
+    const moving = graph.createRegion(c.body);
+    graph.attachRegion(c.body, left);
+    graph.attachRegion(c.body, right);
+    const oldEdge = graph.attachRegion(left, moving);
+    const x = pending(graph, moving, "x");
+    const membership = graph.occurrenceMembership(x.occurrence);
+    const move = graph.beginContentTransaction(c.body);
+    graph.detachEdge(oldEdge);
+    graph.attachRegion(right, moving);
+    graph.commitTransaction(move);
+    graph.commitTransaction(construction);
+    const manifest = { ...c.manifest, regions: [left, right, moving], occurrences: [x] };
+    const receipt = graph.completeBodyEpoch(c.body, manifest);
+    const result = graph.sealWithManifest({ publications: [publish(graph, c.body, receipt)], bodies: [manifest] });
+    expect(result.requiredOccurrences).toEqual([x.occurrence]);
+    expect(graph.occurrenceMembership(x.occurrence)).toBe(membership);
+    expect(result.manifests[0]?.occurrences[0]?.nodeIdentity).toBe(x.nodeIdentity);
+    expect(result.manifests[0]?.occurrences[0]?.owner).toBe(moving);
+    expect(new Set(result.ownership.regions)).toEqual(new Set([left, right, moving]));
+    // Regions are a set census, NOT a proof of physical parent/order equality.
+  });
+
+  it("mutual body recursion authenticates each completed body and selects each witness once", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const a = candidate(graph, "a");
+    const b = candidate(graph, "b");
+    const at = graph.beginContentTransaction(a.body);
+    const x = pending(graph, a.body, "x");
+    const ab = adopt(graph, a.body, b.body);
+    graph.commitTransaction(at);
+    const am = { ...a.manifest, occurrences: [x], adoptions: [ab] };
+    const ar = graph.completeBodyEpoch(a.body, am);
+    const bt = graph.beginContentTransaction(b.body);
+    const y = pending(graph, b.body, "y");
+    const ba = adopt(graph, b.body, a.body);
+    graph.commitTransaction(bt);
+    const bm = { ...b.manifest, occurrences: [y], adoptions: [ba] };
+    graph.completeBodyEpoch(b.body, bm);
+    const result = graph.sealWithManifest({ publications: [publish(graph, a.body, ar)], bodies: [am, bm] });
+    expect(result.ownership.bodies).toHaveLength(2);
+    expect(new Set(result.ownership.bodies)).toEqual(new Set([a.body, b.body]));
+    expect(result.requiredOccurrences).toHaveLength(2);
+    expect(new Set(result.requiredOccurrences)).toEqual(new Set([x.occurrence, y.occurrence]));
+    expect(result.manifests.flatMap((m) => m.adoptions)).toHaveLength(2);
+  });
+
+  it("cancelling one independent adopter edge preserves the other adopter's completed dependency", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const c = candidate(graph, "shared");
+    const ct = graph.beginContentTransaction(c.body);
+    const x = pending(graph, c.body, "x");
+    graph.commitTransaction(ct);
+    const cm = { ...c.manifest, occurrences: [x] };
+    graph.completeBodyEpoch(c.body, cm);
+    const p = candidate(graph, "cancelled-adopter");
+    const q = candidate(graph, "retained-adopter");
+    const pt = graph.beginContentTransaction(p.body);
+    const cancelled = adopt(graph, p.body, c.body);
+    const qt = graph.beginContentTransaction(q.body);
+    const retained = adopt(graph, q.body, c.body);
+    graph.commitTransaction(qt);
+    const qm = { ...q.manifest, adoptions: [retained] };
+    const qr = graph.completeBodyEpoch(q.body, qm);
+    // Q is independently complete while P is still editable.
+    const removal = graph.beginContentTransaction(p.body);
+    graph.detachEdge(cancelled.edge);
+    graph.commitTransaction(removal);
+    graph.commitTransaction(pt);
+    expect(() => graph.completeBodyEpoch(p.body, { ...p.manifest, adoptions: [cancelled] })).toThrow(
+      "Adoption census mismatch",
+    );
+    const pr = graph.completeBodyEpoch(p.body, p.manifest);
+    const result = graph.sealWithManifest({
+      publications: [publish(graph, p.body, pr), publish(graph, q.body, qr)],
+      bodies: [p.manifest, qm, cm],
+    });
+    expect(new Set(result.ownership.bodies)).toEqual(new Set([p.body, q.body, c.body]));
+    expect(result.requiredOccurrences).toEqual([x.occurrence]);
+    expect(result.manifests.flatMap((m) => m.adoptions)).toEqual([retained]);
+    expect(retained.useSiteIdentity).not.toBe(cancelled.useSiteIdentity);
+  });
+
+  it("an authenticated root cannot authorize a selected dependency retained only by the legacy API", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const c = candidate(graph, "legacy-only");
+    const ct = graph.beginContentTransaction(c.body);
+    const x = pending(graph, c.body, "x");
+    graph.commitTransaction(ct);
+    const cm = { ...c.manifest, occurrences: [x] };
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(c.body); // Deliberately omit completion; every other field is valid.
+    const p = candidate(graph, "authenticated-root");
+    const pt = graph.beginContentTransaction(p.body);
+    const adoption = adopt(graph, p.body, c.body);
+    graph.commitTransaction(pt);
+    const pm = { ...p.manifest, adoptions: [adoption] };
+    const receipt = graph.completeBodyEpoch(p.body, pm);
+    const selected = { publications: [publish(graph, p.body, receipt)], bodies: [pm, cm] };
+    expect(() => graph.sealWithManifest(selected)).toThrow("Selected body has no authenticated receipt");
+    expect(() => graph.sealWithManifest(selected)).toThrow("Selected body has no authenticated receipt");
+    // No raw-seal fallback and no assertion that absence of a receipt can activate.
+  });
+});

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+// R1/R2/R3 and adapter census regressions. Authored only; NOT executed.
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import {
+  EmissionOwnership,
+  type BodyEpoch,
+  type RetainedBodyManifest,
+  type RetainedOccurrence,
+} from "../src/codegen/context/emission-ownership.js";
+
+const provenance = (unitId: string) => ({ sourceId: "review.ts", unitId });
+function candidate(graph: EmissionOwnership, name: string) {
+  const identity = { bodyIdentity: {}, bindingEnvironment: {} };
+  const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance(name), identity);
+  const manifest: RetainedBodyManifest = { body, ...identity, regions: [], occurrences: [], adoptions: [] };
+  return { body, manifest };
+}
+function pending(graph: EmissionOwnership, body: BodyEpoch, label: string): RetainedOccurrence {
+  const binding = { nodeIdentity: {}, kind: "required-drive" as const };
+  const occurrence = graph.recordOccurrence(body, provenance(label), binding);
+  return { occurrence, owner: body, ...binding };
+}
+
+describe("emission ownership review corrections", () => {
+  it.each(["rollback", "commit"] as const)(
+    "caller nested inside content: %s cannot clear unscoped-content refusal",
+    (exit) => {
+      const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+      const c = candidate(graph, "c");
+      const content = graph.beginContentTransaction(c.body);
+      const caller = graph.beginTransaction();
+      const x = pending(graph, c.body, "caller-write");
+      if (exit === "rollback") graph.rollbackTransaction(caller);
+      else graph.commitTransaction(caller);
+      graph.commitTransaction(content);
+      // Abort removes x; commit retains x. Neither authenticates its owner.
+      const manifest = { ...c.manifest, occurrences: exit === "commit" ? [x] : [] };
+      expect(() => graph.completeBodyEpoch(c.body, manifest)).toThrow("settled explicit");
+      // Inspect membership without pretending the raw ledger is adapter approval.
+      graph.retainBodyEpoch(c.body);
+      graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("c")), c.body);
+      expect(graph.sealSourcePublication().occurrences).toHaveLength(exit === "commit" ? 1 : 0);
+    },
+  );
+
+  it("R1: a construction token predating its epoch must be bound and closed before retention", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const token = graph.beginContentTransaction();
+    const { body, manifest } = candidate(graph, "B");
+    expect(() => graph.recordOccurrence(body, provenance("unbound"))).toThrow("Bind");
+    graph.bindContentTransaction(token, body);
+    const x = pending(graph, body, "x");
+    expect(() => graph.retainBodyEpoch(body)).toThrow("Close internal");
+    expect(() => graph.completeBodyEpoch(body, { ...manifest, occurrences: [x] })).toThrow("settled explicit");
+    graph.rollbackTransaction(token);
+    expect(() => graph.retainBodyEpoch(body)).toThrow();
+    expect(() => graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("B")), body)).toThrow("discarded");
+  });
+
+  it("R1: same-body nested commit is cancelled by construction-parent abort", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const { body, manifest } = candidate(graph, "B");
+    const outer = graph.beginContentTransaction(body);
+    const inner = graph.beginContentTransaction(body);
+    pending(graph, body, "x");
+    graph.commitTransaction(inner);
+    expect(() => graph.retainBodyEpoch(body)).toThrow("Close internal");
+    graph.rollbackTransaction(outer);
+    expect(() => graph.completeBodyEpoch(body, manifest)).toThrow("settled explicit");
+    expect(() => graph.retainBodyEpoch(body)).toThrow("failed body");
+  });
+
+  it("R1: independent C completion keeps x, excludes local y, and survives P rollback", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const p = candidate(graph, "P");
+    const outer = graph.beginContentTransaction(p.body);
+    const c = candidate(graph, "C");
+    const ct = graph.beginContentTransaction(c.body);
+    const x = pending(graph, c.body, "x");
+    const inner = graph.beginContentTransaction(c.body);
+    pending(graph, c.body, "y");
+    graph.rollbackTransaction(inner);
+    graph.commitTransaction(ct);
+    const cm = { ...c.manifest, occurrences: [x] };
+    graph.completeBodyEpoch(c.body, cm);
+    graph.adoptBody(p.body, c.body, {});
+    graph.rollbackTransaction(outer);
+    const q = candidate(graph, "Q");
+    const qt = graph.beginContentTransaction(q.body);
+    const site = {};
+    const edge = graph.adoptBody(q.body, c.body, site);
+    graph.commitTransaction(qt);
+    const qm = { ...q.manifest, adoptions: [{ edge, owner: q.body, body: c.body, useSiteIdentity: site }] };
+    const qr = graph.completeBodyEpoch(q.body, qm);
+    const root = graph.createPublication("function", provenance("Q"));
+    graph.publishBody(root, q.body);
+    const result = graph.sealWithManifest({ publications: [{ publication: root, receipt: qr }], bodies: [qm, cm] });
+    expect(result.requiredOccurrences).toEqual([x.occurrence]);
+    expect(result.ownership.bodies).not.toContain(p.body);
+  });
+
+  it("R1: wrong-body writes and closed/unbound token reuse are rejected", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const a = candidate(graph, "a"),
+      b = candidate(graph, "b");
+    const token = graph.beginContentTransaction(a.body);
+    expect(() => pending(graph, b.body, "wrong")).toThrow("Wrong body");
+    graph.commitTransaction(token);
+    expect(() => graph.bindContentTransaction(token, b.body)).toThrow("LIFO");
+    const unbound = graph.beginContentTransaction();
+    expect(() => graph.commitTransaction(unbound)).toThrow("unbound");
+    graph.rollbackTransaction(unbound);
+  });
+
+  it("R2: removes y alone from a shared region while retaining x and its membership identity", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("b"));
+    const region = graph.createRegion(body);
+    graph.attachRegion(body, region);
+    const x = graph.recordOccurrence(region, provenance("x"));
+    const y = graph.recordOccurrence(region, provenance("y"));
+    const membership = graph.occurrenceMembership(x);
+    graph.removeOccurrence(y);
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(body);
+    graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("b")), body);
+    expect(graph.sealSourcePublication().occurrences.map((o) => o.occurrence)).toEqual([x]);
+    expect(graph.occurrenceMembership(x)).toBe(membership);
+  });
+
+  it("R2: aborted occurrence removal restores both siblings", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("b"));
+    const x = graph.recordOccurrence(body, provenance("x"));
+    const y = graph.recordOccurrence(body, provenance("y"));
+    const tx = graph.beginTransaction();
+    graph.removeOccurrence(y);
+    graph.rollbackTransaction(tx);
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(body);
+    graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("b")), body);
+    expect(new Set(graph.sealSourcePublication().occurrences.map((o) => o.occurrence))).toEqual(new Set([x, y]));
+  });
+
+  it("R2: committed removal remains absent through later orphan adoption and region movement", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const parent = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("p"));
+    const probe = graph.beginTransaction();
+    const child = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("c"));
+    const a = graph.createRegion(child),
+      b = graph.createRegion(child);
+    const ae = graph.attachRegion(child, a);
+    graph.attachRegion(child, b);
+    const x = graph.recordOccurrence(a, provenance("x"));
+    const y = graph.recordOccurrence(a, provenance("y"));
+    const local = graph.beginTransaction();
+    graph.removeOccurrence(y);
+    graph.commitTransaction(local);
+    graph.detachEdge(ae);
+    graph.attachRegion(b, a);
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(child);
+    graph.adoptBody(parent, child);
+    graph.rollbackTransaction(probe);
+    graph.adoptBody(parent, child);
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(parent);
+    graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("p")), parent);
+    expect(graph.sealSourcePublication().occurrences.map((o) => o.occurrence)).toEqual([x]);
+  });
+
+  it("R2: retained, sealed and foreign occurrence removals are rejected", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("b"));
+    const x = graph.recordOccurrence(body, provenance("x"));
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(body);
+    expect(() => graph.removeOccurrence(x)).toThrow("not editable");
+    expect(() => new EmissionOwnership().removeOccurrence(x)).toThrow("foreign");
+    graph.sealSourcePublication();
+    expect(() => graph.removeOccurrence(x)).toThrow("sealed");
+  });
+
+  it("R3: rejects self-containment before any edge is installed", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("b"));
+    const region = graph.createRegion(body);
+    expect(() => graph.attachRegion(region, region)).toThrow("containment cycle");
+    graph.attachRegion(body, region);
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(body);
+    graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("b")), body);
+    expect(graph.sealSourcePublication().regions).toEqual([region]);
+  });
+
+  it("R3: ancestor-under-descendant fails and rollback restores the old tree", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("b"));
+    const a = graph.createRegion(body),
+      b = graph.createRegion(body);
+    const ae = graph.attachRegion(body, a);
+    graph.attachRegion(a, b);
+    const x = graph.recordOccurrence(b, provenance("x"));
+    const tx = graph.beginTransaction();
+    graph.detachEdge(ae);
+    expect(() => graph.attachRegion(b, a)).toThrow("containment cycle");
+    graph.rollbackTransaction(tx);
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(body);
+    graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("b")), body);
+    expect(graph.sealSourcePublication().occurrences[0]?.occurrence).toBe(x);
+  });
+
+  it("R3: valid sibling reattachment and recursive body adoption remain legal", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("b"));
+    const a = graph.createRegion(body),
+      b = graph.createRegion(body);
+    const ae = graph.attachRegion(body, a);
+    graph.attachRegion(body, b);
+    const x = graph.recordOccurrence(a, provenance("x"));
+    graph.detachEdge(ae);
+    graph.attachRegion(b, a);
+    graph.adoptBody(b, body);
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(body);
+    graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("b")), body);
+    expect(graph.sealSourcePublication().occurrences.map((o) => o.occurrence)).toEqual([x]);
+  });
+
+  it("manifest: missing/extra occurrence and stale frame/body identities cannot complete a body", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const c = candidate(graph, "c");
+    const tx = graph.beginContentTransaction(c.body);
+    const x = pending(graph, c.body, "x");
+    graph.commitTransaction(tx);
+    expect(() => graph.completeBodyEpoch(c.body, c.manifest)).toThrow("census mismatch");
+    expect(() => graph.completeBodyEpoch(c.body, { ...c.manifest, occurrences: [x, x] })).toThrow("census mismatch");
+    expect(() => graph.completeBodyEpoch(c.body, { ...c.manifest, bodyIdentity: {}, occurrences: [x] })).toThrow(
+      "Stale",
+    );
+    expect(() => graph.completeBodyEpoch(c.body, { ...c.manifest, bindingEnvironment: {}, occurrences: [x] })).toThrow(
+      "Stale",
+    );
+    graph.completeBodyEpoch(c.body, { ...c.manifest, occurrences: [x] });
+  });
+
+  it("manifest: one pending node cannot own multiple occurrences and evidence cannot change kind", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const c = candidate(graph, "c");
+    const tx = graph.beginContentTransaction(c.body);
+    const x = pending(graph, c.body, "x");
+    expect(() => graph.recordOccurrence(c.body, provenance("duplicate"), x)).toThrow("already has an occurrence");
+    graph.commitTransaction(tx);
+    expect(() =>
+      graph.completeBodyEpoch(c.body, { ...c.manifest, occurrences: [{ ...x, kind: "seed-edge" }] }),
+    ).toThrow("census mismatch");
+  });
+
+  it("manifest: conflicting use-site targets are rejected without disturbing the original adoption", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const p = candidate(graph, "p"),
+      a = candidate(graph, "a"),
+      b = candidate(graph, "b");
+    const tx = graph.beginContentTransaction(p.body);
+    const site = {};
+    const edge = graph.adoptBody(p.body, a.body, site);
+    expect(() => graph.adoptBody(p.body, b.body, site)).toThrow("already has an adoption target");
+    graph.commitTransaction(tx);
+    graph.completeBodyEpoch(p.body, {
+      ...p.manifest,
+      adoptions: [{ edge, owner: p.body, body: a.body, useSiteIdentity: site }],
+    });
+  });
+
+  it("manifest: root omission and missing selected bodies refuse seal without caching partial approval", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const c = candidate(graph, "c");
+    const tx = graph.beginContentTransaction(c.body);
+    graph.commitTransaction(tx);
+    const receipt = graph.completeBodyEpoch(c.body, c.manifest);
+    const root = graph.createPublication("function", provenance("c"));
+    graph.publishBody(root, c.body);
+    expect(() => graph.sealWithManifest({ publications: [], bodies: [c.manifest] })).toThrow("root census");
+    expect(() => graph.sealWithManifest({ publications: [{ publication: root, receipt }], bodies: [] })).toThrow(
+      "body census",
+    );
+    const selected = { publications: [{ publication: root, receipt }], bodies: [c.manifest] };
+    expect(graph.sealWithManifest(selected).ownership.bodies).toEqual([c.body]);
+    expect(graph.sealWithManifest(selected)).toBe(graph.sealWithManifest(selected));
+  });
+
+  it("manifest: choices/seeds are not activation witnesses and snapshots defensively copy arrays", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const c = candidate(graph, "c");
+    const tx = graph.beginContentTransaction(c.body);
+    const binding = { nodeIdentity: {}, kind: "protocol-choice" as const };
+    const occurrence = graph.recordOccurrence(c.body, provenance("choice"), binding);
+    graph.commitTransaction(tx);
+    const items = [{ occurrence, owner: c.body, ...binding }];
+    const manifest = { ...c.manifest, occurrences: items };
+    const receipt = graph.completeBodyEpoch(c.body, manifest);
+    const root = graph.createPublication("function", provenance("c"));
+    graph.publishBody(root, c.body);
+    const result = graph.sealWithManifest({ publications: [{ publication: root, receipt }], bodies: [manifest] });
+    items.length = 0;
+    expect(result.requiredOccurrences).toEqual([]);
+    expect(result.manifests[0]?.occurrences).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(Object.isFrozen(result.manifests[0]?.occurrences[0])).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it("manifest: legacy caller-scope retention cannot authenticate an independent completion", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const caller = graph.beginTransaction();
+    const c = candidate(graph, "c");
+    const x = pending(graph, c.body, "x");
+    expect(() => graph.completeBodyEpoch(c.body, { ...c.manifest, occurrences: [x] })).toThrow("settled explicit");
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(c.body); // original inspection API, deliberately no receipt
+    graph.rollbackTransaction(caller);
+    graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("c")), c.body);
+    expect(() => graph.sealWithManifest({ publications: [], bodies: [] })).toThrow("root census");
+  });
+});

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+// Metadata foundation only; authored source-only, no compiler or activation.
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { EmissionOwnership, type EmissionProvenance } from "../src/codegen/context/emission-ownership.js";
+
+const provenance = (unitId: string, ast?: object): EmissionProvenance => ({ sourceId: "source.ts", unitId, ast });
+
+describe("context-local emission ownership", () => {
+  it("seals empty metadata without inventing a root", () => {
+    expect(new EmissionOwnership().sealSourcePublication()).toStrictEqual({
+      publications: [],
+      bodies: [],
+      regions: [],
+      occurrences: [],
+      unselectedBodies: [],
+      discardedBodies: [],
+    });
+  });
+
+  it("inner commit remains cancellable by outer rollback", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("root"));
+    const publication = graph.createPublication("function", provenance("root"));
+    const outer = graph.beginTransaction();
+    const inner = graph.beginTransaction();
+    graph.recordOccurrence(body, provenance("rejected"));
+    graph.publishBody(publication, body);
+    graph.commitTransaction(inner);
+    graph.rollbackTransaction(outer);
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(body);
+    graph.publishBody(publication, body);
+    expect(graph.sealSourcePublication().occurrences).toEqual([]);
+  });
+
+  it("inner rollback removes only its emission when the outer transaction commits", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("root"));
+    const outer = graph.beginTransaction();
+    const keep = graph.recordOccurrence(body, provenance("keep"));
+    const inner = graph.beginTransaction();
+    graph.recordOccurrence(body, provenance("drop"));
+    graph.rollbackTransaction(inner);
+    graph.commitTransaction(outer);
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(body);
+    graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("root")), body);
+    expect(graph.sealSourcePublication().occurrences.map((o) => o.occurrence)).toEqual([keep]);
+  });
+
+  it("outer commit and a retained body alone do not publish demand", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const tx = graph.beginTransaction();
+    const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("discovery"));
+    graph.recordOccurrence(body, provenance("discovery"));
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(body);
+    graph.commitTransaction(tx);
+    const result = graph.sealSourcePublication();
+    expect(result.occurrences).toEqual([]);
+    expect(result.unselectedBodies).toEqual([body]);
+  });
+
+  it("roots only the selected initializer/prepared epoch, not its replacement", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const slot = graph.createPublication("module-init", provenance("init"));
+    const first = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("init"));
+    const second = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("init"));
+    graph.recordOccurrence(first, provenance("pass1"));
+    const selected = graph.recordOccurrence(second, provenance("pass2"));
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(first);
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(second);
+    graph.publishBody(slot, first);
+    graph.publishBody(slot, second);
+    const result = graph.sealSourcePublication();
+    expect(result.bodies).toEqual([second]);
+    expect(result.unselectedBodies).toEqual([first]);
+    expect(result.occurrences.map((o) => o.occurrence)).toEqual([selected]);
+  });
+
+  it("rollback restores the prior publication binding after replacement and removal", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const slot = graph.createPublication("function", provenance("f"));
+    const first = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("f"));
+    const second = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("f"));
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(first);
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(second);
+    graph.publishBody(slot, first);
+    const tx = graph.beginTransaction();
+    graph.publishBody(slot, second);
+    graph.unpublishBody(slot);
+    graph.rollbackTransaction(tx);
+    expect(graph.sealSourcePublication().publications[0]?.body).toBe(first);
+  });
+
+  it("an ordinary published function is a pre-DCE root even without a caller", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("neverCalled"));
+    const occurrence = graph.recordOccurrence(body, provenance("neverCalled"));
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(body);
+    graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("neverCalled")), body);
+    expect(graph.sealSourcePublication().occurrences[0]?.occurrence).toBe(occurrence);
+  });
+
+  it("same AST in separate epochs has distinct occurrences and explicit selection", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const ast = {};
+    const first = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("f", ast));
+    const second = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("f", ast));
+    const a = graph.recordOccurrence(first, provenance("f", ast));
+    const b = graph.recordOccurrence(second, provenance("f", ast));
+    expect(a).not.toBe(b);
+    expect(a.id).not.toBe(b.id);
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(first);
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(second);
+    graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("f")), second);
+    const selected = graph.sealSourcePublication().occurrences;
+    expect(selected.map((o) => o.occurrence)).toEqual([b]);
+    expect(selected[0]?.provenance.ast).toBe(ast);
+  });
+
+  it("two visits to one AST within one epoch are not deduplicated", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const ast = {};
+    const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("f"));
+    const a = graph.recordOccurrence(body, provenance("f", ast));
+    const b = graph.recordOccurrence(body, provenance("f", ast));
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(body);
+    graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("f")), body);
+    expect(new Set(graph.sealSourcePublication().occurrences.map((o) => o.occurrence))).toEqual(new Set([a, b]));
+  });
+
+  it("a retained lifted body survives probe rollback and is selected by later exact adoption", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const parent = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("parent"));
+    const probe = graph.beginTransaction();
+    const lifted = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("lifted"));
+    const occurrence = graph.recordOccurrence(lifted, provenance("lifted"));
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(lifted);
+    graph.adoptBody(parent, lifted);
+    graph.rollbackTransaction(probe);
+    // This edge is new. No function/closure registry can implicitly supply it.
+    graph.adoptBody(parent, lifted);
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(parent);
+    graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("parent")), parent);
+    const result = graph.sealSourcePublication();
+    expect(result.bodies).toContain(lifted);
+    expect(result.occurrences[0]?.occurrence).toBe(occurrence);
+  });
+
+  it("a retained probe orphan without a new adoption does not select its occurrence", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const parent = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("parent"));
+    const probe = graph.beginTransaction();
+    const orphan = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("orphan"));
+    graph.recordOccurrence(orphan, provenance("orphan"));
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(orphan);
+    graph.adoptBody(parent, orphan);
+    graph.rollbackTransaction(probe);
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(parent);
+    graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("parent")), parent);
+    const result = graph.sealSourcePublication();
+    expect(result.occurrences).toEqual([]);
+    expect(result.unselectedBodies).toEqual([orphan]);
+  });
+
+  it("unfinished bodies created by a rolled-back probe are discarded, not reusable", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const probe = graph.beginTransaction();
+    const unfinished = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("unfinished"));
+    graph.recordOccurrence(unfinished, provenance("unfinished"));
+    graph.rollbackTransaction(probe);
+    expect(() => graph.retainBodyEpoch(unfinished)).toThrow("not editable");
+    expect(graph.sealSourcePublication().discardedBodies).toEqual([unfinished]);
+  });
+
+  it("explicit orphan discard prevents later adoption", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const parent = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("parent"));
+    const orphan = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("orphan"));
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(orphan);
+    graph.discardBodyEpoch(orphan);
+    expect(() => graph.adoptBody(parent, orphan)).toThrow("discarded");
+    expect(graph.sealSourcePublication().discardedBodies).toEqual([orphan]);
+  });
+
+  it("selected unfinished/discarded dependencies fail seal instead of silently vanishing", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const parent = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("parent"));
+    const child = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("child"));
+    graph.adoptBody(parent, child);
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(parent);
+    graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("parent")), parent);
+    expect(() => graph.sealSourcePublication()).toThrow("unfinished or discarded");
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(child);
+    graph.discardBodyEpoch(child);
+    expect(() => graph.sealSourcePublication()).toThrow("unfinished or discarded");
+  });
+
+  it("requires internal transactions to settle before freezing a preexisting body", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("f"));
+    const tx = graph.beginTransaction();
+    graph.recordOccurrence(body, provenance("tentative"));
+    expect(() => graph.retainBodyEpoch(body)).toThrow("Close internal body transactions");
+    graph.rollbackTransaction(tx);
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(body);
+    graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("f")), body);
+    expect(graph.sealSourcePublication().occurrences).toEqual([]);
+  });
+
+  it("enforces LIFO, single-close transactions and rejects sealing an open scope", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const outer = graph.beginTransaction();
+    const inner = graph.beginTransaction();
+    expect(() => graph.commitTransaction(outer)).toThrow("LIFO");
+    expect(() => graph.sealSourcePublication()).toThrow("open emission transactions");
+    graph.commitTransaction(inner);
+    expect(() => graph.rollbackTransaction(inner)).toThrow("LIFO");
+    graph.rollbackTransaction(outer);
+  });
+
+  it("region detach/move rollback restores the previous edge without cloning occurrences", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("f"));
+    const region = graph.createRegion(body);
+    const wrapper = graph.createRegion(body);
+    const old = graph.attachRegion(body, region);
+    graph.attachRegion(body, wrapper);
+    const occurrence = graph.recordOccurrence(region, provenance("inside"));
+    const tx = graph.beginTransaction();
+    graph.detachEdge(old);
+    graph.attachRegion(wrapper, region);
+    graph.rollbackTransaction(tx);
+    expect(() => graph.attachRegion(wrapper, region)).toThrow("already has an owner");
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(body);
+    graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("f")), body);
+    expect(graph.sealSourcePublication().occurrences.map((o) => o.occurrence)).toEqual([occurrence]);
+  });
+
+  it("detached/truncated regions contribute no selected occurrences", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("f"));
+    const region = graph.createRegion(body);
+    const edge = graph.attachRegion(body, region);
+    graph.recordOccurrence(region, provenance("truncated"));
+    graph.detachEdge(edge);
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(body);
+    graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("f")), body);
+    expect(graph.sealSourcePublication().occurrences).toEqual([]);
+  });
+
+  it("walks cyclic adoption and multiple paths exactly once", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const a = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("a"));
+    const b = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("b"));
+    const oa = graph.recordOccurrence(a, provenance("a"));
+    const ob = graph.recordOccurrence(b, provenance("b"));
+    graph.adoptBody(a, b);
+    graph.adoptBody(a, b);
+    graph.adoptBody(b, a);
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(a);
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(b);
+    graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("a")), a);
+    const result = graph.sealSourcePublication();
+    expect(new Set(result.bodies)).toEqual(new Set([a, b]));
+    expect(result.bodies).toHaveLength(2);
+    expect(new Set(result.occurrences.map((o) => o.occurrence))).toEqual(new Set([oa, ob]));
+    expect(result.occurrences).toHaveLength(2);
+  });
+
+  it("seals once with frozen output and forbids further metadata mutation", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("f"));
+    graph.recordOccurrence(body, provenance("f"));
+    graph.retainBodyEpoch(body);
+    graph.publishBody(graph.createPublication("function", provenance("f")), body);
+    const result = graph.sealSourcePublication();
+    expect(graph.sealSourcePublication()).toBe(result);
+    expect(Object.isFrozen(result)).toBe(true);
+    expect(Object.isFrozen(result.occurrences)).toBe(true);
+    expect(Object.isFrozen(result.occurrences[0])).toBe(true);
+    expect(Object.isFrozen(result.occurrences[0]?.provenance)).toBe(true);
+    expect(() => graph.beginTransaction()).toThrow("sealed");
+    expect(() => graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("late"))).toThrow("sealed");
+    expect(() => graph.discardBodyEpoch(body)).toThrow("sealed");
+  });
+
+  it("isolates contexts even when ids and AST provenance coincide", () => {
+    const a = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const b = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const ast = {};
+    const ba = a.createBodyEpoch(provenance("f", ast));
+    const bb = b.createBodyEpoch(provenance("f", ast));
+    expect(ba.id).toBe(bb.id);
+    expect(ba).not.toBe(bb);
+    expect(() => b.adoptBody(bb, ba)).toThrow("foreign");
+    const tx = a.beginTransaction();
+    expect(() => b.commitTransaction(tx)).toThrow("LIFO");
+    a.rollbackTransaction(tx);
+    a.recordOccurrence(ba, provenance("f", ast));
+    a.retainBodyEpoch(ba);
+    a.publishBody(a.createPublication("function", provenance("f")), ba);
+    expect(a.sealSourcePublication().occurrences).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(b.sealSourcePublication().occurrences).toHaveLength(0);
+  });
+});
