+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+// Initialize the compiler graph before its context adapters.
+import { compile } from "../src/index.js";
+import { analyzeSource } from "../src/checker/index.js";
+import { createEmptyModule, type Instr } from "../src/ir/types.js";
+import { createCodegenContext } from "../src/codegen/context/create-context.js";
+import type { FunctionContext } from "../src/codegen/context/types.js";
+import { ensurePromiseVectorIteratorRuntime } from "../src/codegen/promise-vector-iterator.js";
+
+function registered() {
+  const source = analyzeSource("export function test(): number { return 1; }", "captured-context.ts");
+  const ctx = createCodegenContext(createEmptyModule(), source.checker, { standalone: true, nativeStrings: true });
+  const fctx: FunctionContext = {
+    name: "captured",
+    params: [],
+    locals: [],
+    localMap: new Map(),
+    returnType: { kind: "externref" },
+    body: [],
+    blockDepth: 0,
+    breakStack: [],
+    continueStack: [],
+    labelMap: new Map(),
+    savedBodies: [],
+  };
+  ensurePromiseVectorIteratorRuntime(ctx, fctx);
+  return { ctx, fctx };
+}
+
+function visit(body: Instr[], fn: (instruction: Instr) => void): void {
+  for (const instruction of body) {
+    fn(instruction);
+    const nested = instruction as Instr & {
+      body?: Instr[];
+      then?: Instr[];
+      else?: Instr[];
+      catches?: { body?: Instr[] }[];
+    };
+    for (const children of [nested.body, nested.then, nested.else]) if (children) visit(children, fn);
+    for (const clause of nested.catches ?? []) if (clause.body) visit(clause.body, fn);
+  }
+}
+
+describe("private captured iterator registration", () => {
+  it("reserves exactly three unexported helpers and keeps the record out of JS field enumeration", () => {
+    const { ctx, fctx } = registered();
+    const helpers = ctx.mod.functions.filter((fn) => fn.name.startsWith("__promise_vector_iterator_"));
+    expect(helpers.map((fn) => fn.name)).toEqual([
+      "__promise_vector_iterator_acquire",
+      "__promise_vector_iterator_step",
+      "__promise_vector_iterator_close_throw",
+    ]);
+    expect(helpers.every((fn) => !fn.exported)).toBe(true);
+    expect(ctx.structFields.has("__PromiseVectorIteratorRecord")).toBe(false);
+    const snapshot = structuredClone(ctx.mod);
+    const bodies = helpers.map((fn) => fn.body);
+    ensurePromiseVectorIteratorRuntime(ctx, fctx);
+    expect(ctx.mod).toEqual(snapshot);
+    helpers.forEach((fn, i) => expect(fn.body).toBe(bodies[i]));
+    const record = ctx.mod.types[ctx.structMap.get("__PromiseVectorIteratorRecord")!];
+    expect(record).toMatchObject({
+      kind: "struct",
+      fields: [
+        { name: "iterator", mutable: false },
+        { name: "nextMethod", mutable: false },
+        { name: "done", mutable: true },
+      ],
+    });
+  });
+
+  it("has no shared instruction identities, generic iterator dispatch, or catch-all", () => {
+    const { ctx } = registered();
+    const seen = new Set<Instr>();
+    const forbidden = ["__iterator", "__iterator_next", "__iterator_rest"].map((name) => ctx.funcMap.get(name));
+    let catches = 0;
+    for (const fn of ctx.mod.functions.filter((fn) => fn.name.startsWith("__promise_vector_iterator_"))) {
+      visit(fn.body, (instruction) => {
+        expect(seen.has(instruction)).toBe(false);
+        seen.add(instruction);
+        if (instruction.op === "call") expect(forbidden).not.toContain(instruction.funcIdx);
+        if (instruction.op === "try_table") {
+          catches++;
+          expect(instruction.catches).toHaveLength(1);
+          expect(instruction.catches[0].kind).toBe("catch");
+          expect(instruction.catches[0].tagIdx).toBeDefined();
+        }
+        expect(instruction).not.toHaveProperty("catchAll");
+      });
+    }
+    expect(catches).toBe(2);
+  });
+});
+
+// These use the REAL production all/race entry point. They intentionally need
+// B's property facade and D's opt-in wiring; no synthetic caller/export keeps
+// the new runtime alive. Parent owns execution after those lanes are integrated.
+async function run(body: string, method: "all" | "race"): Promise<number> {
+  const result = await compile(
+    `declare function __drain_microtasks(): void;
+export function test(): number {
+  const a: any[] = [1, 2];
+  let reason: any = null;
+  let settled = 0;
+  let answer = 0;
+  (Promise as any).resolve = function(v: any): any { return {then: function(ok: any): void {ok(v);}}; };
+  ${body.replaceAll("COMBINE", `Promise.${method}`)}
+}`,
+    { fileName: `captured-${method}.ts`, target: "standalone", nativeStrings: true, optimize: 0, emitWat: true },
+  );
+  expect(result.success, JSON.stringify(result.errors)).toBe(true);
+  if (!result.success) throw new Error("captured iterator source failed compilation");
+  expect(result.wat).toContain("__promise_vector_iterator_acquire");
+  const module = await WebAssembly.compile(result.binary);
+  expect(WebAssembly.Module.imports(module)).toEqual([]);
+  expect(WebAssembly.Module.exports(module).some((entry) => entry.name.startsWith("__promise_vector_iterator_"))).toBe(
+    false,
+  );
+  const instance = await WebAssembly.instantiate(module, {});
+  return (instance.exports.test as () => number)();
+}
+
+for (const method of ["all", "race"] as const) {
+  describe(`captured source vector Promise.${method}`, () => {
+    for (const invalid of ["null", "undefined", "7", "{}"]) {
+      it(`rejects ${invalid} iterator method without intrinsic fallback`, async () => {
+        expect(
+          await run(
+            `
+          let reads=0;
+          Object.defineProperty(a,Symbol.iterator,{get:function():any {reads++;return ${invalid};}});
+          COMBINE(a).then(function():void {settled++;},function(e:any):void {reason=e;});
+          __drain_microtasks();
+          return reads===1 && settled===0 && reason instanceof TypeError ? 1 : 0;
+        `,
+            method,
+          ),
+        ).toBe(1);
+      });
+    }
+
+    it("propagates the identical throwing iterator getter reason once", async () => {
+      expect(
+        await run(
+          `
+        const original:any={error:1}; let reads=0;
+        Object.defineProperty(a,Symbol.iterator,{get:function():any {reads++;throw original;}});
+        COMBINE(a).then(function():void {settled++;},function(e:any):void {reason=e;});
+        __drain_microtasks();
+        return reads===1 && settled===0 && reason===original ? 1 : 0;
+      `,
+          method,
+        ),
+      ).toBe(1);
+    });
+
+    it("accepts callable iterator/results and ToBoolean(done) skips value", async () => {
+      expect(
+        await run(
+          `
+        let polls=0; let values=0; let closes=0;
+        const iterator:any=function():void {};
+        iterator.next=function():any {
+          polls++;
+          const result:any=function():void {};
+          Object.defineProperty(result,"done",{get:function():any {return polls===1 ? 0 : {};}});
+          Object.defineProperty(result,"value",{get:function():any {values++;if(polls>1)throw 99;return 9;}});
+          return result;
+        };
+        Object.defineProperty(iterator,"return",{get:function():any {closes++;throw 98;}});
+        (a as any)[Symbol.iterator]=function():any {return iterator;};
+        COMBINE(a).then(function(v:any):void {settled++;answer=${method === "all" ? "v[0]" : "v"};},function(e:any):void {reason=e;});
+        __drain_microtasks();
+        return polls===2 && values===1 && closes===0 && settled===1 && answer===9 && reason===null ? 1 : 0;
+      `,
+          method,
+        ),
+      ).toBe(1);
+    });
+
+    it("captures next once, preserves both receivers and zero arguments, skips terminal value", async () => {
+      expect(
+        await run(
+          `
+        let reads = 0; let polls = 0; let bad = 0; let values = 0; let gets = 0;
+        const iterator: any = {};
+        Object.defineProperty(iterator, "next", {configurable:true, get:function():any {
+          reads++;
+          if(this!==iterator) bad++;
+          return function():any {
+            if(this!==iterator || arguments.length!==0) bad++;
+            polls++;
+            Object.defineProperty(iterator,"next",{configurable:true,get:function():any {throw 99;}});
+            const result:any = {};
+            Object.defineProperty(result,"done",{get:function():any {gets++; return polls>2;}});
+            Object.defineProperty(result,"value",{get:function():any {values++; if(polls>2) throw 98; return polls+8;}});
+            return result;
+          };
+        }});
+        (a as any)[Symbol.iterator]=function():any {
+          if(this!==a || arguments.length!==0) bad++;
+          return iterator;
+        };
+        COMBINE(a).then(function(v:any):void {settled++; answer=${method === "all" ? "v[0]*10+v[1]" : "v"};},function(e:any):void {reason=e;});
+        __drain_microtasks();
+        return reads===1 && polls===3 && gets===3 && values===2 && bad===0 && settled===1 && reason===null && answer===${method === "all" ? 100 : 9} ? 1 : 0;
+      `,
+          method,
+        ),
+      ).toBe(1);
+    });
+
+    for (const primitive of ["null", "undefined", "7", "'x'", "true", "Symbol('x')"]) {
+      it(`rejects primitive iterator ${primitive} before next/close`, async () => {
+        expect(
+          await run(
+            `
+          let calls=0;
+          (a as any)[Symbol.iterator]=function():any {calls++;return ${primitive};};
+          COMBINE(a).then(function():void {settled++;},function(e:any):void {reason=e;});
+          __drain_microtasks();
+          return calls===1 && settled===0 && reason instanceof TypeError ? 1 : 0;
+        `,
+            method,
+          ),
+        ).toBe(1);
+      });
+    }
+
+    for (const failure of [
+      "next-get",
+      "noncallable-next",
+      "next-call",
+      "primitive-result",
+      "done-get",
+      "value-get",
+    ] as const) {
+      it(`does not close after ${failure}`, async () => {
+        const getter =
+          failure === "next-get"
+            ? "throw original;"
+            : failure === "noncallable-next"
+              ? "return 7;"
+              : `return function():any {
+          calls++;
+          ${
+            failure === "next-call"
+              ? "throw original;"
+              : failure === "primitive-result"
+                ? "return 1;"
+                : `
+          const result:any={};
+          Object.defineProperty(result,"done",{get:function():any {${failure === "done-get" ? "throw original;" : "return false;"}}});
+          Object.defineProperty(result,"value",{get:function():any {throw original;}});
+          return result;`
+          }
+        };`;
+        expect(
+          await run(
+            `
+          const original:any={error:1}; let closes=0; let calls=0; let reads=0;
+          const iterator:any={};
+          Object.defineProperty(iterator,"next",{get:function():any {reads++;${getter}}});
+          Object.defineProperty(iterator,"return",{get:function():any {closes++;throw 99;}});
+          (a as any)[Symbol.iterator]=function():any {return iterator;};
+          COMBINE(a).then(function():void {settled++;},function(e:any):void {reason=e;});
+          __drain_microtasks();
+          return reads===1 && calls===${failure === "next-get" || failure === "noncallable-next" ? 0 : 1} && closes===0 && settled===0 && ${failure === "noncallable-next" || failure === "primitive-result" ? "reason instanceof TypeError" : "reason===original"} ? 1 : 0;
+        `,
+            method,
+          ),
+        ).toBe(1);
+      });
+    }
+
+    for (const close of [
+      "absent",
+      "null",
+      "undefined",
+      "noncallable",
+      "getter-throw",
+      "call-throw",
+      "primitive",
+      "object",
+    ] as const) {
+      it(`keeps the original throw with ${close} return and closes before rejection`, async () => {
+        const returned =
+          close === "null"
+            ? "null"
+            : close === "undefined"
+              ? "undefined"
+              : close === "noncallable"
+                ? "7"
+                : `function():any {
+          calls++; trace=trace*10+3;
+          if(this!==iterator || arguments.length!==0) bad++;
+          ${
+            close === "call-throw"
+              ? "throw other;"
+              : close === "primitive"
+                ? "return 1;"
+                : `const result:any={};
+          Object.defineProperty(result,"done",{get:function():any {bad++;throw other;}});
+          Object.defineProperty(result,"value",{get:function():any {bad++;throw other;}});
+          return result;`
+          }
+        }`;
+        const hasCall = ["call-throw", "primitive", "object"].includes(close);
+        expect(
+          await run(
+            `
+          const original:any={error:1}; const other:any={error:2};
+          let reads=0; let calls=0; let polls=0; let bad=0; let trace=0;
+          const iterator:any={next:function():any {polls++;return {done:false,value:9};}};
+          (a as any)[Symbol.iterator]=function():any {return iterator;};
+          (Promise as any).resolve=function():any {
+            trace=trace*10+1;
+            ${
+              close === "absent"
+                ? ""
+                : `Object.defineProperty(iterator,"return",{get:function():any {
+              reads++; trace=trace*10+2; if(this!==iterator) bad++;
+              ${close === "getter-throw" ? "throw other;" : `return ${returned};`}
+            }});`
+            }
+            throw original;
+          };
+          COMBINE(a).then(function():void {settled++;},function(e:any):void {trace=trace*10+4;reason=e;});
+          __drain_microtasks();
+          return polls===1 && reads===${close === "absent" ? 0 : 1} && calls===${hasCall ? 1 : 0} && bad===0 && settled===0 && reason===original && trace===${close === "absent" ? 14 : hasCall ? 1234 : 124} ? 1 : 0;
+        `,
+            method,
+          ),
+        ).toBe(1);
+      });
+    }
+  });
+}

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { beforeEach, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import type { ValType } from "../src/ir/types.js";
+import type { ts } from "../src/ts-api.js";
+import type { CodegenContext, FunctionContext } from "../src/codegen/context/types.js";
+
+const calls = vi.hoisted(() => ({ compile: vi.fn(), prepare: vi.fn(), order: [] as string[] }));
+vi.mock("../src/codegen/shared.js", () => ({ compileExpression: calls.compile }));
+vi.mock("../src/codegen/bigint-field-carrier.js", () => ({ prepareClosedFieldValue: calls.prepare }));
+import { compileReferenceStorageOperand } from "../src/codegen/reference-storage-operand.js";
+
+const value = {} as ts.Expression;
+const frame = {} as FunctionContext;
+function context(type = "bigint", standalone = true, nativeStrings = true) {
+  return { standalone, nativeStrings, oracle: { staticJsTypeOf: vi.fn(() => type) } } as unknown as CodegenContext;
+}
+beforeEach(() => {
+  calls.order.length = 0;
+  calls.prepare.mockReset().mockImplementation(() => calls.order.push("prepare"));
+  calls.compile.mockReset().mockImplementation(() => {
+    calls.order.push("compile");
+    return { kind: "externref" };
+  });
+});
+
+it("prepares a proven native BigInt before its sole evaluation", () => {
+  const ctx = context();
+  expect(compileReferenceStorageOperand(ctx, frame, value)).toEqual({ kind: "externref" });
+  expect(calls.order).toEqual(["prepare", "compile"]);
+  expect(calls.compile).toHaveBeenCalledExactlyOnceWith(ctx, frame, value, { kind: "externref" });
+  expect(calls.prepare).toHaveBeenCalledExactlyOnceWith(ctx, frame, value, { kind: "externref" });
+});
+
+it("retains the caller's existing reference hint", () => {
+  const hint: ValType = { kind: "externref" };
+  compileReferenceStorageOperand(context(), frame, value, hint);
+  expect(calls.compile.mock.calls[0]?.[3]).toBe(hint);
+});
+
+it.each(["number", "mixed", "string"])("preserves the original absent hint for %s", (type) => {
+  const ctx = context(type);
+  compileReferenceStorageOperand(ctx, frame, value);
+  expect(calls.prepare).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  expect(calls.compile).toHaveBeenCalledExactlyOnceWith(ctx, frame, value, undefined);
+});
+
+it.each([
+  [false, true],
+  [true, false],
+])("preserves target gating %s/%s", (standalone, nativeStrings) => {
+  compileReferenceStorageOperand(context("bigint", standalone, nativeStrings), frame, value);
+  expect(calls.prepare).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  expect(calls.compile.mock.calls[0]?.[3]).toBeUndefined();
+});
+
+it("does not override an explicit native scalar ABI", () => {
+  const hint: ValType = { kind: "i64" };
+  compileReferenceStorageOperand(context(), frame, value, hint);
+  expect(calls.prepare).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  expect(calls.compile.mock.calls[0]?.[3]).toBe(hint);
+});
+
+it("does not reconstruct an operand producer's already narrowed result", () => {
+  const result: ValType = { kind: "i64", bigint: true };
+  calls.compile.mockReturnValue(result);
+  expect(compileReferenceStorageOperand(context(), frame, value)).toBe(result);
+  expect(calls.compile).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+});
+
+it("propagates failed compilation without a substitute value", () => {
+  calls.compile.mockReturnValue(null);
+  expect(compileReferenceStorageOperand(context(), frame, value)).toBeNull();
+  expect(calls.compile).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+});

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import type { Instr } from "../src/ir/types.js";
+import { builtinBrandOffsetOf } from "../src/codegen/builtin-brands.js";
+import { buildReferenceWrapperBrandArms } from "../src/codegen/reference-wrapper-brand.js";
+
+// Independent literal form of the previous inlined arms. Pin full instruction
+// order and missing-provider behavior, not only result length or helper calls.
+function original(symbol: number, bigint: number): Instr[] {
+  const arm = (typeIdx: number, brand: "Symbol" | "BigInt"): Instr[] => [
+    { op: "local.get", index: 17 },
+    { op: "ref.test", typeIdx },
+    {
+      op: "if",
+      blockType: { kind: "empty" },
+      then: [{ op: "i32.const", value: builtinBrandOffsetOf(brand)! }, { op: "return" }],
+    },
+  ];
+  return [...(symbol >= 0 ? arm(symbol, "Symbol") : []), ...(bigint >= 0 ? arm(bigint, "BigInt") : [])];
+}
+
+it.each([
+  [-1, -1],
+  [0, -1],
+  [-1, 0],
+  [7, 19],
+])("preserves exact original arms for Symbol=%s BigInt=%s", (symbolTypeIdx, nativeBigIntTypeIdx) => {
+  const ctx = Object.freeze({ symbolTypeIdx, nativeBigIntTypeIdx });
+  const slot = vi.fn((): Instr[] => [{ op: "local.get", index: 17 }]);
+  expect(buildReferenceWrapperBrandArms(ctx, slot)).toEqual(original(symbolTypeIdx, nativeBigIntTypeIdx));
+  expect(slot).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(Number(symbolTypeIdx >= 0) + Number(nativeBigIntTypeIdx >= 0));
+});
+
+it("never shares emitted instructions across builds or the two arms", () => {
+  const ctx = { symbolTypeIdx: 7, nativeBigIntTypeIdx: 19 };
+  const build = () => buildReferenceWrapperBrandArms(ctx, (): Instr[] => [{ op: "local.get", index: 17 }]);
+  const first = build();
+  const second = build();
+  expect(first).toEqual(second);
+  for (let i = 0; i < first.length; i++) expect(first[i]).not.toBe(second[i]);
+  expect(first[0]).not.toBe(first[3]);
+  expect(first[2]).not.toBe(first[5]);
+});

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
+import { runInNewContext } from "node:vm";
+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { compile } from "../src/index.js";
+
+// Open/dynamic routes are deliberate controls, not evidence of closed-field
+// layout preservation. The two closed-field cases remain in this population.
+const cases: [string, string][] = [
+  [
+    "dynamic dot write",
+    'function put(o){o.v=18446744073709551617n;return String(o.v);} function test(){return put({})==="18446744073709551617"?1:-1;}',
+  ],
+  [
+    "dynamic computed write",
+    'function put(o,k){o[k]=-18446744073709551617n;return String(o[k]);} function test(){return put({},"v")==="-18446744073709551617"?1:-1;}',
+  ],
+  [
+    "Reflect.set value",
+    'function test(){const o={};const ok=Reflect.set(o,"v",18446744073709551617n);return ok&&String(Reflect.get(o,"v"))==="18446744073709551617"?1:-1;}',
+  ],
+  [
+    "Reflect.set assignment trace",
+    'function test(){let trace=0;const o={};function receiver(){trace=trace*10+1;return o;}function key(){trace=trace*10+2;return "v";}const ok=Reflect.set(receiver(),key(),(trace=trace*10+3,18446744073709551617n));return ok&&trace===123&&String(Reflect.get(o,"v"))==="18446744073709551617"?1:-1;}',
+  ],
+  [
+    "closed field direct write",
+    'function test(){const o={v:0n};o.v=18446744073709551617n;return String(o.v)==="18446744073709551617"?1:-1;}',
+  ],
+  [
+    "closed descriptor value",
+    'function test(){const o={};Reflect.defineProperty(o,"v",{value:18446744073709551617n});return String(Reflect.get(o,"v"))==="18446744073709551617"?1:-1;}',
+  ],
+  ["dynamic number control", "function put(o){o.v=1.5;return o.v;} function test(){return put({})===1.5?1:-1;}"],
+  [
+    "throwing receiver stops later arguments",
+    'function test(){let count=0;const reason={};function receiver(){throw reason;}try{Reflect.set(receiver(),(count++,"v"),18446744073709551617n);return -1;}catch(e){return e===reason&&count===0?1:-2;}}',
+  ],
+];
+
+for (const [name, program] of cases) {
+  it(name, async () => {
+    const source = `${program}\nexport { test };`;
+    const native = runInNewContext(`${program}\ntest();`, {}, { timeout: 5000 });
+    const sha256 = createHash("sha256").update(source).digest("hex");
+    const result = await compile(source, {
+      fileName: "5883-reference-write.js",
+      allowJs: true,
+      skipSemanticDiagnostics: true,
+      target: "standalone",
+      nativeStrings: true,
+    });
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({ name, source, sha256, native, success: result.success, errors: result.errors }));
+    expect(native).toBe(1);
+    expect(result.success, JSON.stringify(result.errors)).toBe(true);
+    const module = new WebAssembly.Module(result.binary);
+    const imports = WebAssembly.Module.imports(module);
+    expect(imports).toEqual([]);
+    const instance = new WebAssembly.Instance(module, {});
+    const actual = (instance.exports.test as () => number)();
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({ name, sha256, native, actual, imports }));
+    expect(actual).toBe(1);
+  });
+}

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
+import { runInNewContext } from "node:vm";
+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { compile } from "../src/index.js";
+
+const cases = [
+  [
+    "boxes primitive receivers once before deferred property reads",
+    `
+    let receiver;
+    Object.defineProperty(Number.prototype, "length", {
+      configurable: true,
+      get: function () { "use strict"; receiver = this; return 1; }
+    });
+    Object.defineProperty(Number.prototype, "0", {
+      configurable: true,
+      get: function () { "use strict"; return this === receiver && typeof this === "object" ? 7 : -9; }
+    });
+    const iterator = Array.prototype.values.call(3);
+    if (receiver !== undefined) return -1;
+    const first = iterator.next();
+    return !first.done && first.value === 7 ? 1 : -2;
+  `,
+  ],
+  [
+    "does not read length or indices at creation",
+    `
+    let reads = 0;
+    const source = { get length() { reads++; return 1; }, get 0() { reads += 10; return 7; } };
+    const values = Array.prototype.values;
+    const iterator = values.call(source);
+    if (reads !== 0) return -1;
+    const first = iterator.next();
+    if (first.done || first.value !== 7 || reads !== 11) return -2;
+    const end = iterator.next();
+    return end.done && end.value === undefined && reads === 12 ? 1 : -3;
+  `,
+  ],
+  [
+    "observes replacement and growth on the original receiver",
+    `
+    const source = { 0: 1, 1: 2, 2: 3, length: 2 };
+    const values = Array.prototype.values;
+    const iterator = values.call(source);
+    if (iterator.next().value !== 1) return -1;
+    source[1] = 7;
+    source.length = 3;
+    if (iterator.next().value !== 7 || iterator.next().value !== 3) return -2;
+    return iterator.next().done ? 1 : -3;
+  `,
+  ],
+  [
+    "does not revive an exhausted cursor after regrowth",
+    `
+    let reads = 0;
+    let length = 1;
+    const source = { 0: 1, 1: 2, get length() { reads++; return length; } };
+    const iterator = Array.prototype.values.call(source);
+    if (iterator.next().value !== 1 || !iterator.next().done || reads !== 2) return -1;
+    length = 2;
+    const again = iterator.next();
+    return again.done && again.value === undefined && reads === 2 ? 1 : -2;
+  `,
+  ],
+  [
+    "defers a throwing length getter until next",
+    `
+    const reason = {};
+    const source = { get length() { throw reason; } };
+    let iterator;
+    try { iterator = Array.prototype.values.call(source); } catch (_) { return -1; }
+    try { iterator.next(); return -2; } catch (error) { return error === reason ? 1 : -3; }
+  `,
+  ],
+  [
+    "retains nullish receiver rejection",
+    `
+    const values = Array.prototype.values;
+    let caught = 0;
+    try { values.call(null); } catch (error) { if (error instanceof TypeError) caught++; }
+    try { values.call(undefined); } catch (error) { if (error instanceof TypeError) caught++; }
+    return caught === 2 ? 1 : -1;
+  `,
+  ],
+] as const;
+
+for (const [name, body] of cases) {
+  it(name, async () => {
+    const source = `export function test() { ${body} }`;
+    const native = runInNewContext(`(function () { ${body} })()`, {}, { timeout: 5000 });
+    // Record both implementations before the reference canary asserts. Node
+    // 22 rereads length after exhaustion; its failure must not hide Wasm's
+    // result. Neither source nor either expectation is relaxed here.
+    const result = await compile(source, {
+      fileName: "5883-reflective-live-values.js",
+      allowJs: true,
+      skipSemanticDiagnostics: true,
+      target: "standalone",
+      nativeStrings: true,
+    });
+    expect(result.success, JSON.stringify(result.errors)).toBe(true);
+    const module = new WebAssembly.Module(result.binary);
+    expect(WebAssembly.Module.imports(module)).toEqual([]);
+    const instance = new WebAssembly.Instance(module, {});
+    const actual = (instance.exports.test as () => number)();
+    console.log(
+      JSON.stringify({ name, source, sha256: createHash("sha256").update(source).digest("hex"), native, actual }),
+    );
+    expect(native).toBe(1);
+    expect(actual).toBe(native);
+  });
+}

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
+import { runInNewContext } from "node:vm";
+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { compile } from "../src/index.js";
+
+// Actual reflective VALUES factory only: no registration mocks, helper exports,
+// or direct native-helper calls. Each source runs in its own native/Wasm realm.
+// No next() call follows a done result; the Node exhaustion canary stays in
+// issue-5883-reflective-live-values.test.ts with its original expectation.
+const cases: [string, string][] = [];
+
+for (const [name, ctor, expression] of [
+  ["number", "Number", "3"],
+  ["negative zero", "Number", "-0"],
+  ["genuine NaN", "Number", "NaN"],
+  ["boolean false", "Boolean", "false"],
+  ["bigint", "BigInt", "7n"],
+  ["wide bigint", "BigInt", "18446744073709551617n"],
+  ["symbol", "Symbol", 'Symbol("receiver")'],
+] as const) {
+  cases.push([
+    `wraps ${name} once and preserves its primitive slot`,
+    `
+    const primitive = ${expression};
+    let receiver;
+    let lengths = 0;
+    let indices = 0;
+    Object.defineProperty(${ctor}.prototype, "length", {
+      configurable: true,
+      get: function () {
+        "use strict";
+        lengths++;
+        if (receiver === undefined) receiver = this;
+        return this === receiver && typeof this === "object" ? 1 : 0;
+      }
+    });
+    Object.defineProperty(${ctor}.prototype, "0", {
+      configurable: true,
+      get: function () {
+        "use strict";
+        indices++;
+        return this === receiver && typeof this === "object" &&
+          Object.is(${ctor}.prototype.valueOf.call(this), primitive) ? 7 : -9;
+      }
+    });
+    const iterator = Array.prototype.values.call(primitive);
+    if (lengths !== 0 || indices !== 0 || receiver !== undefined) return -1;
+    const first = iterator.next();
+    if (first.done || first.value !== 7 || lengths !== 1 || indices !== 1) return -2;
+    const end = iterator.next();
+    return end.done && end.value === undefined && lengths === 2 && indices === 1 ? 1 : -3;
+  `,
+  ]);
+}
+
+cases.push([
+  "string primitive yields UTF-16 indexed values, not code points",
+  `
+  const iterator = Array.prototype.values.call("A\\uD83D\\uDE00");
+  const a = iterator.next();
+  const hi = iterator.next();
+  const lo = iterator.next();
+  const end = iterator.next();
+  return !a.done && a.value === "A" && !hi.done && hi.value === "\\uD83D" &&
+    !lo.done && lo.value === "\\uDE00" && end.done && end.value === undefined ? 1 : -1;
+`,
+]);
+
+for (const [ctor, expression] of [
+  ["Number", "3"],
+  ["Boolean", "false"],
+  ["BigInt", "7n"],
+  ["Symbol", 'Symbol("boxed")'],
+] as const) {
+  cases.push([
+    `retains existing ${ctor} wrapper identity`,
+    `
+    const primitive = ${expression};
+    const source = Object(primitive);
+    let reads = 0;
+    Object.defineProperty(source, "length", {
+      configurable: true,
+      get: function () { "use strict"; reads++; return this === source ? 1 : 0; }
+    });
+    Object.defineProperty(source, "0", {
+      configurable: true,
+      get: function () {
+        "use strict";
+        reads += 10;
+        return this === source && Object.is(${ctor}.prototype.valueOf.call(this), primitive) ? 7 : -9;
+      }
+    });
+    const iterator = Array.prototype.values.call(source);
+    if (reads !== 0) return -1;
+    const first = iterator.next();
+    return !first.done && first.value === 7 && reads === 11 ? 1 : -2;
+  `,
+  ]);
+}
+
+// A String wrapper's own length/index descriptors cannot be replaced by
+// observing getters. A transparent Proxy observes the actual Get receiver
+// while retaining the original raw String wrapper as its target.
+cases.push([
+  "retains a String wrapper target and its explicit proxy receiver",
+  `
+  const boxed = Object("ab");
+  let reads = 0;
+  let wrong = 0;
+  const source = new Proxy(boxed, {
+    get: function (target, key, receiver) {
+      if (target !== boxed || receiver !== source) wrong++;
+      if (key === "length" || key === "0" || key === "1") reads++;
+      return Reflect.get(target, key, receiver);
+    }
+  });
+  const iterator = Array.prototype.values.call(source);
+  if (reads !== 0 || wrong !== 0) return -1;
+  const first = iterator.next();
+  const second = iterator.next();
+  return !first.done && first.value === "a" && !second.done && second.value === "b" &&
+    reads === 4 && wrong === 0 ? 1 : -2;
+`,
+]);
+
+for (const [name, expression] of [
+  ["ordinary object", "{}"],
+  ["array", "[0]"],
+  ["function", "function () {}"],
+] as const) {
+  cases.push([
+    `preserves ${name} receiver identity`,
+    `
+    const source = ${expression};
+    let reads = 0;
+    ${name === "array" ? "" : 'Object.defineProperty(source, "length", { configurable: true, value: 1 });'}
+    Object.defineProperty(source, "0", {
+      configurable: true,
+      get: function () { "use strict"; reads++; return this === source ? 7 : -9; }
+    });
+    const iterator = Array.prototype.values.call(source);
+    if (reads !== 0) return -1;
+    const first = iterator.next();
+    return !first.done && first.value === 7 && reads === 1 ? 1 : -2;
+  `,
+  ]);
+}
+
+cases.push([
+  "does not Get or coerce an object receiver at factory creation",
+  `
+  let reads = 0;
+  let conversions = 0;
+  const reason = {};
+  const source = {
+    get length() { reads++; return 1; },
+    get 0() { reads += 10; return this === source ? 7 : -9; },
+    valueOf: function () { conversions++; throw reason; },
+    toString: function () { conversions++; throw reason; },
+    [Symbol.toPrimitive]: function () { conversions++; throw reason; }
+  };
+  let iterator;
+  try { iterator = Array.prototype.values.call(source); } catch (_) { return -1; }
+  if (reads !== 0 || conversions !== 0) return -2;
+  const first = iterator.next();
+  return !first.done && first.value === 7 && reads === 11 && conversions === 0 ? 1 : -3;
+`,
+]);
+
+cases.push([
+  "allocates fresh primitive wrappers per factory call and retains each across steps",
+  `
+  let firstReceiver;
+  let secondReceiver;
+  let current;
+  let lengths = 0;
+  Object.defineProperty(Number.prototype, "length", {
+    configurable: true,
+    get: function () {
+      "use strict";
+      lengths++;
+      current = this;
+      if (lengths === 1) firstReceiver = this;
+      if (lengths === 2) secondReceiver = this;
+      return 2;
+    }
+  });
+  const getter = function () {
+    "use strict";
+    if (this !== current || typeof this !== "object") return -9;
+    return this === firstReceiver ? 7 : this === secondReceiver ? 8 : -8;
+  };
+  Object.defineProperty(Number.prototype, "0", { configurable: true, get: getter });
+  Object.defineProperty(Number.prototype, "1", { configurable: true, get: getter });
+  const values = Array.prototype.values;
+  const first = values.call(3);
+  const second = values.call(3);
+  if (lengths !== 0) return -1;
+  if (first.next().value !== 7 || second.next().value !== 8) return -2;
+  if (firstReceiver === secondReceiver) return -3;
+  const again = first.next();
+  return !again.done && again.value === 7 && current === firstReceiver && lengths === 3 ? 1 : -4;
+`,
+]);
+
+for (const expression of ["null", "undefined"]) {
+  cases.push([
+    `rejects ${expression} at factory creation with TypeError`,
+    `
+    try {
+      Array.prototype.values.call(${expression});
+      return -1;
+    } catch (error) {
+      return error instanceof TypeError ? 1 : -2;
+    }
+  `,
+  ]);
+}
+
+for (const [name, body] of cases) {
+  it(name, async () => {
+    const source = `export function test() { ${body} }`;
+    const sha256 = createHash("sha256").update(source).digest("hex");
+    const native = runInNewContext(`(function () { ${body} })()`, {}, { timeout: 5000 });
+    const result = await compile(source, {
+      fileName: "5883-runtime-to-object.js",
+      allowJs: true,
+      skipSemanticDiagnostics: true,
+      target: "standalone",
+      nativeStrings: true,
+    });
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({ name, source, sha256, native, success: result.success, errors: result.errors }));
+    expect(result.success, JSON.stringify(result.errors)).toBe(true);
+    const module = new WebAssembly.Module(result.binary);
+    const imports = WebAssembly.Module.imports(module);
+    expect(imports).toEqual([]);
+    const instance = new WebAssembly.Instance(module, {});
+    const actual = (instance.exports.test as () => number)();
+    // Record BOTH values before either reference or Wasm assertion.
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({ name, source, sha256, native, actual, imports }));
+    expect(native).toBe(1);
+    expect(actual).toBe(1);
+  });
+}

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+// Isolated physical/ledger editor controls. Authored only; not executed.
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { EmissionOwnership, type BodyEpoch } from "../src/codegen/context/emission-ownership.js";
+import { StagingBodyEditor } from "../src/codegen/staging-edit.js";
+import { inspectStageBody, type StagePlacement } from "../src/codegen/staging-census.js";
+import { stageContainer, stageLeaf, stageRegion } from "../src/codegen/staging-instr.js";
+
+const provenance = (unitId: string) => ({ sourceId: "editor.ts", unitId });
+const empty = { kind: "empty" as const };
+function setup(graph = new EmissionOwnership(), adopted?: BodyEpoch) {
+  const identity = { bodyIdentity: {}, bindingEnvironment: {} };
+  const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("body"), identity);
+  const token = graph.beginContentTransaction(body);
+  const root = graph.createRegion(body);
+  graph.attachRegion(body, root);
+  function leaf(index: number) {
+    const nodeIdentity = {};
+    const kind = "protocol-choice" as const;
+    const occurrence = graph.recordOccurrence(root, provenance(`leaf-${index}`), { nodeIdentity, kind });
+    return stageLeaf({ op: "local.get", index }, { nodeIdentity, occurrence: { occurrence, kind } });
+  }
+  let x = leaf(0);
+  const y = leaf(1);
+  if (adopted) {
+    const useSiteIdentity = {};
+    const edge = graph.adoptBody(root, adopted, useSiteIdentity);
+    x = stageLeaf(x.instruction, { ...x, adoptions: [{ edge, body: adopted, useSiteIdentity }] });
+  }
+  const staged = { body, ...identity, root: stageRegion(body, root, [x, y]) };
+  // Expected physical layout is assembled from the intended construction, not
+  // from inspectStageBody's result or the ledger's retained edge enumeration.
+  const expected: StagePlacement[] = [
+    { region: root, parent: { kind: "body", body }, nodeIdentities: [x.nodeIdentity, y.nodeIdentity] },
+  ];
+  const editor = new StagingBodyEditor(graph, staged, token, expected);
+  return { graph, body, token, root, x, y, staged, expected, editor };
+}
+
+describe("paired immutable staging edits", () => {
+  it("removes one physical leaf and its occurrence before authenticated completion", () => {
+    const f = setup();
+    const before = f.editor.snapshot();
+    f.editor.removeLeaf(f.x.nodeIdentity);
+    expect(before.root.nodes).toEqual([f.x, f.y]);
+    expect(f.editor.snapshot().root.nodes).toEqual([f.y]);
+    const done = f.editor.complete();
+    const manifest = inspectStageBody(done.body).manifest;
+    const publication = f.graph.createPublication("function", provenance("root"));
+    f.graph.publishBody(publication, f.body);
+    const result = f.graph.sealWithManifest({
+      publications: [{ publication, receipt: done.receipt }],
+      bodies: [manifest],
+    });
+    expect(result.ownership.occurrences.map((o) => o.occurrence)).toEqual([f.y.occurrence!.occurrence]);
+  });
+
+  it("rollback restores the exact physical root and occurrence membership", () => {
+    const f = setup();
+    const before = f.editor.snapshot();
+    const membership = f.graph.occurrenceMembership(f.x.occurrence!.occurrence);
+    const token = f.editor.beginEdit();
+    f.editor.removeLeaf(f.x.nodeIdentity);
+    f.editor.rollbackEdit(token);
+    expect(f.editor.snapshot()).toBe(before);
+    expect(f.graph.occurrenceMembership(f.x.occurrence!.occurrence)).toBe(membership);
+    expect(inspectStageBody(f.editor.complete().body).manifest.occurrences).toHaveLength(2);
+  });
+
+  it("same-body child commit remains cancellable by its editor parent", () => {
+    const f = setup();
+    const before = f.editor.snapshot();
+    const outer = f.editor.beginEdit();
+    const inner = f.editor.beginEdit();
+    f.editor.removeLeaf(f.x.nodeIdentity);
+    f.editor.commitEdit(inner);
+    expect(f.editor.snapshot().root.nodes).toEqual([f.y]);
+    f.editor.rollbackEdit(outer);
+    expect(f.editor.snapshot()).toBe(before);
+    expect(inspectStageBody(f.editor.complete().body).manifest.occurrences).toHaveLength(2);
+  });
+
+  it("reorders physical siblings and expected order without changing ledger memberships", () => {
+    const f = setup();
+    f.editor.reorderSequence(f.root, [f.y.nodeIdentity, f.x.nodeIdentity]);
+    expect(f.editor.snapshot().root.nodes).toEqual([f.y, f.x]);
+    expect(f.editor.expectedLayout()[0]?.nodeIdentities).toEqual([f.y.nodeIdentity, f.x.nodeIdentity]);
+    expect(inspectStageBody(f.editor.complete().body).manifest.occurrences.map((o) => o.occurrence)).toEqual([
+      f.y.occurrence!.occurrence,
+      f.x.occurrence!.occurrence,
+    ]);
+  });
+
+  it("a reordered physical version is discarded by local rollback", () => {
+    const f = setup();
+    const before = f.editor.snapshot();
+    const expected = f.editor.expectedLayout();
+    const token = f.editor.beginEdit();
+    f.editor.reorderSequence(f.root, [f.y.nodeIdentity, f.x.nodeIdentity]);
+    f.editor.rollbackEdit(token);
+    expect(f.editor.snapshot()).toBe(before);
+    expect(f.editor.expectedLayout()).toBe(expected);
+    f.editor.complete();
+  });
+
+  it("updates descendant expected preorder when containers exchange sibling positions", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const identity = { bodyIdentity: {}, bindingEnvironment: {} };
+    const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("containers"), identity);
+    const token = graph.beginContentTransaction(body);
+    const root = graph.createRegion(body),
+      a = graph.createRegion(body),
+      b = graph.createRegion(body);
+    graph.attachRegion(body, root);
+    graph.attachRegion(root, a);
+    graph.attachRegion(root, b);
+    const left = stageContainer(
+      { op: "block", blockType: empty, body: stageRegion(body, a, []) },
+      { nodeIdentity: {} },
+    );
+    const right = stageContainer(
+      { op: "loop", blockType: empty, body: stageRegion(body, b, []) },
+      { nodeIdentity: {} },
+    );
+    const staged = { body, ...identity, root: stageRegion(body, root, [left, right]) };
+    const editor = new StagingBodyEditor(graph, staged, token, [
+      { region: root, parent: { kind: "body", body }, nodeIdentities: [left.nodeIdentity, right.nodeIdentity] },
+      {
+        region: a,
+        parent: { kind: "node", nodeIdentity: left.nodeIdentity, slot: { kind: "body" } },
+        nodeIdentities: [],
+      },
+      {
+        region: b,
+        parent: { kind: "node", nodeIdentity: right.nodeIdentity, slot: { kind: "body" } },
+        nodeIdentities: [],
+      },
+    ]);
+    expect(() => editor.removeLeaf(left.nodeIdentity)).toThrow("attachment-receipt");
+    editor.reorderSequence(root, [right.nodeIdentity, left.nodeIdentity]);
+    expect(editor.expectedLayout().map((p) => p.region)).toEqual([root, b, a]);
+    expect(editor.snapshot().root.nodes).toEqual([right, left]);
+    editor.complete();
+  });
+
+  it("copies a frozen try/catch ancestor while retaining unrelated regions and logical identities", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const identity = { bodyIdentity: {}, bindingEnvironment: {} };
+    const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("catch"), identity);
+    const token = graph.beginContentTransaction(body);
+    const root = graph.createRegion(body),
+      protectedId = graph.createRegion(body),
+      handlerId = graph.createRegion(body);
+    graph.attachRegion(body, root);
+    graph.attachRegion(root, protectedId);
+    graph.attachRegion(root, handlerId);
+    const nodeIdentity = {};
+    const kind = "protocol-choice" as const;
+    const occurrence = graph.recordOccurrence(handlerId, provenance("handler"), { nodeIdentity, kind });
+    const leaf = stageLeaf({ op: "local.get", index: 0 }, { nodeIdentity, occurrence: { occurrence, kind } });
+    const protectedRegion = stageRegion(body, protectedId, []);
+    const handler = stageRegion(body, handlerId, [leaf]);
+    const container = stageContainer(
+      { op: "try", blockType: empty, body: protectedRegion, catches: [{ tagIdx: 3, body: handler }] },
+      { nodeIdentity: {} },
+    );
+    const editor = new StagingBodyEditor(
+      graph,
+      { body, ...identity, root: stageRegion(body, root, [container]) },
+      token,
+      [
+        { region: root, parent: { kind: "body", body }, nodeIdentities: [container.nodeIdentity] },
+        {
+          region: protectedId,
+          parent: { kind: "node", nodeIdentity: container.nodeIdentity, slot: { kind: "body" } },
+          nodeIdentities: [],
+        },
+        {
+          region: handlerId,
+          parent: { kind: "node", nodeIdentity: container.nodeIdentity, slot: { kind: "catch", index: 0 } },
+          nodeIdentities: [leaf.nodeIdentity],
+        },
+      ],
+    );
+    editor.removeLeaf(leaf.nodeIdentity);
+    const updated = editor.snapshot().root.nodes[0]!;
+    expect(updated).not.toBe(container);
+    expect(updated.nodeIdentity).toBe(container.nodeIdentity);
+    if (updated.kind !== "container" || updated.instruction.op !== "try") throw new Error("positive control");
+    expect(updated.instruction.body).toBe(protectedRegion);
+    expect(updated.instruction.catches[0]?.tagIdx).toBe(3);
+    expect(updated.instruction.catches[0]?.body.nodes).toEqual([]);
+    expect(handler.nodes).toEqual([leaf]);
+    editor.complete();
+  });
+
+  it("rejects stale or non-bijective commands without changing the physical root", () => {
+    const f = setup();
+    const before = f.editor.snapshot();
+    expect(() => f.editor.removeLeaf({})).toThrow("Unknown physical node");
+    expect(() => f.editor.reorderSequence(f.root, [f.x.nodeIdentity, f.x.nodeIdentity])).toThrow("permutation");
+    expect(() => f.editor.reorderSequence(f.root, [f.y.nodeIdentity])).toThrow("permutation");
+    expect(f.editor.snapshot()).toBe(before);
+    f.editor.complete();
+  });
+
+  it("checks nested token order before closing either physical or ledger scope", () => {
+    const f = setup();
+    const outer = f.editor.beginEdit(),
+      inner = f.editor.beginEdit();
+    expect(() => f.editor.rollbackEdit(outer)).toThrow("LIFO");
+    expect(() => f.editor.complete()).toThrow("nested");
+    f.editor.rollbackEdit(inner);
+    f.editor.commitEdit(outer);
+    expect(() => f.editor.commitEdit(outer)).toThrow("LIFO");
+    f.editor.complete();
+  });
+
+  it("construction rollback never promotes the restored physical snapshot to a completed body", () => {
+    const f = setup();
+    f.editor.removeLeaf(f.x.nodeIdentity);
+    f.editor.rollbackEdit(f.token);
+    expect(f.editor.snapshot().root).toBe(f.staged.root);
+    expect(() => f.editor.complete()).toThrow("closed");
+    expect(() => f.graph.retainBodyEpoch(f.body)).toThrow("failed");
+  });
+
+  it("completed body content cannot be edited or completed again", () => {
+    const f = setup();
+    f.editor.complete();
+    expect(() => f.editor.beginEdit()).toThrow("closed");
+    expect(() => f.editor.removeLeaf(f.x.nodeIdentity)).toThrow("closed");
+    expect(() => f.editor.complete()).toThrow("closed");
+  });
+
+  it("independent child completion survives parent abort and fresh later adoption", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const parent = setup(graph);
+    const child = setup(graph);
+    child.editor.removeLeaf(child.x.nodeIdentity);
+    const done = child.editor.complete();
+    parent.editor.rollbackEdit(parent.token);
+    const later = setup(graph, child.body);
+    const selected = later.editor.complete();
+    const publication = graph.createPublication("function", provenance("later"));
+    graph.publishBody(publication, later.body);
+    const result = graph.sealWithManifest({
+      publications: [{ publication, receipt: selected.receipt }],
+      bodies: [inspectStageBody(selected.body).manifest, inspectStageBody(done.body).manifest],
+    });
+    expect(result.ownership.bodies).not.toContain(parent.body);
+    expect(result.ownership.occurrences.map((o) => o.occurrence)).toContain(child.y.occurrence!.occurrence);
+    expect(result.ownership.occurrences.map((o) => o.occurrence)).not.toContain(child.x.occurrence!.occurrence);
+  });
+
+  it("leaf removal cancels its exact child-use edge as well as its occurrence", () => {
+    const graph = new EmissionOwnership();
+    const child = setup(graph);
+    child.editor.complete();
+    const parent = setup(graph, child.body);
+    parent.editor.removeLeaf(parent.x.nodeIdentity);
+    const done = parent.editor.complete();
+    const manifest = inspectStageBody(done.body).manifest;
+    expect(manifest.adoptions).toEqual([]);
+    const publication = graph.createPublication("function", provenance("parent"));
+    graph.publishBody(publication, parent.body);
+    const result = graph.sealWithManifest({
+      publications: [{ publication, receipt: done.receipt }],
+      bodies: [manifest],
+    });
+    expect(result.ownership.bodies).toEqual([parent.body]);
+    expect(result.ownership.unselectedBodies).toContain(child.body);
+  });
+
+  it("ledger-step failure rolls back earlier occurrence removal without installing the physical version", () => {
+    const f = setup();
+    const foreign = setup();
+    const useSiteIdentity = {};
+    const edge = foreign.graph.adoptBody(foreign.root, foreign.body, useSiteIdentity);
+    // Deliberate malformed adapter input. The one-body physical census cannot
+    // authenticate a foreign edge; ledger mutation must refuse it atomically.
+    const bad = stageLeaf(f.x.instruction, { ...f.x, adoptions: [{ edge, body: foreign.body, useSiteIdentity }] });
+    const staged = { ...f.staged, root: stageRegion(f.body, f.root, [bad, f.y]) };
+    // Transfer the still-open token instead of using the original test editor.
+    const editor = new StagingBodyEditor(f.graph, staged, f.token, f.expected);
+    const before = editor.snapshot();
+    expect(() => editor.removeLeaf(bad.nodeIdentity)).toThrow("foreign");
+    expect(editor.snapshot()).toBe(before);
+    // A missing undo would instead report an already-detached occurrence here.
+    expect(() => editor.removeLeaf(bad.nodeIdentity)).toThrow("foreign");
+    expect(editor.snapshot()).toBe(before);
+    editor.rollbackEdit(f.token);
+    foreign.editor.rollbackEdit(foreign.token);
+  });
+});

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+// Isolated staging foundation, source-only; no execution grant for these cases.
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import type { Instr } from "../src/wasm/model/instructions.js";
+import { EmissionOwnership, type EmissionRegion } from "../src/codegen/context/emission-ownership.js";
+import {
+  isExecutableStageLeaf,
+  materializedStageChildren,
+  stageContainer,
+  stageLeaf,
+  stageRegion,
+  type ExecutableStageLeaf,
+  type StageContainerInstruction,
+  type StageNode,
+  type StageRegion,
+} from "../src/codegen/staging-instr.js";
+import { assertStageLayout, inspectStageBody } from "../src/codegen/staging-census.js";
+
+const provenance = (unitId: string) => ({ sourceId: "staging.ts", unitId });
+const empty = { kind: "empty" as const };
+const leaf = (value: number) => stageLeaf({ op: "i32.const", value }, { nodeIdentity: {} });
+
+function fixture(graph = new EmissionOwnership()) {
+  const identity = { bodyIdentity: {}, bindingEnvironment: {} };
+  const body = graph.createBodyEpoch(provenance("body"), identity);
+  const token = graph.beginContentTransaction(body);
+  const root = graph.createRegion(body);
+  graph.attachRegion(body, root);
+  function region(owner = root) {
+    const id = graph.createRegion(body);
+    graph.attachRegion(owner, id);
+    return id;
+  }
+  function occurrence(owner = root) {
+    const nodeIdentity = {};
+    const kind = "required-drive" as const;
+    const occurrence = graph.recordOccurrence(owner, provenance("node"), { nodeIdentity, kind });
+    // Synthetic binding evidence, NOT an implementation of a pending recipe.
+    const node = stageLeaf({ op: "local.get", index: 0 }, { nodeIdentity, occurrence: { occurrence, kind } });
+    return { node, occurrence };
+  }
+  function tree(nodes: readonly StageNode[]) {
+    return { body, ...identity, root: stageRegion(body, root, nodes) };
+  }
+  return { graph, body, token, root, region, occurrence, tree };
+}
+
+describe("isolated typed staging / independent census", () => {
+  it("keeps executable Instr closed and recursively excludes all existing container shapes from leaves", () => {
+    type HiddenRecursiveLeaf = Extract<
+      ExecutableStageLeaf,
+      { body: Instr[] } | { then: Instr[] } | { else: Instr[] } | { catches: { body: Instr[] }[] }
+    >;
+    const excluded: [HiddenRecursiveLeaf] extends [never] ? true : false = true;
+    expect(excluded).toBe(true);
+    const f = fixture();
+    const region = stageRegion(f.body, f.root, []);
+    const node = stageContainer({ op: "block", blockType: empty, body: region }, { nodeIdentity: {} });
+    function assertRejectedTypes() {
+      // @ts-expect-error Staged wrappers are not executable Wasm instructions.
+      const executable: Instr = node;
+      // @ts-expect-error Recursive executable children cannot enter a stage leaf.
+      stageLeaf({ op: "block", blockType: empty, body: [] }, { nodeIdentity: {} });
+      // @ts-expect-error Recursive staged children cannot be Instr[].
+      const container: StageContainerInstruction = { op: "loop", blockType: empty, body: [] };
+      void executable;
+      void container;
+    }
+    void assertRejectedTypes;
+    expect(isExecutableStageLeaf({ op: "try", blockType: empty, body: [], catches: [] })).toBe(false);
+    expect(isExecutableStageLeaf({ op: "ref.func", funcIdx: 1 })).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it("walks all recursive variants including catches in lexical preorder and exact label depth", () => {
+    const f = fixture();
+    const blockId = f.region();
+    const loopId = f.region(blockId);
+    const thenId = f.region();
+    const elseId = f.region();
+    const tryId = f.region();
+    const catch0 = f.region();
+    const catch1 = f.region();
+    const catchAll = f.region();
+    const tableId = f.region();
+    const r = (id: EmissionRegion, value: number) => stageRegion(f.body, id, [leaf(value)]);
+    const loop = stageContainer({ op: "loop", blockType: empty, body: r(loopId, 1) }, { nodeIdentity: {} });
+    const nodes = [
+      stageContainer(
+        { op: "block", blockType: empty, body: stageRegion(f.body, blockId, [loop]) },
+        { nodeIdentity: {} },
+      ),
+      stageContainer({ op: "if", blockType: empty, then: r(thenId, 2), else: r(elseId, 3) }, { nodeIdentity: {} }),
+      stageContainer(
+        {
+          op: "try",
+          blockType: empty,
+          body: r(tryId, 4),
+          catches: [
+            { tagIdx: 0, body: r(catch0, 5) },
+            { tagIdx: 1, body: r(catch1, 6) },
+          ],
+          catchAll: r(catchAll, 7),
+        },
+        { nodeIdentity: {} },
+      ),
+      stageContainer(
+        {
+          op: "try_table",
+          blockType: empty,
+          body: r(tableId, 8),
+          catches: [
+            { kind: "catch", tagIdx: 0, depth: 2 },
+            { kind: "catch_all", depth: 3 },
+          ],
+        },
+        { nodeIdentity: {} },
+      ),
+    ];
+    const census = inspectStageBody(f.tree(nodes));
+    expect(census.visits.map((v) => v.node.instruction.op)).toEqual([
+      "block",
+      "loop",
+      "i32.const",
+      "if",
+      "i32.const",
+      "i32.const",
+      "try",
+      "i32.const",
+      "i32.const",
+      "i32.const",
+      "i32.const",
+      "try_table",
+      "i32.const",
+    ]);
+    expect(census.visits.map((v) => v.labelDepth)).toEqual([0, 1, 2, 0, 1, 1, 0, 1, 1, 1, 1, 0, 1]);
+    expect(materializedStageChildren(nodes[2]!).map((c) => c.slot)).toEqual([
+      { kind: "body" },
+      { kind: "catch", index: 0 },
+      { kind: "catch", index: 1 },
+      { kind: "catchAll" },
+    ]);
+    expect(materializedStageChildren(nodes[3]!)).toHaveLength(1);
+    f.graph.commitTransaction(f.token);
+    f.graph.completeBodyEpoch(f.body, census.manifest);
+  });
+
+  it("supplies the independent physical node census to authenticated completion and seal", () => {
+    const f = fixture();
+    const { node, occurrence } = f.occurrence();
+    const census = inspectStageBody(f.tree([node]));
+    expect(census.manifest.occurrences).toEqual([
+      {
+        occurrence,
+        owner: f.root,
+        nodeIdentity: node.nodeIdentity,
+        kind: "required-drive",
+      },
+    ]);
+    f.graph.commitTransaction(f.token);
+    const receipt = f.graph.completeBodyEpoch(f.body, census.manifest);
+    const publication = f.graph.createPublication("function", provenance("root"));
+    f.graph.publishBody(publication, f.body);
+    const verified = f.graph.sealWithManifest({ publications: [{ publication, receipt }], bodies: [census.manifest] });
+    expect(verified.requiredOccurrences).toEqual([occurrence]);
+    expect(Object.isFrozen(census.manifest.occurrences[0])).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it("does not invent pending operations or change executable leaf identity/source positions", () => {
+    const instruction: ExecutableStageLeaf = {
+      op: "call",
+      funcIdx: 9,
+      sourcePos: { file: "x.ts", line: 2, column: 3 },
+    };
+    const f = fixture();
+    const node = stageLeaf(instruction, { nodeIdentity: {} });
+    const census = inspectStageBody(f.tree([node]));
+    expect(census.visits[0]?.node.instruction).toBe(instruction);
+    expect(census.visits[0]?.node.instruction.sourcePos).toBe(instruction.sourcePos);
+    expect(census.manifest.occurrences).toEqual([]);
+    expect(census.manifest.adoptions).toEqual([]);
+  });
+
+  it("owns structural arrays and annotation records without cloning opaque identities", () => {
+    const f = fixture();
+    const { node } = f.occurrence();
+    const input = [node];
+    const tree = f.tree(input);
+    input.length = 0;
+    expect(tree.root.nodes).toEqual([node]);
+    expect(Object.isFrozen(tree.root.nodes)).toBe(true);
+    const body = stageRegion(f.body, f.region(), []);
+    const catches = [{ tagIdx: 7, body: stageRegion(f.body, f.region(), []) }];
+    const container = stageContainer({ op: "try", blockType: empty, body, catches }, { nodeIdentity: {} });
+    catches[0]!.tagIdx = 8;
+    catches.length = 0;
+    if (container.kind !== "container" || container.instruction.op !== "try") throw new Error("positive control");
+    expect(container.instruction.catches[0]?.tagIdx).toBe(7);
+    expect(Object.isFrozen(container.instruction.catches)).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it.each(["node", "identity", "instruction"] as const)(
+    "rejects aliased physical %s without deduplicating it away",
+    (kind) => {
+      const f = fixture();
+      const first = leaf(1);
+      const second =
+        kind === "node"
+          ? first
+          : kind === "identity"
+            ? stageLeaf({ op: "i32.const", value: 2 }, { nodeIdentity: first.nodeIdentity })
+            : stageLeaf(first.instruction, { nodeIdentity: {} });
+      expect(() => inspectStageBody(f.tree([first, second]))).toThrow("multiple physical positions");
+    },
+  );
+
+  it("rejects shared then/else regions rather than hiding multiple parents behind a visited set", () => {
+    const f = fixture();
+    const child = stageRegion(f.body, f.region(), [leaf(1)]);
+    const node = stageContainer({ op: "if", blockType: empty, then: child, else: child }, { nodeIdentity: {} });
+    expect(() => inspectStageBody(f.tree([node]))).toThrow("multiple physical parents");
+  });
+
+  it("rejects a containment cycle in a structurally supplied tree", () => {
+    const f = fixture();
+    const nodes: StageNode[] = [];
+    const root: StageRegion = { kind: "stage-region", body: f.body, region: f.root, nodes };
+    nodes.push(stageContainer({ op: "block", blockType: empty, body: root }, { nodeIdentity: {} }));
+    expect(() => inspectStageBody({ ...f.tree([]), root })).toThrow("cycle");
+  });
+
+  it("rejects two physical versions of the same region handle", () => {
+    const f = fixture();
+    const id = f.region();
+    const node = stageContainer(
+      { op: "if", blockType: empty, then: stageRegion(f.body, id, []), else: stageRegion(f.body, id, []) },
+      { nodeIdentity: {} },
+    );
+    expect(() => inspectStageBody(f.tree([node]))).toThrow("multiple physical versions");
+  });
+
+  it("rejects a foreign-body child before it can contribute to a manifest", () => {
+    const f = fixture();
+    const other = fixture();
+    const child = stageContainer({ op: "block", blockType: empty, body: other.tree([]).root }, { nodeIdentity: {} });
+    expect(() => inspectStageBody(f.tree([child]))).toThrow("different body epoch");
+  });
+
+  it("rejects one occurrence annotated onto two distinct physical nodes", () => {
+    const f = fixture();
+    const { node } = f.occurrence();
+    const extra = stageLeaf({ op: "local.get", index: 1 }, { nodeIdentity: {}, occurrence: node.occurrence });
+    expect(() => inspectStageBody(f.tree([node, extra]))).toThrow("Occurrence has multiple physical nodes");
+  });
+
+  it.each(["missing", "removed", "wrong-node"] as const)(
+    "independent census detects %s ledger/node disagreement",
+    (kind) => {
+      const f = fixture();
+      const { node, occurrence } = f.occurrence();
+      if (kind === "removed") f.graph.removeOccurrence(occurrence);
+      const nodes =
+        kind === "missing"
+          ? []
+          : kind === "wrong-node"
+            ? [stageLeaf({ op: "local.get", index: 0 }, { nodeIdentity: {}, occurrence: node.occurrence })]
+            : [node];
+      const census = inspectStageBody(f.tree(nodes));
+      f.graph.commitTransaction(f.token);
+      expect(() => f.graph.completeBodyEpoch(f.body, census.manifest)).toThrow("census mismatch");
+    },
+  );
+
+  it("excludes detached physical fragments instead of enumerating every ledger record", () => {
+    const f = fixture();
+    const detached = f.graph.createRegion(f.body);
+    const { node } = f.occurrence(detached);
+    const orphan = stageRegion(f.body, detached, [node]);
+    const census = inspectStageBody(f.tree([]));
+    expect(census.manifest.regions).toEqual([f.root]);
+    expect(census.manifest.occurrences).toEqual([]);
+    expect(orphan.nodes).toHaveLength(1); // Physical disposal/remap remains a separate obligation.
+    f.graph.commitTransaction(f.token);
+    f.graph.completeBodyEpoch(f.body, census.manifest);
+  });
+
+  it("checks parent slots independently even when the ledger region and occurrence sets match", () => {
+    const f = fixture();
+    const child = stageRegion(f.body, f.region(), [leaf(1)]);
+    const emptyElse = stageRegion(f.body, f.region(), []);
+    const nodeIdentity = {};
+    const before = inspectStageBody(
+      f.tree([stageContainer({ op: "if", blockType: empty, then: child, else: emptyElse }, { nodeIdentity })]),
+    );
+    const after = inspectStageBody(
+      f.tree([stageContainer({ op: "if", blockType: empty, then: emptyElse, else: child }, { nodeIdentity })]),
+    );
+    expect(new Set(after.manifest.regions)).toEqual(new Set(before.manifest.regions));
+    expect(() => assertStageLayout(after, before.layout)).toThrow("parent or region order mismatch");
+    assertStageLayout(before, before.layout);
+    // Set census equality is NOT proof of physical parent/slot/order equality.
+  });
+
+  it("checks exact sibling instruction order outside the ledger's unordered occurrence census", () => {
+    const f = fixture();
+    const x = f.occurrence();
+    const y = f.occurrence();
+    const before = inspectStageBody(f.tree([x.node, y.node]));
+    const after = inspectStageBody(f.tree([y.node, x.node]));
+    expect(new Set(after.manifest.occurrences.map((o) => o.occurrence))).toEqual(
+      new Set(before.manifest.occurrences.map((o) => o.occurrence)),
+    );
+    expect(() => assertStageLayout(after, before.layout)).toThrow("node order mismatch");
+  });
+
+  it("reads exact adoption edges from physical nodes without traversing adopted body content", () => {
+    const f = fixture();
+    const child = fixture(f.graph);
+    const cm = inspectStageBody(child.tree([])).manifest;
+    child.graph.commitTransaction(child.token);
+    child.graph.completeBodyEpoch(child.body, cm);
+    const useSiteIdentity = {};
+    const edge = f.graph.adoptBody(f.root, child.body, useSiteIdentity);
+    const adoptions = [{ edge, body: child.body, useSiteIdentity }];
+    const node = stageLeaf({ op: "ref.func", funcIdx: 5 }, { nodeIdentity: {}, adoptions });
+    const census = inspectStageBody(f.tree([node]));
+    expect(census.manifest.adoptions).toEqual([{ ...adoptions[0], owner: f.root }]);
+    expect(census.manifest.regions).not.toContain(child.root);
+    f.graph.commitTransaction(f.token);
+    const receipt = f.graph.completeBodyEpoch(f.body, census.manifest);
+    const publication = f.graph.createPublication("function", provenance("root"));
+    f.graph.publishBody(publication, f.body);
+    expect(
+      f.graph.sealWithManifest({ publications: [{ publication, receipt }], bodies: [census.manifest, cm] }).ownership
+        .bodies,
+    ).toHaveLength(2);
+  });
+
+  it("rejects an adoption duplicated across physical nodes", () => {
+    const f = fixture();
+    const useSiteIdentity = {};
+    const edge = f.graph.adoptBody(f.root, f.body, useSiteIdentity);
+    const adoptions = [{ edge, body: f.body, useSiteIdentity }];
+    const a = stageLeaf({ op: "ref.func", funcIdx: 0 }, { nodeIdentity: {}, adoptions });
+    const b = stageLeaf({ op: "ref.func", funcIdx: 1 }, { nodeIdentity: {}, adoptions });
+    expect(() => inspectStageBody(f.tree([a, b]))).toThrow("Adoption edge or use site");
+  });
+});
diff --git a/tests/issue-5883-symbol-valueof-route.test.ts b/tests/issue-5883-symbol-valueof-route.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..b8c46e19dc
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/issue-5883-symbol-valueof-route.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,79 @@
+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
+import { runInNewContext } from "node:vm";
+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { compile } from "../src/index.js";
+
+// Supplemental localization controls. Original acceptance fixtures remain unchanged.
+const cases = [
+  {
+    name: "Symbol valueOf apply primitive",
+    source:
+      'export function test(){const p=Symbol("p");const v=Symbol.prototype.valueOf.apply(p,[]);return v===p?1:-1;}',
+  },
+  {
+    name: "Symbol valueOf apply wrapper",
+    source:
+      'export function test(){const p=Symbol("p");const v=Symbol.prototype.valueOf.apply(Object(p),[]);return v===p?1:-1;}',
+  },
+  {
+    name: "Symbol valueOf rejects plain object",
+    source:
+      "export function test(){try{Symbol.prototype.valueOf.call({});return -1;}catch(e){return e instanceof TypeError?1:-2;}}",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "Symbol valueOf rejects number",
+    source:
+      "export function test(){try{Symbol.prototype.valueOf.call(7);return -1;}catch(e){return e instanceof TypeError?1:-2;}}",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "Symbol valueOf rejects null",
+    source:
+      "export function test(){try{Symbol.prototype.valueOf.call(null);return -1;}catch(e){return e instanceof TypeError?1:-2;}}",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "Symbol valueOf replacement retains receiver",
+    source:
+      'export function test(){const p=Symbol("p");Symbol.prototype.valueOf=function(){"use strict";return this===p?17:-1;};return Symbol.prototype.valueOf.call(p)===17?1:-2;}',
+  },
+  {
+    name: "Symbol name shadowing retains user method",
+    source:
+      'export function test(){const p={tag:17};const Symbol={prototype:{valueOf:function(){"use strict";return this===p?17:-1;}}};return Symbol.prototype.valueOf.call(p)===17?1:-2;}',
+  },
+];
+for (const { name, source } of cases) {
+  it(name, async () => {
+    const sha256 = createHash("sha256").update(source).digest("hex");
+    const native = runInNewContext(
+      source.replace("export function test", "function test") + ";test()",
+      {},
+      { timeout: 5000 },
+    );
+    const receipt: Record<string, unknown> = { name, source, sha256, native };
+    try {
+      const result = await compile(source, {
+        fileName: "5883-symbol-valueof-route.js",
+        allowJs: true,
+        skipSemanticDiagnostics: true,
+        target: "standalone",
+        nativeStrings: true,
+      });
+      receipt.success = result.success;
+      receipt.errors = result.errors;
+      expect(result.success, JSON.stringify(result.errors)).toBe(true);
+      const module = new WebAssembly.Module(result.binary);
+      receipt.imports = WebAssembly.Module.imports(module);
+      expect(receipt.imports).toEqual([]);
+      const instance = new WebAssembly.Instance(module, {});
+      receipt.actual = (instance.exports.test as () => number)();
+      expect(native).toBe(1);
+      expect(receipt.actual).toBe(1);
+    } catch (error) {
+      receipt.failure = String(error);
+      throw error;
+    } finally {
+      console.log(JSON.stringify(receipt));
+    }
+  });
+}

diff --git a/tests/issue-5883-validated-array-length-store.test.ts b/tests/issue-5883-validated-array-length-store.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..934f64b6f0
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/issue-5883-validated-array-length-store.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,37 @@
+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { beforeEach, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import type { Instr } from "../src/ir/types.js";
+import type { ts } from "../src/ts-api.js";
+import type { CodegenContext, FunctionContext } from "../src/codegen/context/types.js";
+const mocks = vi.hoisted(() => ({ holeFill: vi.fn() }));
+vi.mock("../src/codegen/vec-length-hole-fill.js", () => ({ emitVecLengthHoleFill: mocks.holeFill }));
+import { buildValidatedArrayLengthStore } from "../src/codegen/array-filter-length-set.js";
+beforeEach(() => {
+  mocks.holeFill.mockReset();
+});
+
+it("constructs hole fill before the plain store without emitting into the enclosing body", () => {
+  const ctx = { standalone: false } as CodegenContext;
+  const outer: Instr[] = [{ op: "i32.const", value: 42 }];
+  const fill: Instr = { op: "i32.const", value: 7 };
+  const store: Instr = { op: "drop" };
+  const fctx = { body: outer } as FunctionContext;
+  mocks.holeFill.mockImplementation((_ctx, frame) => frame.body.push(fill));
+  const result = buildValidatedArrayLengthStore(ctx, fctx, 2, 3, {} as ts.Node, [store]);
+  expect(mocks.holeFill).toHaveBeenCalledExactlyOnceWith(ctx, fctx, 2, 3, "shrink-only", true);
+  expect(result).toEqual([fill, store]);
+  expect(result[1]).toBe(store);
+  expect(fctx.body).toBe(outer);
+  expect(outer).toEqual([{ op: "i32.const", value: 42 }]);
+});
+it("restores the enclosing body and propagates a hole-fill failure", () => {
+  const ctx = { standalone: true, vecAccessorDescriptorDirty: false } as CodegenContext;
+  const outer: Instr[] = [];
+  const fctx = { body: outer } as FunctionContext;
+  const error = new Error("original fill failure");
+  mocks.holeFill.mockImplementation(() => {
+    throw error;
+  });
+  expect(() => buildValidatedArrayLengthStore(ctx, fctx, 2, 3, {} as ts.Node, [])).toThrow(error);
+  expect(fctx.body).toBe(outer);
+});

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
+import { runInNewContext } from "node:vm";
+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { compile } from "../src/index.js";
+
+// Supplemental localization controls. Original acceptance fixtures remain unchanged.
+const cases = [
+  {
+    name: "BigInt SameValue without method",
+    source:
+      "export function test() { try { const p=7n; return Object.is(p,p) && !Object.is(p,8n) ? 1 : -1; } catch(error) { return -90; } }",
+    expected: 1,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "Symbol SameValue without method",
+    source:
+      'export function test() { try { const p=Symbol("same"); const q=Symbol("same"); return Object.is(p,p) && !Object.is(p,q) ? 1 : -1; } catch(error) { return -90; } }',
+    expected: 1,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "BigInt extracted SameValue without method",
+    source:
+      "export function test() { try { const p=7n; const same=Object.is; return same(p,p) && !same(p,8n) ? 1 : -1; } catch(error) { return -90; } }",
+    expected: 1,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "Symbol extracted SameValue without method",
+    source:
+      'export function test() { try { const p=Symbol("same"); const q=Symbol("same"); const same=Object.is; return same(p,p) && !same(p,q) ? 1 : -1; } catch(error) { return -90; } }',
+    expected: 1,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "BigInt direct method primitive arithmetic",
+    source:
+      'export function test() { try { const p=7n; const v=BigInt.prototype.valueOf.call(p); if(typeof v!=="bigint")return -1; return v+2n===9n && v-p===0n ? 1 : -2; } catch(error) { return -90; } }',
+    expected: 1,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "BigInt direct method wrapper arithmetic",
+    source:
+      'export function test() { try { const p=7n; const v=BigInt.prototype.valueOf.call(Object(p)); if(typeof v!=="bigint")return -1; return v+2n===9n && v-p===0n ? 1 : -2; } catch(error) { return -90; } }',
+    expected: 1,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "BigInt extracted method wrapper arithmetic",
+    source:
+      'export function test() { try { const p=7n; const method=BigInt.prototype.valueOf; const v=method.call(Object(p)); if(typeof v!=="bigint")return -1; return v+2n===9n && v-p===0n ? 1 : -2; } catch(error) { return -90; } }',
+    expected: 1,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "Symbol direct method primitive key identity",
+    source:
+      'export function test() { try { const p=Symbol("probe"); const q=Symbol("probe"); const o={}; o[p]=17; o[q]=23; const v=Symbol.prototype.valueOf.call(p); if(typeof v!=="symbol")return -1; return v===p && v!==q && o[v]===17 ? 1 : -2; } catch(error) { return -90; } }',
+    expected: 1,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "Symbol direct method wrapper key identity",
+    source:
+      'export function test() { try { const p=Symbol("probe"); const q=Symbol("probe"); const o={}; o[p]=17; o[q]=23; const v=Symbol.prototype.valueOf.call(Object(p)); if(typeof v!=="symbol")return -1; return v===p && v!==q && o[v]===17 ? 1 : -2; } catch(error) { return -90; } }',
+    expected: 1,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "Symbol extracted method wrapper key identity",
+    source:
+      'export function test() { try { const p=Symbol("probe"); const q=Symbol("probe"); const o={}; o[p]=17; o[q]=23; const method=Symbol.prototype.valueOf; const v=method.call(Object(p)); if(typeof v!=="symbol")return -1; return v===p && v!==q && o[v]===17 ? 1 : -2; } catch(error) { return -90; } }',
+    expected: 1,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "Symbol key identity without method",
+    source:
+      'export function test() { try { const p=Symbol("probe"); const q=Symbol("probe"); const o={}; o[p]=17; o[q]=23; return p!==q && o[p]===17 && o[q]===23 ? 1 : -1; } catch(error) { return -90; } }',
+    expected: 1,
+  },
+  {
+    name: "BigInt arithmetic without method",
+    source:
+      "export function test() { try { const p=7n; return p+2n===9n && p-7n===0n ? 1 : -1; } catch(error) { return -90; } }",
+    expected: 1,
+  },
+];
+for (const { name, source } of cases) {
+  it(name, async () => {
+    const sha256 = createHash("sha256").update(source).digest("hex");
+    const native = runInNewContext(
+      source.replace("export function test", "function test") + ";test()",
+      {},
+      { timeout: 5000 },
+    );
+    const receipt: Record<string, unknown> = { name, source, sha256, native };
+    try {
+      const result = await compile(source, {
+        fileName: "5883-valueof-samevalue-diagnostic.js",
+        allowJs: true,
+        skipSemanticDiagnostics: true,
+        target: "standalone",
+        nativeStrings: true,
+      });
+      receipt.success = result.success;
+      receipt.errors = result.errors;
+      expect(result.success, JSON.stringify(result.errors)).toBe(true);
+      const module = new WebAssembly.Module(result.binary);
+      receipt.imports = WebAssembly.Module.imports(module);
+      expect(receipt.imports).toEqual([]);
+      const instance = new WebAssembly.Instance(module, {});
+      receipt.actual = (instance.exports.test as () => number)();
+      expect(native).toBe(1);
+      expect(receipt.actual).toBe(1);
+    } catch (error) {
+      receipt.failure = String(error);
+      throw error;
+    } finally {
+      console.log(JSON.stringify(receipt));
+    }
+  });
+}

+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { compile } from "../src/index.js";
+import { getFixupEvents } from "../src/codegen/stack-balance.js";
+
+it("does not invent fallthrough results for Get or its inlined Pop use", async () => {
+  const fileName = fileURLToPath(new URL("../website/playground/examples/benchmarks.ts", import.meta.url));
+  const source = readFileSync(fileName, "utf8");
+  const result = await compile(source, { fileName });
+  expect(result.success, JSON.stringify(result.errors)).toBe(true);
+  const events = getFixupEvents();
+  // Positive telemetry control: unrelated existing repairs remain visible.
+  expect(events.some((event) => event.func === "__vec_len" && event.kind === "default-value-lossy")).toBe(true);
+  expect(
+    events.filter(
+      (event) => (event.func === "__vec_get" || event.func === "__vec_pop") && event.kind === "default-value-lossy",
+    ),
+  ).toEqual([]);
+}, 30000);

index 0000000000..6f93703a54
