+// Copyright (c) 2026 Loopdive GmbH. Licensed under Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception.
+import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
+import { runInNewContext } from "node:vm";
+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { compile } from "../src/index.js";
+
+// No receiver annotations: exercise the carriers emitted for ordinary JS.
+// Each fixture retains its complete native and compiled observations.
+const cases = [
+  [
+    "stores a custom prototype on an ordinary vector without an Array subclass",
+    `const a = [1]; const p = { token: 7 };
+     const returned = Object.setPrototypeOf(a, p);
+     return returned === a && Object.getPrototypeOf(a) === p ? 1 : -1;`,
+  ],
+  [
+    "distinguishes an explicit null prototype from the default Array prototype",
+    `const a = [1]; Object.setPrototypeOf(a, null);
+     return Object.getPrototypeOf(a) === null ? 1 : -1;`,
+  ],
+  [
+    "restores Array prototype after an explicit null link",
+    `const a = [1]; Object.setPrototypeOf(a, null);
+     if (Object.getPrototypeOf(a) !== null) return -1;
+     Object.setPrototypeOf(a, Array.prototype);
+     return Object.getPrototypeOf(a) === Array.prototype ? 1 : -2;`,
+  ],
+  [
+    "preserves a callable prototype identity",
+    `const a = [1]; function p() {} Object.setPrototypeOf(a, p);
+     return Object.getPrototypeOf(a) === p ? 1 : -1;`,
+  ],
+  [
+    "preserves a vector as another vector's prototype",
+    `const a = [1]; const p = [7]; Object.setPrototypeOf(a, p);
+     return Object.getPrototypeOf(a) === p ? 1 : -1;`,
+  ],
+  [
+    "rejects a prototype cycle and retains the original link",
+    `const a = [1]; const p = [2]; Object.setPrototypeOf(a, p);
+     let caught = false;
+     try { Object.setPrototypeOf(p, a); } catch (e) { caught = e instanceof TypeError; }
+     return caught && Object.getPrototypeOf(a) === p && Object.getPrototypeOf(p) === Array.prototype ? 1 : -1;`,
+  ],
+  [
+    "allows the same prototype but rejects a new one after preventExtensions",
+    `const a = [1]; const p = { token: 7 }; Object.setPrototypeOf(a, p);
+     Object.preventExtensions(a);
+     if (Object.setPrototypeOf(a, p) !== a) return -1;
+     let caught = false;
+     try { Object.setPrototypeOf(a, null); } catch (e) { caught = e instanceof TypeError; }
+     return caught && Object.getPrototypeOf(a) === p ? 1 : -2;`,
+  ],
+  [
+    "uses the custom prototype for live indexed reads with the source as receiver",
+    `const a = [, undefined, NaN]; let receiver;
+     const p = Object.create(Array.prototype);
+     Object.defineProperty(p, "0", { get: function () { "use strict"; receiver = this; return 41; } });
+     Object.setPrototypeOf(a, p);
+     const iterator = Array.prototype.values.call(a);
+     const first = iterator.next(); const second = iterator.next(); const third = iterator.next();
+     return receiver === a && first.value === 41 && !first.done &&
+       second.value === undefined && !second.done && third.value !== third.value && !third.done ? 1 : -1;`,
+  ],
+] as const;
+
+for (const [name, body] of cases) {
+  it(name, async () => {
+    const source = `export function test() { ${body} }`;
+    const native = runInNewContext(`(function () { ${body} })()`, {}, { timeout: 5000 });
+    expect(native).toBe(1);
+    const result = await compile(source, {
+      fileName: "5883-vector-prototype-storage.js",
+      allowJs: true,
+      skipSemanticDiagnostics: true,
+      target: "standalone",
+      nativeStrings: true,
+    });
+    expect(result.success, JSON.stringify(result.errors)).toBe(true);
+    const module = new WebAssembly.Module(result.binary);
+    expect(WebAssembly.Module.imports(module)).toEqual([]);
+    const instance = new WebAssembly.Instance(module);
+    const actual = (instance.exports.test as () => number)();
+    console.log(
+      JSON.stringify({ name, source, sha256: createHash("sha256").update(source).digest("hex"), native, actual }),
+    );
+    expect(actual).toBe(native);
+  });
+}
